# Products & Programmes

Named products and programmes others adopt: software and platforms, curricula, assessments and instruments, datasets, and branded programmes and initiatives, each with its own frameworks and the evidence about it. A design is one setting's.

**1963 entries** · 0 stable · 1963 in review · 0 drafts

---

## In Review

#### A {: #letter-a }

* [A guide for states on evaluating ARRA-funded programs and other educational reforms](a-guide-for-states-on-evaluating-arra-funded-programs-and-other-educational-reforms.md) - A jointly developed evaluation-planning guide from the American Institutes for Research and Mathematica Policy Research to help state education agencies evaluate ARRA-funded initiatives and other educational reforms.
* [A Toolkit to Support Evidence-Based Writing Instruction in Grades 2 Through 4 (Historical)](a-toolkit-to-support-evidence-based-writing-instruction-in-grades-2-through-4-historical.md) - A historical toolkit providing evidence-based writing-instruction guidance, tip sheets, and professional-development supports for grades 2–4 teachers and school leaders.
* [A Wearable Exam Stress Dataset for Predicting Cognitive Performance in Real-World Settings](a-wearable-exam-stress-dataset-for-predicting-cognitive-performance-in-real-world-settings.md) - An open-access PhysioNet dataset of Empatica E4 physiological signals collected from college students during exams, released for studying stress and predicting cognitive performance.
* [A.I. Math Personalization Tool (AMPT)](ai-math-personalization-tool-ampt.md) - A generative-AI system developed by Norberg et al.
* [A2i](a2i.md) - A Learning Ovations-partnered system that uses online assessments, instructional recommendations, lesson-planning support, and professional development to help Pre-K through Grade 3 teachers individualize literacy instruction.
* [A4L Lessons curriculum](a4l-lessons-curriculum.md) - An arts-integrated curriculum package for elementary students in grades 3–5, developed and delivered through the Arts for Learning program.
* [AACTE and Westat pilot survey of beginning teachers and their supervisors](aacte-and-westat-pilot-survey-of-beginning-teachers-and-their-supervisors.md) - A state-oriented survey instrument developed by the American Association of Colleges for Teacher Education and Westat to collect information about beginning teachers’ classroom experiences, performance, and needs for educator-preparation program improvement and cross-program comparisons.
* [AARP Foundation Experience Corps](aarp-foundation-experience-corps.md) - AARP Foundation Experience Corps is a volunteer-based literacy tutoring programme run by AARP Foundation in which trained adults aged 50 and older tutor struggling K–3 readers individually or in small groups.
* [AASEE framework](aasee-framework.md) - The AI-Augmented Software Engineering Education framework, proposed by Qusay H.
* [Abbreviated 12-item Raven's Progressive Matrices assessment](abbreviated-12-item-ravens-progressive-matrices-assessment.md) - A shortened computerized Raven's Progressive Matrices assessment developed by Mattingly Jameson K MD et al.
* [Abbreviated Acceptability Rating Profile (AARP)](abbreviated-acceptability-rating-profile-aarp.md) - The AARP is an eight-item Likert-scaled assessment developed by Tarnowski and Simonian to measure the acceptability of treatments.
* [Absence Reports](absence-reports.md) - Absence Reports is an attendance-focused intervention delivered by school districts to provide absence information and support improved student attendance.
* [Abstrackr](abstrackr.md) - Abstrackr is a natural-language-processing-assisted software tool for supporting systematic-review evidence-synthesis workflows, including study screening, developed for research teams.
* [Academic Emotions Questionnaire (AEQ)](academic-emotions-questionnaire-aeq.md) - The AEQ is a self-report assessment developed by Reinhard Pekrun and colleagues to measure nine academic emotions across class-related, learning-related, and test-related situations.
* [Academic integrity indicator framework: 130 items organised under eight dimensions of academic integrity](academic-integrity-indicator-framework-130-items-organised-under-eight-dimensions-of-acade.md) - A framework developed by Hamish Coates and colleagues that measures and reports institutional academic-assessment integrity across eight dimensions using 130 indicators.
* [Academic Parent-Teacher Teams (APTT)](academic-parent-teacher-teams-aptt.md) - Academic Parent-Teacher Teams (APTT) is a structured family-engagement programme, developed for implementation by schools and districts, in which teachers convene parents, share assessment data, and support learning activities at home.
* [AcademiClaw](academiclaw.md) - AcademiClaw is an 80-task bilingual benchmark dataset with rubrics and evaluation code for assessing OpenClaw AI agents on complex academic workflows, created by its article authors.
* [Accelerate, TN ALL Corps, and Vetted Texas Tutor Corps](accelerate-tn-all-corps-and-vetted-texas-tutor-corps.md) - These are statewide intensive-tutoring programmes run by Louisiana, Tennessee, and Texas education agencies, respectively, with dedicated public funding, approved or supported providers, tutor training, instructional materials, and implementation assistance.
* [Accelerated Reader](accelerated-reader.md) - Accelerated Reader™ is Renaissance's software-based K–12 reading practice and assessment system in which students read books, complete computerized quizzes, and receive progress data for teacher monitoring and goal setting.
* [Accelerated Study in Associate Programs (ASAP)](accelerated-study-in-associate-programs-asap.md) - A three-year City University of New York programme that combines financial, academic, cohort-based, and personal supports to help community-college students complete associate degrees.
* [Acciona Program (AP)](acciona-program-ap.md) - A Chilean National Council for Culture and the Arts, Ministry of Education, and Balmaceda Youth Art Foundation programme in which professional artists lead semester-long weekly arts workshops in public high schools, alongside schoolteachers.
* [ACE in Higher Education (ACE-HE)](ace-in-higher-education-ace-he.md) - The ACE-HE, developed by Graham et al., is a bilingual survey instrument measuring affective, behavioral, and cognitive engagement, institutional support, and personal-environment barriers among online and blended higher-education students.
* [ACEs Aware Initiative](aces-aware-initiative.md) - A California statewide initiative led by the California Surgeon General and the Department of Health Care Services that promotes adverse childhood experiences screening, provider training, and trauma-informed systems of care.
* [Achieve3000](achieve3000.md) - Achieve3000® is a supplemental online literacy platform developed and provided by Achieve3000 that delivers differentiated nonfiction reading and writing instruction for learners from preK through grade 12.
* [ACTIONS framework](actions-framework.md) - Boniwell's framework classifies positive psychology interventions into Activity, Calming, Thinking, Identity, Optimising, Nourishing, and Social categories for organising and comparing interventions.
* [ADA](ada.md) - ADA is a GDPR-compliant, GPT-4-based domain-specific AI chatbot developed by Cilia Ricarda Rücker for Socratic mathematics support, curriculum-grounded retrieval, and calculation verification in secondary education.
* [AdaPT](adapt.md) - AdaPT is a Vue.js and TypeScript NestJS web application developed by Yanjie Zhang and colleagues that structures existing lesson plans, analyzes student learning profiles, and supports teacher-in-the-loop adaptation for differentiated K–12 instruction.
* [Adapted 15-item Internet Skills Scale](adapted-15-item-internet-skills-scale.md) - A culturally adapted self-report scale measuring operational, formal/navigation, and informational/evaluation Internet skills among Latin American college students.
* [Adapted ATES low-inference observation instrument for coding explicit and implicit SRL instruction](adapted-ates-low-inference-observation-instrument-for-coding-explicit-and-implicit-srl-ins.md) - An adapted low-inference ATES classroom-observation instrument developed by Sins et al.
* [Adapted Ottawa Global Rating Scale for leadership and communication in CRM simulations](adapted-ottawa-global-rating-scale-for-leadership-and-communication-in-crm-simulations.md) - An expert-rated, video-based performance assessment adapted by Emergency Medicine researchers to score medical residents' crisis-resource-management leadership and communication on anchored ordinal scales.
* [AdaptED Stories](adapted-stories.md) - A practitioner-facing web application developed by the AdaptED Stories research team for authoring, personalizing, and delivering Social Stories to autistic children, with practitioner review, reading-session supports, and analytics.
* [Adapted System Usability Scale (SUS)](adapted-system-usability-scale-sus.md) - A ten-item, five-point Likert self-report instrument adapted from Brooke's System Usability Scale to measure users' perceived usability of an interactive learning environment.
* [Adapted TPACK-21 questionnaire for GenAI-infused higher education instruction](adapted-tpack-21-questionnaire-for-genai-infused-higher-education-instruction.md) - A six-point faculty self-report questionnaire adapted from the Valtonen et al.
* [Adaptive Cognitive Evaluation Classroom (ACE-C)](adaptive-cognitive-evaluation-classroom-ace-c.md) - A group-administered iPad battery developed by Gallen et al.
* [Adaptive Through-Year Assessment (ATYA)](adaptive-through-year-assessment-atya.md) - NWEA's proposed through-year adaptive assessment system designed to measure both grade-level standards proficiency and academic growth while replacing an end-of-year state summative assessment.
* [Admiral Squad](admiral-squad.md) - Admiral Squad is a Middletown City Schools fellowship and recruitment initiative that supports, develops, and helps hire Black male educators in the district.
* [Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS v1.1)](adult-adhd-self-report-scale-asrs-v11.md) - The World Health Organization Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS v1.1) is a six-item self-report screening assessment for probable adult ADHD symptoms.
* [Adult Promise initiative](adult-promise-initiative.md) - A Lumina Foundation multiyear state-grant program launched in 2017 to engage and support adults ages 25 to 64 in postsecondary education, with Mathematica serving as its learning and evaluation partner from 2017 to 2021.
* [AEQ-ES adapted to English as L2 for primary school children](aeq-es-adapted-to-english-as-l2-for-primary-school-children.md) - A 32-item, face-supported Likert questionnaire adapted from the Achievement Emotion Questionnaire–Elementary School to measure primary pupils’ enjoyment, anxiety, and boredom when learning English as a second language.
* [Agent4Edu](agent4edu.md) - Agent4Edu is an LLM-based personalized learning simulator developed by Luis Marquez-Carpintero et al.
* [AgenticMath](agenticmath.md) - A Liu et al.
* [AgenticMathQA](agenticmathqa.md) - AgenticMathQA is a curated 30K, 60K, or 90K mathematical problem-solution dataset released by Liu et al.
* [AgentSchool](agentschool.md) - AgentSchool is an LLM-powered multi-agent simulation system for modeling, inspecting, and comparing student–teacher interactions and learning trajectories in simulated educational settings.
* [AI & Data Acumen Learning Outcomes Framework](ai-data-acumen-learning-outcomes-framework.md) - A University of Arizona faculty, student, and staff task force framework that crosses seven AI-and-data knowledge dimensions with four cognitive proficiency levels to support higher-education curriculum development and assessment.
* [AI adaptive learning environment perception scale (15 items, three dimensions)](ai-adaptive-learning-environment-perception-scale-15-items-three-dimensions.md) - A 15-item Likert self-report scale developed by Li J et al.
* [AI Certificate Pedagogical Transformation Effectiveness Evaluation Indicator System (20 indicators, S1–S20)](ai-certificate-pedagogical-transformation-effectiveness-evaluation-indicator-system-20-ind.md) - A 20-indicator, TPACK-mapped evaluation framework developed by Zijing Wu et al.
* [AI Ecological Education Policy Framework](ai-ecological-education-policy-framework.md) - A framework proposed by Chan for guiding institutional AI integration through pedagogical, governance, and operational dimensions.
* [AI Guilt Index](ai-guilt-index.md) - A four-item Likert-scale instrument developed by Diane Vassallo to measure university academic staff's guilt-related moral distress about using AI in academic practice.
* [AI Teaching & Learning Assistant](ai-teaching-learning-assistant.md) - An open-source Moodle plugin developed by Anna Ostrowska et al.
* [AI Use Scale (AIUS)](ai-use-scale-aius.md) - A researcher-developed eight-item Likert-type scale for measuring undergraduate students' frequency and intensity of using AI tools for academic tasks.
* [AI-Enabled EdTech Adoption Cycle](ai-enabled-edtech-adoption-cycle.md) - A four-phase adoption framework described by Ruiz et al.
* [AI-SEE framework](ai-see-framework.md) - The AI-SEE framework proposed by Feng Liu et al.
* [AI4K12 Five Big Ideas in Artificial Intelligence](ai4k12-five-big-ideas-in-artificial-intelligence.md) - A framework developed by the AI for K-12 working group that organizes K–12 artificial-intelligence education around five thematic strands and four grade bands.
* [AiAWE](aiawe.md) - AiAWE is an open-source Django platform developed by John Maurice Gayed and collaborators for real-time and batch automated essay scoring, custom-rubric feedback, and formative writing support.
* [AICoFe](aicofe.md) - AICoFe is a human-centered AI collaborative feedback system developed by Alvaro Becerra and colleagues that supports peer, self-, and teacher assessment through customized rubrics, multimodal performance recording, dashboards, and mediated AI-generated feedback.
* [AICoFe Visualization Module](aicofe-visualization-module.md) - AICoFe's Visualization Module is a Dash-based learning-analytics dashboard system developed by Becerra et al.
* [AIDA](aida.md) - A human-centred generative-AI digital assistant developed by the Open University to provide provenance-grounded, pedagogically configured learning support, feedback, quiz practice, and study guidance.
* [Air Tutors](air-tutors.md) - Air Tutors is a virtual high-dosage math tutoring programme run by Air Tutors that provides small-group online tutoring for upper-elementary and early-middle-school students.
* [AISSA](aissa.md) - AISSA is a web-based platform developed by Alvaro Becerra et al.
* [AITAQS four-dimensional framework of AI tutoring acceptance quality](aitaqs-four-dimensional-framework-of-ai-tutoring-acceptance-quality.md) - A learner-perceived self-report assessment framework for higher-education students that measures AI tutoring acceptance quality across feedback quality, instructional effectiveness, interaction experience, and user trust.
* [AIvaluate](aivaluate.md) - AIvaluate is a browser-based conversational assessment agent developed by Habeeb Yusuf et al.
* [ALAS dropout prevention program](alas-dropout-prevention-program.md) - ALAS is a multi-component dropout-prevention programme for middle- and high-school students that combines counselor mentoring, attendance and performance monitoring, resilience and self-control instruction, parent training, recognition activities, and community-service coordination.
* [Alder's high-touch, whole adult support approach](alders-high-touch-whole-adult-support-approach.md) - Alder's framework for proactively coordinating academic, financial, social, health, housing, and career supports for teacher-residency candidates facing barriers to completing graduate-level preparation.
* [ALEKS and ALEKS PPL behavioral trace datasets for studying AI-era learning behavior](aleks-and-aleks-ppl-behavioral-trace-datasets-for-studying-ai-era-learning-behavior.md) - Large-scale datasets made available by ALEKS containing learning interactions, placement-assessment responses, response times, and retention-item outcomes for studying mathematics learning behavior.
* [Alelo VCAT Virtual Cultural Awareness Trainers with multi-role Virtual Coach](alelo-vcat-virtual-cultural-awareness-trainers-with-multi-role-virtual-coach.md) - Alelo's VCAT software systems teach cultural knowledge and intercultural interaction through simulated encounters with virtual role-players supported by a fading virtual coach.
* [ALF (Augmented Lectures Feedback System)](alf-augmented-lectures-feedback-system.md) - ALF is an augmented-reality classroom feedback system developed by Zarraonandia and colleagues that displays students' reported understanding and responses to teachers wearing AR glasses.
* [Algebra Toolkit](algebra-toolkit.md) - The Algebra Toolkit is a Regional Educational Laboratory Central at Mathematica professional development programme that supports middle and high school algebra educators through facilitated PLC sessions, preparation, classroom implementation, and school-leader guidance.
* [AlgoRAG](algorag.md) - A retrieval-augmented generation software system developed by Sushan Adhikari for providing context-grounded assistance in theoretical computer science education.
* [Alharbi et al. (2026) Scenario Rubric, Artefact Rubric, and engagement index](alharbi-et-al-2026-scenario-rubric-artefact-rubric-and-engagement-index.md) - A set of original instruments developed by Alharbi et al.
* [Alice in Wonderland silent-versus-oral reproduction tests](alice-in-wonderland-silent-versus-oral-reproduction-tests.md) - A reading-comprehension assessment developed by Mead that has pupils read an Alice in Wonderland passage silently and orally, then reproduces and scores the points recalled under each condition.
* [Alignment and coherence framework with six interrelated domains for system improvement](alignment-and-coherence-framework-with-six-interrelated-domains-for-system-improvement.md) - A Walrond et al.
* [Alliance for Resource Equity (ARE) diagnostic toolkit organized around ten dimensions of equity](alliance-for-resource-equity-are-diagnostic-toolkit-organized-around-ten-dimensions-of-equ.md) - A toolkit developed by the Education Trust and Education Resource Strategies that helps district and school leaders diagnose inequities across ten dimensions of student experience and select guidebook actions for improvement.
* [Alternative Uses Test response dataset of over 20,000 responses from more than 2,000 participants](alternative-uses-test-response-dataset-of-over-20000-responses-from-more-than-2000-partici.md) - An open dataset of more than 20,000 Alternative Uses Test responses from over 2,000 adult participants, with study materials and analysis code released through the OSF repository by Organisciak et al.
* [Alumni Survey Roadmap](alumni-survey-roadmap.md) - The authors’ nine-step framework guides organizations through co-designing, launching, interpreting, and acting on a first alumni survey.
* [America's Teacher Corps (ATC)](americas-teacher-corps-atc.md) - A proposed federal programme to recognize highly effective K–12 public school teachers and provide eligible teachers, especially those in high-poverty Title I schools, with recognition, salary supplements, and a portable credential.
* [American Board for Certification of Teacher Excellence (ABCTE) alternative teacher certification](american-board-for-certification-of-teacher-excellence-abcte-alternative-teacher-certifica.md) - A teacher-certification pathway, including the Passport to Teaching credential, developed and administered by the American Board for Certification of Teacher Excellence for alternative-route teacher candidates.
* [American Industrial Arts Student Association (AIASA) student industrial arts clubs](american-industrial-arts-student-association-aiasa-student-industrial-arts-clubs.md) - A national student-club programme organized by the American Industrial Arts Association to familiarize students with industrial and technical fields through tours, club activities, and leadership conferences.
* [American Sign Language Assessment Instrument (ASLAI)](american-sign-language-assessment-instrument-aslai.md) - The ASLAI is a computerized battery of video-presented multiple-choice receptive ASL language tasks developed by the research team for assessing deaf students' ASL abilities and informing IEP planning.
* [Ames Philosophical Belief Inventory](ames-philosophical-belief-inventory.md) - A forced-choice questionnaire developed to measure education students' beliefs across five philosophical dimensions: Realism, Idealism, Pragmatism, Existentialism, and Phenomenology.
* [Amico dual-mode prototype (AmicoMio and AmicoTuo)](amico-dual-mode-prototype-amicomio-and-amicotuo.md) - A Benedetti-developed AI-mediated educational interaction device, deployable as a digital agent or social robot, that provides either procedural task guidance or reflective dialogue under age-sensitive configuration and governance constraints.
* [Amira](amira.md) - Amira Learning's Amira is an AI-powered personal reading tutor that assesses oral reading fluency, provides individualized reading practice and intervention, and reports diagnostic information to educators and families.
* [Amplify Science](amplify-science.md) - Amplify Science is a phenomena-based K–8 science curriculum developed and provided by Amplify Education, integrating hands-on investigation, literacy practices, modeling, and explanation to support three-dimensional science learning.
* [AMSTI](amsti.md) - The Alabama Math, Science, and Technology Initiative is a state-run programme that combines summer teacher training, school-year coaching, and rotating instructional materials kits to support hands-on, inquiry-based mathematics and science instruction.
* [An American Citizenship Course in United States History](an-american-citizenship-course-in-united-states-history.md) - A four-book United States history curriculum with an additional General Course, published for the American Citizenship League for use in American citizenship education.
* [Analyst image-detection open code, data, and stimulus release](analyst-image-detection-open-code-data-and-stimulus-release.md) - A public GitHub release by Negar Kamali and colleagues containing study code, de-identified data, and balanced real and AI-generated stimulus images for research on human detection of diffusion-model imagery.
* [Annotated oral English corpus of 4,374 recordings from 486 Chinese university students across five diagnostic dimensions](annotated-oral-english-corpus-of-4374-recordings-from-486-chinese-university-students-acro.md) - A corpus created by Chai Rui containing 4,374 recorded oral-English performances from 486 Chinese university students, scored by trained evaluators across five diagnostic dimensions for model training and evaluation.
* [Annotated R syntax and OSF repository for replicating MAIHDA analyses](annotated-r-syntax-and-osf-repository-for-replicating-maihda-analyses.md) - An OSF-hosted set of annotated R scripts and step-by-step analytical code for conducting and reproducing intersectional MAIHDA analyses, created by Keller et al.
* [Annotated student-utterance emotion dataset (224 turns) for text-based emotion recognition in math tutoring](annotated-student-utterance-emotion-dataset-224-turns-for-text-based-emotion-recognition-i.md) - Debanjana Kar et al.'s dataset contains 224 manually annotated MathDial-Bridge student turns with boredom, engagement, or neutral labels and polarity scores for developing emotion-recognition models in math tutoring dialogues.
* [Anonymized Dutch student-monitoring test-score dataset covering 15% of primary schools, 2017 to 2020](anonymized-dutch-student-monitoring-test-score-dataset-covering-15-of-primary-schools-2017.md) - A confidential, anonymized dataset assembled by Engzell et al.
* [ANSYS Workbench](ansys-workbench.md) - ANSYS Workbench is an engineering simulation platform made by Ansys, Inc., used to build and analyze finite-element models for applications such as heat transfer.
* [Anti-RAMSing toolkit: annotated R scripts and worked example dataset on OSF](anti-ramsing-toolkit-annotated-r-scripts-and-worked-example-dataset-on-osf.md) - A publicly available toolkit by Bicakci et al.
* [AntiSkillBench](antiskillbench.md) - AntiSkillBench is a benchmark dataset and evaluation suite created by Yongli Xiang et al.
* [ANVIL](anvil.md) - ANVIL is an open-source generative software pipeline developed by Noviello et al.
* [APLUS (Artificial Peer Learning Using SimStudent)](aplus-artificial-peer-learning-using-simstudent.md) - APLUS is an online learning-by-teaching environment developed by its researchers in which students tutor the SimStudent virtual agent to solve algebraic equations through guided problem solving.
* [ARC Core](arc-core.md) - ARC Core is a K–12 English and Spanish literacy curriculum developed by American Reading Company that organizes extensive reading, writing, research, and analysis into knowledge-building units integrating literacy with science, social studies, and literature.
* [ARDESOS-DIAPROVE program](ardesos-diaprove-program.md) - A 16-week classroom programme developed by the ARDESOS-DIAPROVE team that teaches critical thinking through problem-based and cooperative learning activities, contextualized problems, rubrics, and feedback.
* [Argus](argus.md) - Argus is a static-analysis triage tool developed by David Racovan et al.
* [Arizona linked education–child welfare student database](arizona-linked-educationchild-welfare-student-database.md) - A study-created database linking longitudinal K–12 education records from the Arizona Department of Education with child records from the Arizona Department of Child Safety to identify and describe students in foster care.
* [ARPG+ dual-process real-time coaching system](arpg-dual-process-real-time-coaching-system.md) - ARPG+ is a real-time AI coaching system developed by Ye et al.
* [Arthur book-based educational television program](arthur-book-based-educational-television-program.md) - Arthur is a PBS Kids educational television programme based on Marc Brown’s storybooks, produced and distributed for young children through PBS stations and online materials.
* [Arts for Learning (A4L) Lessons Project](arts-for-learning-a4l-lessons-project.md) - A Young Audiences, Inc.
* [ASAP 2.0 dataset](asap-20-dataset.md) - The ASAP 2.0 dataset is a public benchmark of approximately 24,000 student argumentative essays with human-assigned scores from 1 to 6, developed for automated essay-scoring research.
* [ASE-26](ase-26.md) - ASE-26 is Mikael Gorsky's twenty-one-module undergraduate curriculum for teaching agentic software engineering to engineering students with foundational computer science and software-engineering knowledge.
* [ASEAN subregional EE curriculum prototypes for primary and secondary schools and pre-/in-service teacher training](asean-subregional-ee-curriculum-prototypes-for-primary-and-secondary-schools-and-pre-in-se.md) - A set of adaptable environmental-education curriculum prototypes for primary and secondary schools and teacher education, developed by UNESCO-UNEP's International Environmental Education Programme with Malaysia's Curriculum Development Centre and the University of the Philippines' ISMED.
* [Ask Me Anything (AMA)](ask-me-anything-ama.md) - A Python and ChatGPT API chatbot developed by Saniya Vahedian Movahed et al.
* [Aspiring to Educate](aspiring-to-educate.md) - A Pennsylvania state-supported programme that creates multiple pathways into teaching through preparation partnerships and financial supports for high school students, adults, paraprofessionals, and college students.
* [ASReview](asreview.md) - ASReview is an open-source machine-learning platform developed for efficient abstract screening in systematic reviews by iteratively prioritizing records for reviewer classification.
* [Assessment Integration Management (AIM)](assessment-integration-management-aim.md) - Assessment Integration Management (AIM) is a web-based system for creating tests, uploading test models and item pools, and running large-scale computerized-based-exam simulations with configurable testing and student parameters.
* [ASSISTments](assistments.md) - ASSISTments is a free web-based platform developed and operated by the ASSISTments Project for assigning mathematics practice, providing feedback, and reporting student-performance data to teachers.
* [ASSISTments2009, EdNet-KT1, and XES3G5M knowledge-tracing benchmark datasets](assistments2009-ednet-kt1-and-xes3g5m-knowledge-tracing-benchmark-datasets.md) - A set of publicly used learner–question interaction datasets from online tutoring and exercise platforms, used to develop and evaluate knowledge-tracing models.
* [ASTRA](astra.md) - ASTRA is an end-to-end air-traffic-control training simulator developed by Ethan Chew and colleagues that automates the simpilot role through speech recognition, response generation, and geospatial scenario visualization.
* [ASU CreateAI Chat platform](asu-createai-chat-platform.md) - ASU CreateAI Chat is a secure, institutionally managed Arizona State University platform that provides a closed-system GPT-4o environment with retrieval-augmented generation for working with approved sources.
* [Asyncform (AF) dataset](asyncform-af-dataset.md) - A corpus of 863 AI-generated educational questions in English, Mathematics, and Science, labeled by K–12 teachers with Bloom Taxonomy levels and related subject, grade-level, objective, and question metadata; it was created and released by Michael Lawrence Castanares et al.
* [ATI-UP](ati-up.md) - ATI-UP is a preventive, school-wide, multi-tiered attendance intervention developed for school teams to promote attendance and reduce chronic absenteeism through problem-solving, family and community engagement, attendance promotion, and behavior motivation.
* [Attachment and Biobehavioral Catch-up (ABC) intervention](attachment-and-biobehavioral-catch-up-abc-intervention.md) - A manualized 10-session parenting programme developed by Mary Dozier and colleagues and delivered by trained interventionists to help caregivers provide nurturing, synchronous care to young children who have experienced adversity.
* [Attitude Scale towards Scientific Knowledge (SKS)](attitude-scale-towards-scientific-knowledge-sks.md) - The SKS is a 25-item five-point Likert assessment developed by Ayvacı (2007) to measure attitudes toward scientific knowledge and views about its nature.
* [Attitudes Toward Trafficked Women and Sex Trafficking Scale (ATTS)](attitudes-toward-trafficked-women-and-sex-trafficking-scale-atts.md) - The ATTS is a modified 29-item attitude scale developed by Litam in collaboration with the original APPS developers to measure counselors' views of trafficked women and sex trafficking using person-first language.
* [Auditory consonant-span immediate memory test with positional scoring (3/2/1 marks)](auditory-consonant-span-immediate-memory-test-with-positional-scoring-321-marks.md) - A classroom-administered auditory immediate-memory test in which learners recall six-consonant sequences and receive weighted positional scores, used by Winch to assess mental fatigue in schoolchildren.
* [Augustus F. Hawkins Center for Excellence grant program](augustus-f-hawkins-center-for-excellence-grant-program.md) - A competitive federal grant programme administered by the U.S.
* [Aurora Institute's Seven Critical Elements of Instructional Practice](aurora-institutes-seven-critical-elements-of-instructional-practice.md) - A framework developed by the Aurora Institute to guide competency-based instructional practice through learner agency, meaningful assessment, differentiated support, mastery-based progression, varied pathways and pacing, equity strategies, and transparent expectations.
* [Austrian national educational standard assessment 2012 (mathematics, eighth grade)](austrian-national-educational-standard-assessment-2012-mathematics-eighth-grade.md) - A BIFIE-administered census assessment measuring mathematics achievement and math academic self-concept among Austria's eighth-grade students in 2012.
* [AutoGen, MetaGPT, CrewAI, LangGraph, and Phidata](autogen-metagpt-crewai-langgraph-and-phidata.md) - A set of software platforms developed by their respective makers for building, orchestrating, and deploying agentic AI systems, including multi-agent workflows and role-based delegation.
* [Automated Extended Essay Grading Software (AEEGS)](automated-extended-essay-grading-software-aeegs.md) - A web-based application developed by Olaoye DD et al.
* [AutoTutor Script Authoring Tool (ASAT) and generic AutoTutor Conversation Engine (ACE)](autotutor-script-authoring-tool-asat-and-generic-autotutor-conversation-engine-ace.md) - The AutoTutor project’s ASAT authoring tool and ACE conversation engine let organizations create and deploy scripted conversational tutoring applications across domains.
* [Ayres scale](ayres-scale.md) - A standardized handwriting-legibility rating scale developed by Leonard P.

#### B {: #letter-b }

* [Bachelor's Degree Attainment Index (Bachelor's DAI) and Direct Bachelor's DAI](bachelors-degree-attainment-index-bachelors-dai-and-direct-bachelors-dai.md) - These are UChicago Consortium measurement metrics estimating the proportion of a ninth-grade cohort that earns a bachelor's degree, distinguishing direct four-year pathways from pathways involving delayed entry or two-year colleges.
* [Balance Board Math (BBM)](balance-board-math-bbm.md) - Balance Board Math is a sensor-equipped balance-board environment and interface developed by Sofia Tancredi's design-based research project to let children explore mathematical functions through rocking movements, real-time graphs, colors, and tones.
* [Balanced 192-text evaluation dataset of EFL, professional, AI-generated, and hybrid texts](balanced-192-text-evaluation-dataset-of-efl-professional-ai-generated-and-hybrid-texts.md) - A research dataset created by Mohammad Hadra et al.
* [Balanced Leadership® professional development](balanced-leadership-professional-development.md) - A McREL International professional-development programme for current and aspiring school leaders, delivered through facilitated training with optional coaching and online support to develop leadership actions and behaviours.
* [Balloons: A Concepts About Print Assessment](balloons-a-concepts-about-print-assessment.md) - A locally constructed, criterion-referenced 17-item assessment in the Kindergarten Assessment Portfolio used by program teachers to measure kindergarten pupils' concepts about print.
* [BASIE](basie.md) - A downloadable software tool released by John Deke et al.
* [BASIE R and Stan code](basie-r-and-stan-code.md) - The BASIE R and Stan code is a released implementation by John Deke et al.
* [Basketball Mathematics (BM) intervention program](basketball-mathematics-bm-intervention-program.md) - An 8-week programme run by trained instructors that integrates fraction learning into basketball drills within existing physical-education lessons for grades 6–7.
* [BATS-R-US (Block-Adaptive-Tree Solarwind Roe Upwind Scheme)](bats-r-us-block-adaptive-tree-solarwind-roe-upwind-scheme.md) - A three-dimensional global magnetohydrodynamics simulation code developed by the University of Michigan and made publicly runnable through NASA's Community Coordinated Modeling Center.
* [Battery measuring explicit and implicit beliefs about science and knowledge about how science works](battery-measuring-explicit-and-implicit-beliefs-about-science-and-knowledge-about-how-scie.md) - A researcher-developed battery for university students measuring explicit and implicit beliefs about science and knowledge of how science works, used by Schoor and colleagues.
* [Battery of twelve tests for originality with self-recording time device](battery-of-twelve-tests-for-originality-with-self-recording-time-device.md) - A Chassell (1916) battery of twelve individually or group-administered tests for measuring and ranking originality in university students and adults, using oral and placard instructions and a self-recorded time device.
* [Bayes Net Toolbox for Matlab](bayes-net-toolbox-for-matlab.md) - Bayes Net Toolbox for Matlab is a software toolbox developed by Kevin Murphy for representing, manipulating, and performing inference with Bayesian networks.
* [bcause](bcause.md) - bcause is open-source software for causal inference in Bayesian and credal networks, supporting exact Bayesian-network inference and interventional and counterfactual queries.
* [Beable](beable.md) - Beable is a K–12 digital learning platform developed by Beable Life-Ready Learning that personalizes literacy, content learning, language acquisition, intervention, and career-connected learning for students.
* [Beable Academy](beable-academy.md) - Beable Academy is Beable's self-paced online professional-development programme for educators, complemented by orientation and ongoing platform support.
* [Beacon](beacon.md) - Beacon is a web-based conversational academic-support system developed by Andy Gray that uses retrieval-augmented generation over approved course teaching materials to assist undergraduate computing students.
* [Becoming Effective Learners Student Survey (BEL-S)](becoming-effective-learners-student-survey-bel-s.md) - The BEL-S is a Rasch-based student self-report survey developed by Camille A.
* [Bell Chemistry Test and appended Payne correction sheet](bell-chemistry-test-and-appended-payne-correction-sheet.md) - A 25-question secondary-school chemistry achievement test with a Payne-prepared correction and partial-credit scoring sheet, originally used to assess knowledge of electrolysis, oxidation-reduction, valence, water purification, gases, and commercial chemistry.
* [Berea College Promise Neighborhood initiative](berea-college-promise-neighborhood-initiative.md) - A Berea College–run place-based education initiative serving students and communities across Clay, Jackson, and Owsley Counties in rural Appalachian Kentucky.
* [Berlin end-of-schooling tracking dataset (2,155 students, 87 schools)](berlin-end-of-schooling-tracking-dataset-2155-students-87-schools.md) - A research dataset from Dumont et al.
* [Bernauer and Tomei's integrated matrix of five faculty integrator quadrants](bernauer-and-tomeis-integrated-matrix-of-five-faculty-integrator-quadrants.md) - A higher-education faculty-development framework that organizes competencies, objectives, and teaching practices into five stages of pedagogical and technology integration, from apprentice to master integrator.
* [Best Practice Framework for Online Safety Education](best-practice-framework-for-online-safety-education.md) - A national framework developed by Walsh et al.
* [bettermarks](bettermarks.md) - bettermarks is a mathematics intelligent tutoring system that provides adaptive practice, detects learner errors and knowledge gaps, and assigns targeted learning materials for school students.
* [BFpack](bfpack.md) - BFpack is an R package developed by Mulder and colleagues that provides Bayesian factor hypothesis-testing functions for common statistical models and exploratory or confirmatory tests with equality and order constraints.
* [BILearn-CS](bilearn-cs.md) - BILearn-CS is an instructor-authored and teaching-assistant-validated benchmark dataset of 600 programming-support queries for evaluating educational responses in introductory programming and data-structures contexts.
* [Bilingual benchmark of 1107 Chinese-English children's stories with silver developmental references and structured annotations](bilingual-benchmark-of-1107-chinese-english-childrens-stories-with-silver-developmental-re.md) - A bilingual benchmark dataset constructed by Sijing Yin et al.
* [Bilingual Cooperative Integrated Reading and Composition (BCIRC)](bilingual-cooperative-integrated-reading-and-composition-bcirc.md) - BCIRC is a bilingual adaptation of the Cooperative Integrated Reading and Composition program that organizes Spanish- and English-language reading, writing, vocabulary, and comprehension instruction in cooperative student teams for Spanish-speaking learners.
* [Bilingual Cultural Map Interface](bilingual-cultural-map-interface.md) - An open-source AI-assisted bilingual geospatial visualization prototype created by the DiscoverKunshan project for mapping culinary and cultural heritage sites through categorized markers, filters, and bilingual information panels.
* [Blueprint for Statewide Pathways Scaling](blueprint-for-statewide-pathways-scaling.md) - A Babineau et al.
* [Blueprint Math Fellows program](blueprint-math-fellows-program.md) - A middle-school mathematics tutoring programme operated by Blueprint that uses AmeriCorps volunteers for daily in-person group tutoring supplemented by a web-based learning platform.
* [BMED 2300 benchmark corpus and evaluation harness](bmed-2300-benchmark-corpus-and-evaluation-harness.md) - A benchmark dataset and evaluation harness developed by Md Zabirul Islam et al.
* [BNL (Bayesian networks with logistic regression nodes)](bnl-bayesian-networks-with-logistic-regression-nodes.md) - A Matlab toolbox for specifying Bayesian networks with logistic regression nodes and related statistical models, developed by Rijmen.
* [Book-cover recognition test](book-cover-recognition-test.md) - A child-completed picture-book familiarity assessment developed by Davidse et al.
* [BookNook](booknook.md) - BookNook is a provider-run virtual, synchronous high-dosage reading tutoring programme for K–8 students, using a science-of-reading curriculum focused on phonological awareness, phonics, fluency, vocabulary, and comprehension.
* [Boston Naming Test (BNT)](boston-naming-test-bnt.md) - A 60-item confrontation-naming assessment developed by Kaplan, Goodglass, and Weintraub that measures expressive vocabulary by asking examinees to name increasingly difficult line drawings, with stimulus and phonemic cues after errors.
* [Boston University Prison Education Program](boston-university-prison-education-program.md) - A degree-granting liberal arts higher-education programme run by Boston University in Massachusetts correctional facilities for incarcerated adults, funded through university scholarships and leading to a Bachelor of Liberal Arts degree.
* [Bottom Line](bottom-line.md) - Bottom Line is an intensive advising programme run by Bottom Line that supports primarily low-income, first-generation students with college applications, financial aid, college choice, and postsecondary persistence.
* [Bottom Line College Advising Program](bottom-line-college-advising-program.md) - A branded, multi-year college-access and persistence advising program developed and operated by the nonprofit Bottom Line for low-income and first-generation students.
* [Bridges Educator Site](bridges-educator-site.md) - The Bridges Educator Site is a web-based platform operated by The Math Learning Center that provides digital Bridges curriculum materials, interactive displays, and optional digital manipulatives for teachers and students.
* [Bridges in Mathematics](bridges-in-mathematics.md) - Bridges in Mathematics is a comprehensive preK–5 mathematics curriculum developed by The Math Learning Center that combines direct instruction, investigation, exploration, discourse, visual models, and hands-on activities.
* [Bridgit](bridgit.md) - Bridgit is a school-counseling platform used to track students’ pre-matriculation tasks, tailor college-enrollment steps, and support counselor communication.
* [Bright Beginnings](bright-beginnings.md) - Bright Beginnings is a child-centered, literacy-focused preschool curriculum developed for early childhood education, comprising nine thematic units with classroom and home activities and parent involvement.
* [British Council Knowledge-based Vocabulary Lists (KVL) difficulty dataset](british-council-knowledge-based-vocabulary-lists-kvl-difficulty-dataset.md) - A dataset developed from the British Council's Knowledge-based Vocabulary Lists containing productive English-vocabulary test items and GLMM-based difficulty scores for Chinese, German, and Spanish learners of English.
* [Briya family literacy program](briya-family-literacy-program.md) - Briya Public Charter School runs this free Washington, D.C., family-literacy programme, providing adults with English, digital-literacy, and parenting education while their children receive early education.
* [Buffalo Promise Neighborhood](buffalo-promise-neighborhood.md) - Buffalo Promise Neighborhood is a local Promise Neighborhoods initiative serving a high-poverty Buffalo community through coordinated education, early-childhood, and family-support programming.
* [Building Assets, Reducing Risks (BARR)](building-assets-reducing-risks-barr.md) - A schoolwide K–12 training and coaching program run by the BARR Center that integrates relationship-building, coordinated staff practices, and systematic academic and non-academic data use to improve student outcomes.
* [Building Blocks for Math](building-blocks-for-math.md) - A preschool mathematics curriculum from SRA Real Math that sequences instruction and software tasks along empirically based learning trajectories linking children's informal mathematical knowledge to formal concepts.
* [Building Decision Skills](building-decision-skills.md) - Building Decision Skills is a character-education curriculum developed by the Institute for Global Ethics for middle and high school students, using lessons and supporting materials to develop ethical awareness and decision-making strategies.
* [Building Pathways for Student Success (BPSS)](building-pathways-for-student-success-bpss.md) - A partnership-led logic model supporting the Arkansas Department of Education in examining and improving college- and career-readiness programming for Arkansas students.
* [By Heart](by-heart.md) - A memorization application that uses one card type with three learner-selectable retrieval modes of varying cueing difficulty for practicing poems, scripts, and other extended passages verbatim.

#### C {: #letter-c }

* [CALI (Content-Area Literacy Instruction)](cali-content-area-literacy-instruction.md) - CALI is an individualized K–4 content-area literacy curriculum developed by Dombek et al.
* [California Classified School Employee Teacher Credentialing Program (Grow Your Own)](california-classified-school-employee-teacher-credentialing-program-grow-your-own.md) - A California state programme that funds and supports classified school employees and paraprofessionals in completing undergraduate education and teacher preparation to become credentialed teachers.
* [California College Promise Guidebook](california-college-promise-guidebook.md) - A California College Promise planning guidebook and toolkit developed by the California College Promise Project with worksheets, cost-estimation tools, and example implementation artifacts for college leaders and partners.
* [California Community Schools Partnership Program (CCSPP)](california-community-schools-partnership-program-ccspp.md) - California's state-approved community-schools framework, administered through the California Department of Education, organizes grantee implementation and reporting around four areas and 16 features for whole-child school transformation.
* [California Educator Effectiveness Block Grant](california-educator-effectiveness-block-grant.md) - A California state grant programme that funds local educational agencies' professional learning and related educator-effectiveness, school-climate, social-emotional learning, mental-health, restorative-justice, and equity activities.
* [California Golden State Teacher Grant (GSTG) Program](california-golden-state-teacher-grant-gstg-program.md) - A California Student Aid Commission programme that provides grants to teacher and Pupil Personnel Services candidates in exchange for service in priority schools or California State Preschool Programs.
* [California Healthy Kids Survey (CHKS)](california-healthy-kids-survey-chks.md) - The California Department of Education's California Healthy Kids Survey, administered statewide with WestEd, is a K–12 survey system that measures student well-being, school conditions, and related behavioral-health outcomes.
* [California Practitioners' Guide for Educating English Learners with Disabilities](california-practitioners-guide-for-educating-english-learners-with-disabilities.md) - A California Department of Education practitioner guide providing research-based guidance on assessment, pre-referral supports, and special-education referral practices for English learners with disabilities.
* [California School Staff Survey (CSSS)](california-school-staff-survey-csss.md) - A California Department of Education staff survey, developed and administered with WestEd, that measures school staff perceptions of school climate conditions including instructional equity and respect for diversity.
* [California's job-embedded administrator induction model](californias-job-embedded-administrator-induction-model.md) - California's two-year induction programme for Administrative Services Credential holders, administered through Commission-approved providers and combining individualized coaching, professional development, and standards-aligned assessments.
* [California's Statewide System of Support (SSOS)](californias-statewide-system-of-support-ssos.md) - A California state-run system coordinated by the CCEE, CDE, and SBE that provides tiered technical assistance, tools, and resources to local educational agencies and schools.
* [CALPADS student-level longitudinal course record dataset](calpads-student-level-longitudinal-course-record-dataset.md) - A California Department of Education administrative dataset containing student-level longitudinal K–12 course records for federal reporting and education research.
* [Cambridge Oracy Assessment Toolkit](cambridge-oracy-assessment-toolkit.md) - A research-informed set of teacher-administered tasks and procedures developed by Cambridge researchers for assessing the spoken-language skills of students aged 11–12.
* [Canfield's Learning Styles Inventory and Instructional Styles Inventory](canfields-learning-styles-inventory-and-instructional-styles-inventory.md) - A pair of multidimensional inventories developed by Canfield to assess students' learning styles and teachers' instructional styles and compare their congruence for instructional counselling.
* [Capacity Building for Strategic Learning (CBSL) pilot](capacity-building-for-strategic-learning-cbsl-pilot.md) - A Mathematica and Catalyst:Ed programme, supported by the Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation, that built nine K–12 education grantees’ equity-centered strategic-learning capacity through project scoping, provider matching, cohort learning, and thought partnership.
* [CAPIT Reading](capit-reading.md) - CAPIT Reading is a blended supplemental PK–3 phonics programme providing explicit, systematic Sound-to-Print instruction through individualized digital learning, immediate feedback, and educator data.
* [CAPS](caps.md) - CAPS is a LISP-based interpreter and production-system software developed by Thibadeau to implement the READER model and related cognitive-processing models.
* [Career Academies](career-academies.md) - Career Academies is a school-within-a-school programme model that combines small learning communities, career-themed academic and career courses, work experience, and partnerships with employers, higher education, and the community.
* [Career-connected learning (CCL) framework](career-connected-learning-ccl-framework.md) - A Chicago Public Schools framework that coordinates college-preparatory and career-readiness activities across grades 6–12 through strengthened CTE and districtwide planning.
* [Caring School Community™](caring-school-community.md) - A multiyear K–6 school improvement programme developed by the Developmental Studies Center that uses class meetings, cross-age buddies, family activities, and schoolwide community-building to promote prosocial behavior and shared values.
* [Carnegie Learning High School Math Solution Algebra I](carnegie-learning-high-school-math-solution-algebra-i.md) - A Carnegie Learning first-year Algebra I curriculum that combines textbook and workbook materials with self-paced individualized instruction through automated tutoring software.
* [CASEL 5](casel-5.md) - A framework developed by the Collaborative for Academic, Social, and Emotional Learning (CASEL) that organizes social and emotional competence into five interrelated areas: self-awareness, self-management, social awareness, relationship skills, and responsible decision-making.
* [CASEL Wheel](casel-wheel.md) - A framework developed by CASEL that organizes five interrelated social and emotional learning competencies across classrooms, schools, families and caregivers, and communities.
* [Catapult Learning's Wraparound Supplemental Educational Services](catapult-learnings-wraparound-supplemental-educational-services.md) - A Catapult Learning supplemental-education programme that delivers AchieveLiteracy and AchieveMath curricula through structured, low-ratio small-group instruction in literacy and mathematics.
* [Causal Dimension Scale (CDS) and revised CDSII](causal-dimension-scale-cds-and-revised-cdsii.md) - The CDS, developed by Russell, and its CDSII revision by McAuley, Duncan, and Russell are self-report assessments that measure perceived causal attributions across dimensions such as locus, stability, and controllability.
* [CAVA360VR](cava360vr.md) - CAVA360VR is a virtual-reality software platform developed for collaborative analysis, annotation, discussion, and debate around 360-degree video recordings.
* [CBM Computation progress-monitoring probes for first-grade math](cbm-computation-progress-monitoring-probes-for-first-grade-math.md) - These are brief, standardized first-grade mathematics computation probes used to monitor student progress in RTI, with performance scored by correctly computed digits.
* [CBM ID-TABLET](cbm-id-tablet.md) - Patricia A.
* [cc-self-train auto-updating sync pipeline](cc-self-train-auto-updating-sync-pipeline.md) - A Zain Naboulsi onboarding and maintenance pipeline that detects upstream Claude Code changes, updates affected teaching modules, and verifies their structural integrity.
* [CCC product badges](ccc-product-badges.md) - A set of three competency-based badges proposed for vendors to demonstrate alignment of educational products with community college student and faculty priorities, including diverse lived experiences, continuous improvement, and faculty agency and connection.
* [CCSR five-category framework of noncognitive factors](ccsr-five-category-framework-of-noncognitive-factors.md) - A research-synthesis framework developed by the University of Chicago Consortium on Chicago School Research (CCSR) that organises noncognitive factors into academic behaviors, academic perseverance, social skills, learning strategies, and academic mindsets.
* [CCSR longitudinal data archive on Chicago Public Schools](ccsr-longitudinal-data-archive-on-chicago-public-schools.md) - A linked longitudinal administrative and survey database of Chicago Public Schools students, schools, personnel, achievement, course-taking, postsecondary outcomes, and organizational conditions maintained by the Consortium on Chicago School Research.
* [CCSR theory of action](ccsr-theory-of-action.md) - A Consortium on Chicago School Research (CCSR) framework describing how interactive research-practice partnerships build district capacity through an ongoing search for solutions.
* [CCSSO's implementation chain](ccssos-implementation-chain.md) - A phased, multi-level implementation framework developed by the Council of Chief State School Officers (CCSSO) to guide coordinated adoption, implementation, and sustainability of educational initiatives.
* [CCTV-view classroom incident recognition benchmark (synthetic plus real-world preschool pose data)](cctv-view-classroom-incident-recognition-benchmark-synthetic-plus-real-world-preschool-pos.md) - A privacy-aware benchmark dataset from Paritosh Parmar et al.
* [CE-S-DSP & SOS hybrid self-report measure of cognitive engagement](ce-s-dsp-sos-hybrid-self-report-measure-of-cognitive-engagement.md) - A hybrid self-report assessment assembled by Meg Guerreiro (2017) from existing cognitive-engagement measures to assess students' reported cognitive engagement, including in grades 6–8 mathematics.
* [Center of Black Student Excellence](center-of-black-student-excellence.md) - A planned Long Beach Unified School District entity that provides tutoring, mentoring, cultural programming, wellness services, legal aid, career support, and advocacy for Black students and families.
* [Cerrado Succession Hypothesis (CSH) qualitative model library](cerrado-succession-hypothesis-csh-qualitative-model-library.md) - A GARP-based qualitative ecological model library developed by Anders Bouwer and collaborators to simulate Cerrado vegetation succession, population dynamics, and fire-management effects through composable model fragments and scenarios.
* [CGScholar AI Helper](cgscholar-ai-helper.md) - CGScholar AI Helper is a cloud-based web application made by CGScholar that provides rubric-guided, teacher-steerable AI feedback to support student writing and revision.
* [Challenge Based Learning (CBL) Framework](challenge-based-learning-cbl-framework.md) - A challenge-driven learning framework developed and maintained through Apple-supported Challenge Based Learning initiatives for educators and learners working on authentic real-world challenges.
* [Chapter One](chapter-one.md) - Chapter One is a push-in, one-on-one early-literacy tutoring programme that embeds interventionists in K–1 classrooms and provides aligned digital practice, operated by Chapter One.
* [Charter School Response to the Pandemic Survey](charter-school-response-to-the-pandemic-survey.md) - A two-wave fixed-response and Likert-scale survey instrument developed and administered by the Center for Research on Education Outcomes (CREDO) to document charter-school responses to COVID-19 closures.
* [Charter Schools Program (CSP) technical-assistance set-aside](charter-schools-program-csp-technical-assistance-set-aside.md) - A federally funded component of the U.S.
* [Charting Reform](charting-reform.md) - These Consortium on Chicago School Research survey instruments collect students' and teachers' views of school environment, classroom learning, parent involvement, governance, and teachers' professional work life.
* [ChatGPT-FLGA learning system](chatgpt-flga-learning-system.md) - A ChatGPT-based learning system designed for flipped or online university courses to support learners' self-regulated learning, self-efficacy, motivation, and creative thinking.
* [ChatTutor](chattutor.md) - ChatTutor is a course-grounded GenAI chatbot developed by Guido Makransky and colleagues that engages students in personalized teaching conversations, prompts self-explanation, and provides targeted feedback based on course materials.
* [Cheating Tendency (TC) scale](cheating-tendency-tc-scale.md) - A 20-item, four-dimension Likert self-report scale developed by Yilmaz A to measure undergraduate students' cognitive predisposition toward academic dishonesty, including AI-assisted cheating.
* [Check & Connect](check-connect.md) - Check & Connect is a dropout-prevention programme developed and implemented through monitored student performance, individualized mentoring, case management, and coordinated support from school, family, and community partners.
* [Chicago Annenberg Challenge](chicago-annenberg-challenge.md) - A school-improvement initiative that organized schools into networks supported by external partner organizations and was run by the Chicago Annenberg Challenge.
* [Chicago High School Redesign Initiative](chicago-high-school-redesign-initiative.md) - A school-redesign initiative that supported the creation and development of small Chicago public high schools through partnerships among Chicago Public Schools, funders, and participating school communities.
* [Chicago Public Education Fund](chicago-public-education-fund.md) - The Chicago Public Education Fund's framework and initiative support the concentration and development of talented teachers and school leaders to promote school improvement.
* [Chicago Public Schools Elementary On-Track indicator](chicago-public-schools-elementary-on-track-indicator.md) - A Chicago Public Schools indicator system that classifies elementary students by GPA and attendance thresholds to identify academic and attendance support needs and monitor later educational outcomes, including its four-category Condensed EOT version.
* [Chicago Public Schools' school diagnostic data reports](chicago-public-schools-school-diagnostic-data-reports.md) - These are data-reporting products run by Chicago Public Schools that display grade and attendance patterns by student background and over time for school-improvement planning.
* [Chicago Public Schools' three-pronged theory of change](chicago-public-schools-three-pronged-theory-of-change.md) - A Chicago Public Schools reform framework linking improved classroom instruction, school-level leadership, and differentiated district support to instructional improvement and student learning.
* [Chicago Public Schools’ on-track system](chicago-public-schools-on-track-system.md) - A Chicago Public Schools framework that combines practical student data reports with professional supports to help school staff identify and keep students on track during their first year of high school.
* [Children and Youth Behavioral Health Initiative (CYBHI)](children-and-youth-behavioral-health-initiative-cybhi.md) - A California statewide initiative that builds an upstream behavioral-health ecosystem for children and youth through screening, prevention, treatment capacity, awareness, and access to evidence-based care.
* [Children's Progress Academic Assessment](childrens-progress-academic-assessment.md) - The CPAA is a computerized adaptive assessment developed for preschool settings to measure children's literacy and mathematical skills and supplement teacher ratings of developmental progress.
* [Chinese version of the Sleep Self-Report (SSR)](chinese-version-of-the-sleep-self-report-ssr.md) - A translated and psychometrically validated 23-item, 3-point self-report assessment developed by Chen et al.
* [Chiu et al. (2022) 12-chapter school-based AI curriculum](chiu-et-al-2022-12-chapter-school-based-ai-curriculum.md) - A year-long, 12-chapter secondary-school AI curriculum covering machine learning, computer vision, speech and language processing, ethics, societal implications, and the future of work, developed by Chiu et al.
* [Chormmunity](chormmunity.md) - Chormmunity is a named participatory workshop programme run by its creators, using conversation, imagery, movement, and ritual performance to develop co-created work.
* [Chronic Stereotype Threat Scale](chronic-stereotype-threat-scale.md) - A seven-item, six-point Likert self-report scale adapted from established stereotype-threat measures to assess secondary students' chronic stereotype-threat experiences in academic settings.
* [Chula Vista Promise Neighborhood](chula-vista-promise-neighborhood.md) - A place-based Promise Neighborhood community programme serving the Castle Park neighborhood of Chula Vista, California, across early childhood, education, college and career readiness, child development, and family support.
* [Ciena Solutions Challenge](ciena-solutions-challenge.md) - Ciena's challenge-based learning initiative provides educators and students with global problem-solving projects, a resource library, facilitation guidance, a challenge builder, and related learning materials.
* [Cignition](cignition.md) - Cignition is an online mathematics tutoring programme that combines one-to-one teacher tutoring with the FogStone Isle fractions game, operated by Cignition Learning.
* [CIRCLS](circls.md) - CIRCLS.org is a Digital Promise–maintained online community hub connecting and supporting NSF-funded research on emerging technologies for teaching and learning.
* [Cities of Learning](cities-of-learning.md) - A digital platform led by Nichole Pinkard with NSF support that aggregates, maps, and describes youth learning resources across cities and connects learners with opportunities, badges, routes, and community members.
* [Civic Online Reasoning Video Library](civic-online-reasoning-video-library.md) - A library of videos developed by the Civic Online Reasoning project to teach students and educators how to evaluate online information and data sources.
* [Civil Rights Data Collection (CRDC)](civil-rights-data-collection-crdc.md) - A biennial school- and district-level dataset collected and maintained by the U.S.
* [CLaaS (Cybersecurity Labs-as-a-Service)](claas-cybersecurity-labs-as-a-service.md) - CLaaS is an AWS-based, browser-accessible cybersecurity lab platform developed for university cybersecurity courses, with an integrated OCR-to-LLM assistant for simplifying lab instructions.
* [CLARA (Collaboration Literacy through Artifact Reasoning and Augmentation)](clara-collaboration-literacy-through-artifact-reasoning-and-augmentation.md) - CLARA is an agentic analytics system developed by Dawei Xie and colleagues that transcribes discussions, generates and indexes collaboration artifacts, and provides artifact-grounded analyses for learners and educators.
* [Claremont Critical Social Justice Teaching Competencies](claremont-critical-social-justice-teaching-competencies.md) - A framework used by Claremont's Teacher Education Program to integrate critical social justice teaching competencies across coursework, clinical practice, reflection, and evaluation for teacher candidates.
* [Clark and Malecki 10-item Academic Grit Scale (Turkish adaptation)](clark-and-malecki-10-item-academic-grit-scale-turkish-adaptation.md) - A 10-item, single-factor, five-point Likert self-report scale adapted into Turkish by Kaya et al.
* [CLASP](clasp.md) - A three-quarter introductory physics curriculum for bioscience majors, developed and run at UC Davis and implemented in other university settings.
* [Classroom Assessment Scoring System Pre-K (CLASS Pre-K)](classroom-assessment-scoring-system-pre-k-class-pre-k.md) - A standardized observational assessment developed by Teachstone that rates the quality of teacher–child interactions across Emotional Support, Classroom Organization, and Instructional Support domains in early-childhood classrooms.
* [Classroom Discourse Analyser (CDA) platform](classroom-discourse-analyser-cda-platform.md) - A platform that processes classroom videos into synchronised transcripts, speaker-turn visualisations, and participation statistics for teacher professional learning and student reflection.
* [Classroom Organization and Management Program](classroom-organization-and-management-program.md) - A manualized teacher-training classroom-management programme that equips teachers to implement strategies for organizing classrooms and reducing student problem behavior.
* [Classroom Video Analysis (CVA) assessment](classroom-video-analysis-cva-assessment.md) - A video-based assessment developed by Ramirez and colleagues that elicits and scores teachers' usable mathematical teaching knowledge through written analyses of authentic mathematics-instruction episodes.
* [Classtime](classtime.md) - Classtime is a web-based formative-assessment platform made by Classtime that lets educators deliver interactive question sets, view responses and dashboards in real time, and provide feedback across subjects.
* [ClassWide Peer Tutoring](classwide-peer-tutoring.md) - A peer-assisted instructional programme in which students tutor one another in structured pairs with immediate feedback, practice, and reinforcement, developed for integration with existing curricula.
* [Classworks](classworks.md) - Classworks is an online, assessment-driven tiered intervention platform that generates individualized learning paths and units for students, used by schools and educators.
* [CLICKSTREAM](clickstream.md) - CLICKSTREAM is a cloud-hosted learning-data infrastructure service developed for educators and researchers to collect, enrich, and analyze timestamped interaction data across digital learning platforms.
* [Clinical Interview for Moral Injury—Military Version](clinical-interview-for-moral-injurymilitary-version.md) - The CIMI-M is a structured, non-diagnostic clinical interview developed by de Goede et al.
* [CNMCC Breaking Through Construction Apprenticeship Program](cnmcc-breaking-through-construction-apprenticeship-program.md) - A construction-apprenticeship program that integrates accelerated basic-skills instruction with streamlined curriculum, flexible delivery, industry links, alignment, and intensive support services, run by CNMCC.
* [Co-Census](co-census.md) - Co-Census is interactive museum software that collects visitor identity and life-context data and uses it to personalize visualizations and support dialogue among visitors.
* [CoBi](cobi.md) - CoBi is an AI-based classroom-wide collaboration support system developed by Breideband et al.
* [CocoFlow](cocoflow.md) - CocoFlow is an AI agent builder platform developed by COCOROBO Limited that enables teachers to create subject-specific pedagogical agents through prompt engineering, uploaded knowledge bases, and visual workflow design.
* [CODE-GEN](code-gen.md) - CODE-GEN is a retrieval-augmented, dual-agent educational AI system developed by Xiaojing Duan et al.
* [CodeCarbon](codecarbon.md) - CodeCarbon is an open-source Python package developed by Mila and collaborators that estimates the carbon emissions of local computing workloads from hardware power use and electricity-grid carbon intensity.
* [COEMET — Classroom Observation of Early Mathematics Environment and Teaching](coemet-classroom-observation-of-early-mathematics-environment-and-teaching.md) - A live classroom-observation instrument developed by Schenke et al.
* [CogEvol-4B open-weight model release with GGUF build and MAIC-UI editing harness](cogevol-4b-open-weight-model-release-with-gguf-build-and-maic-ui-editing-harness.md) - CogEvol-4B is an Apache 2.0-licensed open-weight model and deployment package from the CogEvol Team for generating presentation slides and interactive HTML courseware, including an on-device GGUF build and MAIC-UI editing harness.
* [CogMath-948](cogmath-948.md) - CogMath-948 is a dataset constructed by Wei Zhang et al.
* [Cognitive Flexibility Scale](cognitive-flexibility-scale.md) - A 19-item adjective-pair self-report assessment developed by Bilgin to measure individuals' cognitive flexibility.
* [Cognitive Load Questionnaire (CLQ) by Klepsch et al. (2017)](cognitive-load-questionnaire-clq-by-klepsch-et-al-2017.md) - An eight-item self-report assessment developed by Klepsch and colleagues for measuring learners’ perceived intrinsic, extraneous, and germane cognitive load after a learning task.
* [Cognitive Modifiability Battery (CMB)](cognitive-modifiability-battery-cmb.md) - A dynamic assessment and intervention instrument developed by Tzuriel for evaluating and supporting cognitive modifiability in young children, including children with complex learning difficulties.
* [Cognitive Tutor®](cognitive-tutor.md) - A Carnegie Learning curriculum for secondary mathematics that combines textbooks, adaptive software, teacher guides, assessments, and professional development across Algebra, Geometry, and Integrated Math courses.
* [CoLaLe](colale.md) - A prototype mobile app developed to support German and Thai vocabulary learning through context-linked flashcards and geo-fence-triggered learning sessions.

* [Collaborative Strategic Reading](collaborative-strategic-reading.md) - A structured reading-comprehension program developed by Janette K.
* [Collaborative Way](collaborative-way.md) - A teamwork framework used by the Team Learning Foundation that structures collaboration around generous listening, direct communication, encouragement, commitment-keeping, appreciation, and a shared team mission.
* [Collective Learning platform](collective-learning-platform.md) - An online collective-intelligence platform developed by researchers at the University of Zaragoza and Kampal Data Solutions that moderates networked participant interactions to generate collaborative solutions.
* [College Experiences Survey](college-experiences-survey.md) - A survey instrument used in a larger evaluation of college persistence to collect data about students' college experiences, as reported by David DesRoches et al.
* [College Explorer](college-explorer.md) - College Explorer is a data-visualization tool from NWEA Research that links MAP Growth scores with national benchmarks for colleges, universities, and majors to support post-secondary planning.
* [College match indicator](college-match-indicator.md) - A measurement tool developed by the University of Chicago Consortium on School Research to gauge how students are progressing toward college enrollment by comparing their academic qualifications with college enrollment outcomes.
* [College Promise](college-promise.md) - A place-based postsecondary access and completion framework combining financial assistance, student supports, early messaging, and cross-sector collaboration, used by College Promise programmes and their partners.
* [College Readiness Indicator Systems (CRIS)](college-readiness-indicator-systems-cris.md) - A framework developed by the Annenberg Institute for School Reform and partners for organizing indicators of students' academic preparedness, academic tenacity, and college knowledge.
* [College Results Online](college-results-online.md) - An Education Trust interactive web tool for examining and comparing four-year college graduation rates, including trends and outcomes for diverse student groups.
* [College Track](college-track.md) - College Track is a comprehensive college-preparation programme run by College Track for students from underserved communities, supporting college application, enrollment, and persistence.
* [Colorado Educational Stability Grant Program (HB 18-1306)](colorado-educational-stability-grant-program-hb-18-1306.md) - A Colorado state grant program established through HB 18-1306 that funds districts and education providers to build community partnerships supporting school stability for highly mobile students.
* [Colorado Learning Attitudes about Science Survey](colorado-learning-attitudes-about-science-survey.md) - A standardized, Likert-scale survey developed to measure students’ beliefs and attitudes about learning science by comparing responses with expert-response patterns.
* [Combined inhibition and social cognition classroom training program](combined-inhibition-and-social-cognition-classroom-training-program.md) - A classroom training programme for kindergarten and first-grade children that combines inhibition and executive-function activities with Theory of Mind and social-information-processing activities, delivered through 18 weekly sessions by a trained psychologist with teacher follow-up.
* [Combined TRUST and Panorama Teacher and Staff Survey instrument](combined-trust-and-panorama-teacher-and-staff-survey-instrument.md) - A teacher-and-staff survey instrument assembled by Tommy Wells et al.
* [Community Ecocycle](community-ecocycle.md) - A reflection and action framework published by Equal Measure and Mathematica to help philanthropic funders consider developmental phases and co-design investment approaches with communities pursuing place-based systems change.
* [Community of Inquiry Survey and Metacognition Questionnaire](community-of-inquiry-survey-and-metacognition-questionnaire.md) - The CoI Survey, developed by Arbaugh et al., measures students’ perceptions of teaching, social, and cognitive presence, while the Garrison and Akyol metacognition questionnaire measures self-regulation and co-regulation.
* [Community schools approach](community-schools-approach.md) - A whole-child, whole-school, whole-community framework organized around integrated student supports, expanded learning opportunities, family and community engagement, and collaborative leadership, used by schools and community partners.

* [Competencies of Knowledge Integration questionnaire](competencies-of-knowledge-integration-questionnaire.md) - A 23-item Likert-scale questionnaire developed by Olga Vogel to assess reflexivity, perspective taking, analogical reasoning, and tolerance of ambiguity and uncertainty as competencies supporting knowledge integration in interdisciplinary and transdisciplinary work.

* [Comprehensive Assessment Battery for Children - Working Memory (CABC-WM)](comprehensive-assessment-battery-for-children-working-memory-cabc-wm.md) - The CABC-WM is a computer-based, pirate-themed battery of 13 individually administered working-memory tasks for children, developed by Gray Shelley et al.

* [Computational Thinking for Educators](computational-thinking-for-educators.md) - Google's free online professional-learning programme helps K–12 teachers learn computational-thinking concepts and classroom applications through course materials and a community of practice for sharing ideas, challenges, solutions, and resources.

* [Concerns-Based Adoption Model](concerns-based-adoption-model.md) - CBAM is an implementation framework developed by researchers at the University of Texas at Austin for assessing and supporting individuals’ adoption of educational innovations.

* [Corpus of 100 children's picture books (68,103 words)](corpus-of-100-childrens-picture-books-68103-words.md) - A corpus created by Montag Jessica L.

* [Corrective Reading](corrective-reading.md) - Corrective Reading is a scripted, structured reading intervention curriculum for upper-elementary struggling readers, focused on phonics, fluency, and comprehension.

* [CoRubric](corubric.md) - CoRubric is a free online e-rubric tool developed by the study authors for managing peer, self, and teacher assessment with criteria, scores, notes, comments, and exportable results.
* [CoTrack](cotrack.md) - CoTrack is a web-based multimodal collaboration analytics tool developed by Reet Kasepalu and colleagues that gives teachers mirrored collaboration data and model-based alerts and guidance for improving group-work quality.

* [CourseBlueprint](courseblueprint.md) - CourseBlueprint is a retrieval-augmented course-blueprint and instructional-video generation pipeline developed by Md Zabirul Islam et al.
* [CourseChat](coursechat.md) - CourseChat is an on-premises retrieval-augmented tutoring platform developed by Shapiro et al.

* [Courseware Initiative](courseware-initiative.md) - A Gates Foundation initiative and theory-of-change framework that combines equity-centered digital courseware with instructors' evidence-based teaching practices to improve engagement and achievement, especially for students at risk of failing to earn course credit.
