* [Critical Thinking Disposition Scale and Philosophy Preference Assessment Scale](critical-thinking-disposition-scale-and-philosophy-preference-assessment-scale.md) - A paired set of Likert-type instruments comprising the Ricketts and Rudd Critical Thinking Disposition Scale, adapted into Turkish by Demircioglu, and the Cetin, Ilhan, and Arslan Philosophy Preference Assessment Scale, used to measure critical-thinking dispositions and traditional versus contempora
* [CRITICS](critics.md) - CRITICS is a project-developed suite including an LLM-based scientific document translation system that preserves domain terminology, notation, and document structure for multilingual science education.

* [CulturAIEd](culturaied.md) - CulturAIEd is an LLM-powered Streamlit tool developed by Jiayi Wang et al.

* [Curated Nigerian Pidgin English corpus of 416,343 text entries](curated-nigerian-pidgin-english-corpus-of-416343-text-entries.md) - Nwogo et al.'s publicly released corpus of Nigerian Pidgin text entries scraped, cleaned, and formatted from BBC Pidgin and Prime9ja for fine-tuning and evaluating Pidgin-aware language models.

* [Curiosity Corner](curiosity-corner.md) - Curiosity Corner 2nd Edition is a preschool curriculum package developed and provided by the Success for All Foundation, including interactive whiteboard lessons and other classroom materials.
* [Curriculum-Based Professional Learning (CBPL) Questionnaire](curriculum-based-professional-learning-cbpl-questionnaire.md) - The Curriculum-Based Professional Learning (CBPL) Questionnaire is a survey instrument for districts and schools to characterize professional-learning structures, CBPL features, and implementation of high-quality instructional materials; its maker is not identified in the page description.

#### D {: #letter-d }

* [Dataset of 434 expert-labelled authentic learner questions augmented to balance class distributions](dataset-of-434-expert-labelled-authentic-learner-questions-augmented-to-balance-class-dist.md) - A dataset created by Lee et al.
* [Dataset of 484 de-identified student project reports with instructor reviews collected over twelve semesters](dataset-of-484-de-identified-student-project-reports-with-instructor-reviews-collected-ove.md) - A publicly released research dataset created by Qinjin Jia et al.
* [Dataset of 69 Qassim University PBL course offerings with coded forum interactions](dataset-of-69-qassim-university-pbl-course-offerings-with-coded-forum-interactions.md) - A dataset compiled from 69 Qassim University problem-based learning course offerings, containing coded Moodle forum interactions, student participation, and course-assessment information for studying online collaborative learning.
* [Dataset of approximately 18,000 multiple-choice questions from a high-stakes medical exam](dataset-of-approximately-18000-multiple-choice-questions-from-a-high-stakes-medical-exam.md) - A research dataset assembled by Kang Xue et al.

* [Davison et al. (2011) longitudinal bilingual language and literacy dataset](davison-et-al-2011-longitudinal-bilingual-language-and-literacy-dataset.md) - A longitudinal dataset from 81 bilingual Puerto Rican-heritage Head Start children containing repeated English and Spanish language measures and reading outcomes, collected and analyzed by Davison et al.
* [Day2Day Environment multimodal longitudinal dataset](day2day-environment-multimodal-longitudinal-dataset.md) - A longitudinal dataset created by Falkenstein Kim et al.
* [DBagent](dbagent.md) - DBagent is a course-specific LLM-based educational agent developed by Li et al.

* [DCPS Special Education Service Delivery Model (SDM)](dcps-special-education-service-delivery-model-sdm.md) - A District of Columbia Public Schools model that organizes decentralized special-education identification, assessment, placement, and early intervention through building-level collaborative teams.
* [De Sanctis six-test series for measuring and grading mental deficiency](de-sanctis-six-test-series-for-measuring-and-grading-mental-deficiency.md) - A six-test medico-pedagogical assessment series proposed by Sante De Sanctis for classifying the degree of mental deficiency in institutionalized and special-school populations.
* [Dearborn Intelligence Examination Series I and Series II](dearborn-intelligence-examination-series-i-and-series-ii.md) - A standardized group intelligence examination series developed by Walter F.
* [DebugTracker](debugtracker.md) - DebugTracker is a Visual Studio Code extension created by Jiatong Liu et al.
* [DeepTutor](deeptutor.md) - DeepTutor is an AI-powered personalized tutoring platform developed by Zhao et al.
* [Degree Qualifications Profile (DQP)](degree-qualifications-profile-dqp.md) - The Lumina Foundation's Degree Qualifications Profile is a framework describing expected knowledge and abilities across associate's, bachelor's, and master's degree levels in five learning areas.
* [DELICATE checklist](delicate-checklist.md) - The DELICATE checklist is a framework developed by Drachsler and Greller for guiding privacy-aware and trusted learning-analytics practice in educational organizations.
* [Dementia Knowledge Assessment Scale 2.0 (DKAS 2.0)](dementia-knowledge-assessment-scale-20-dkas-20.md) - A 25-item, expert-derived assessment developed to measure dementia knowledge about its characteristics, trajectory, risk factors, and care among carers, care workers, health professionals, and the general public.
* [Democracy Prep Public Schools](democracy-prep-public-schools.md) - A charter-school network operated by Democracy Prep that serves middle- and high-school students through a civic-education-focused school model intended to prepare them for effective citizenship.
* [Design Research Plan Template](design-research-plan-template.md) - Mathematica's Design Research Plan Template is a planning tool that helps organizations document theories of change, objectives, research activities, timelines, findings, and design adjustments while incorporating community needs.
* [Desired Results Development Profile-Preschool (DRDP-PS)](desired-results-development-profile-preschool-drdp-ps.md) - The DRDP-PS is a California Department of Education teacher-rated developmental assessment used to document and monitor preschool children's learning and progress.
* [Developing Talkers](developing-talkers.md) - Developing Talkers is a supplemental pre-kindergarten and kindergarten curriculum developed by Tricia A.
* [Diagnostic Analysis of Nonverbal Accuracy (DANVA)](diagnostic-analysis-of-nonverbal-accuracy-danva.md) - The DANVA is a standardized assessment developed by researchers to measure facial and vocal emotion-recognition accuracy through emotion-labeled photographs and audio recordings.
* [DIALANG](dialang.md) - DIALANG is a free adaptive web-based diagnostic language assessment system that reports learners' proficiency in relation to the Common European Framework of Reference for Languages and provides feedback and advice for progression.
* [Differentiated Literacy System (DLS)](differentiated-literacy-system-dls.md) - The Differentiated Literacy System is a literacy assessment-and-grouping tool with instructional coaching that supports K–3 teachers in organizing differentiated small-group instruction, developed and delivered by DLS.
* [Digalo](digalo.md) - Digalo is a graphical computer-supported collaborative-learning and argumentation tool developed for organizing learners' shared dialogue and representations across activity phases.
* [Digital Equity Competencies for K-12 School Systems](digital-equity-competencies-for-k-12-school-systems.md) - A framework defining the knowledge, skills, and mindsets that teachers, instructional technology coaches, and administrators need to advance digital equity across five domains.
* [Digital Equity Framework](digital-equity-framework.md) - A framework developed by Judi Fusco and colleagues for guiding equitable access to, use of, and design with digital technologies in education.
* [Digital Equity Frameworks](digital-equity-frameworks.md) - A framework for planning and guiding digital-equity work in schools and districts through the principles of inclusive innovation, sustainability, and continuous improvement.
* [Digital Promise 2016 ed-tech research-use campaign and scoring rubric](digital-promise-2016-ed-tech-research-use-campaign-and-scoring-rubric.md) - A Digital Promise campaign that invited ed-tech companies to report how they use research in product development and evaluated their submissions with a rubric covering research quality, researcher involvement, and public sharing of results.
* [Digital Promise AI Literacy Brief Series](digital-promise-ai-literacy-brief-series.md) - A series of Digital Promise implementation briefs that provides audience-specific AI literacy recommendations, strategies, examples, and equity considerations for PK-12, postsecondary, workforce, and informal learning settings.
* [Digital Promise COVID-19 Student Survey (May-June 2020 topline dataset)](digital-promise-covid-19-student-survey-may-june-2020-topline-dataset.md) - A national survey dataset and topline report produced for Digital Promise by Langer Research Associates with Ipsos Public Affairs, documenting college students' experiences of courses moved fully online during COVID-19.
* [Digital Promise Edtech Pilot Framework](digital-promise-edtech-pilot-framework.md) - Digital Promise's portfolio of an evidence-gathering framework, a collaborative analytics workspace, and a survey-based improvement initiative that supports schools and education organizations conducting rapid-cycle edtech testing.
* [Digital Promise Micro-credential Platform](digital-promise-micro-credential-platform.md) - A competency-based micro-credential platform developed and operated by Digital Promise through which K–12 educators select credentials, submit implementation evidence, and receive expert-reviewed competency recognition.
* [Digital Promise Micro-credentials](digital-promise-micro-credentials.md) - Digital Promise runs a platform and programme through which educators earn competency-based micro-credentials, with issuers including universities, nonprofits, districts, and the NEA.
* [Digital Promise Product Certifications](digital-promise-product-certifications.md) - A Digital Promise certification initiative in which edtech companies submit evidence for expert review and qualifying products receive time-limited Open Badges signaling areas such as research-based design, learner variability, and interoperability.
* [Digital Promise Product Certifications Pilot](digital-promise-product-certifications-pilot.md) - A Digital Promise pilot initiative that certifies education technology products designed and developed using learning-sciences research to support more informed product selection.
* [Digital Promise Research-Based Design Product Certification](digital-promise-research-based-design-product-certification.md) - A Digital Promise certification program that recognizes edtech products whose significant design decisions are informed by rigorous learning-sciences research and whose research basis is accessible to practitioners, learners, and communities.
* [Digital Promise's Competency-Based Assessment Framework](digital-promises-competency-based-assessment-framework.md) - A framework created and maintained by Digital Promise to guide the design and accessibility review of competency-based micro-credentials on its platform.
* [Digital Promise's Inclusive Innovation model](digital-promises-inclusive-innovation-model.md) - An equity-centered research-and-development framework created by Digital Promise that places historically marginalized communities at the center of identifying, designing, developing, and implementing educational and workforce technologies.
* [Digital Promise's Powerful Learning framework](digital-promises-powerful-learning-framework.md) - A framework developed by Digital Promise that describes powerful teaching and learning through agency, purpose, curiosity, and connection.

* [Disciplinary Learning Companion (DLC)](disciplinary-learning-companion-dlc.md) - An online tool developed by Sijmkens et al.
* [Disconumbers, Shapedance, and Digitspan game-based assessment packages](disconumbers-shapedance-and-digitspan-game-based-assessment-packages.md) - A package of serious games developed for assessing non-social cognitive abilities such as working memory, visuospatial ability, and math-based memory in job-selection contexts.
* [District-Charter Collaboration Grants program](district-charter-collaboration-grants-program.md) - A Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation grant program that funded cross-sector collaboration between public and charter schools in seven cities.
* [DLM Early Childhood Express Math software](dlm-early-childhood-express-math-software.md) - DLM Early Childhood Express Math software, developed by Douglas H.
* [Domain-specific GenAI declaration framework translating an activity taxonomy into task-specific disclosure instruments](domain-specific-genai-declaration-framework-translating-an-activity-taxonomy-into-task-spe.md) - A Computer Science higher-education framework developed by Nicholas Micallef for structuring students' disclosure, explanation, and reflection on GenAI use in writing and coding coursework.
* [Doors to Discovery™](doors-to-discovery.md) - Doors to Discovery™ is a preschool literacy curriculum developed and distributed by Wright Group/McGraw-Hill, organized into eight thematic units with shared-literacy activities targeting oral language, phonological awareness, print knowledge, writing, and comprehension.
* [Dot comparison congruency-effect measure of numerical inhibition](dot-comparison-congruency-effect-measure-of-numerical-inhibition.md) - A non-symbolic numerical comparison assessment developed by Gilmore et al.
* [DRAFTTRACE multi-view writing analytics environment](drafttrace-multi-view-writing-analytics-environment.md) - A research-developed web application that records, reconstructs, and analyzes higher-education students’ writing and AI-assistant interactions for instructor-facing process monitoring.
* [DrawEduMath](drawedumath.md) - DrawEduMath is a benchmark dataset created by Li Lucy et al.
* [DreamBox Learning](dreambox-learning.md) - DreamBox Learning is a supplemental adaptive online mathematics program developed by DreamBox Learning for students in grades K–5.
* [DreamBox Math](dreambox-math.md) - DreamBox Learning's adaptive online mathematics program for K–8 learners, designed to supplement classroom instruction through personalized, standards-aligned practice and instruction.
* [Dual Enrollment Equity Pathway (DEEP) framework](dual-enrollment-equity-pathway-deep-framework.md) - A Community College Research Center framework that organizes four equity-oriented practice areas for designing and supporting dual-enrollment programs, especially for underserved high school students.
* [Duolingo](duolingo.md) - Duolingo is a language-learning software platform operated by Duolingo, Inc., offering courses, practice activities, and community discussion boards across many languages.
* [Dutch COOL triennial cohort study dataset](dutch-cool-triennial-cohort-study-dataset.md) - A Dutch national longitudinal cohort dataset following elementary students with repeated teacher reports, student motivational measures, and nationally normed reading and mathematics achievement tests.
* [Dynamic hands-on ramp task for assessing kindergartners' CVS use across four variable-count levels](dynamic-hands-on-ramp-task-for-assessing-kindergartners-cvs-use-across-four-variable-count.md) - A researcher-administered hands-on ramp-task assessment adapted from Chen and Klahr's ramp task to measure kindergartners' control-of-variables strategy through progressively complex experiment designs and feedback.
* [Dynamic Learning Project](dynamic-learning-project.md) - A school-based instructional technology coaching programme run by Digital Promise that places full-time coaches in underserved schools to support teachers through iterative coaching cycles, modeling, co-teaching, and reflection.
* [DysLexLens](dyslexlens.md) - DysLexLens is a low-resource, evidence-traceable LLM framework developed by Dana Rezazadegan et al.

* [Early Risers](early-risers.md) - Early Risers is a multi-year prevention programme for elementary-aged children with early aggressive or disruptive behavior, integrating child-, school-, and family-focused interventions.
* [Early Science with Nico & Nor](early-science-with-nico-nor.md) - A co-designed preschool science curriculum developed by teachers, families, curriculum and media developers, and researchers that uses playful, interest-based home and school experiences, digital supports, and family and teacher guides to connect science with engineering and mathematics.
* [Early Steps](early-steps.md) - Early Steps is a structured one-to-one tutoring programme for first-grade pupils, delivered by tutors according to its named instructional schedule.

* [East Alabama Regional Cybersecurity Alliance (EARCA)](east-alabama-regional-cybersecurity-alliance-earca.md) - A regional alliance run by partner school districts, industry, and postsecondary organizations in Alabama Workforce Region 2 to expand rural cybersecurity education, workforce preparation, and career opportunities.
* [ECED](eced.md) - Every Classroom, Every Day (ECED) is a two-year stand-alone high-school literacy and mathematics curriculum programme using Reading Matters, Writing Matters, and Power 10 strategies.
* [eChemBook digital chemistry textbook for the particle model of matter](echembook-digital-chemistry-textbook-for-the-particle-model-of-matter.md) - A publisher-scientist-developed interactive digital chemistry textbook with learning units, multimedia, activities, summaries, nonlinear navigation, and activity logging for teaching the particle model of matter.
* [ECNUClaw](ecnuclaw.md) - An open-source Python framework developed by Yizhou Zhou et al.
* [ECoach](ecoach.md) - ECoach is a web-based student-success coaching platform developed and operated by the University of Michigan to provide course-specific guidance, feedback, progress tracking, and study-support tools.
* [Ecree automated writing feedback tool](ecree-automated-writing-feedback-tool.md) - Ecree is an automated writing-feedback software tool used by teachers and students to support writing instruction and revision in classroom settings.
* [Ed Trust Teacher Diversity 50-State Scan data tool](ed-trust-teacher-diversity-50-state-scan-data-tool.md) - A publicly available Ed Trust data and rating tool that compares states' progress on educator-diversity policies using evidence-based criteria and three-level ratings.
* [ED-AI Lit framework](ed-ai-lit-framework.md) - Developed by Allen and Kendeou, the ED-AI Lit framework organizes AI literacy into six dimensions: Knowledge, Evaluation, Collaboration, Contextualization, Autonomy, and Ethics.
* [Edcamp](edcamp.md) - Edcamp is a participant-driven professional-learning programme and event model, supported by the Edcamp Foundation, in which educators choose topics, lead peer sessions, and collaboratively share resources.
* [eDia computer-based diagnostic assessment system](edia-computer-based-diagnostic-assessment-system.md) - eDia is an integrated online assessment platform developed and operated for Hungarian education that administers technology-based diagnostic tests, supports IRT-based analysis, and provides interpretable feedback.
* [EDIT measure](edit-measure.md) - The EDIT is a measurement tool developed for the Office of Planning, Research, and Evaluation by Mathematica Policy Research and partners to examine early childhood teachers' ongoing assessment, individualization, and instructional decision-making practices.
* [EDLS](edls.md) - A Digital Promise student survey measuring course experiences, digital-learning practices and challenges, and equity and inclusion perspectives, with demographic data for disaggregation and dashboard reporting.
* [Edmentum Exact Path](edmentum-exact-path.md) - Edmentum Exact Path is a personalized K–12 learning platform that uses diagnostic and adaptive assessments to create individualized Math and English Language Arts learning paths and report student growth.
* [Edpuzzle](edpuzzle.md) - Edpuzzle is a software platform developed and operated by Edpuzzle that lets educators embed questions, commentary, and quizzes in videos and track learners’ viewing and responses.
* [Edpuzzle Originals](edpuzzle-originals.md) - Edpuzzle Originals are ready-made, standards-aligned video lessons developed and provided by Edpuzzle for teacher assignment and classroom adaptation.
* [EdTech Index](edtech-index.md) - Digital Promise's EdTech Index is a searchable directory of educational technology tools that hosts third-party validations concerning safety, effectiveness, usability, inclusivity, and interoperability.
* [Edtech Procurement Framework](edtech-procurement-framework.md) - A six-step framework developed by Foster et al.
* [EdTrust's ten-metric framework for student-centered state financial aid](edtrusts-ten-metric-framework-for-student-centered-state-financial-aid.md) - A framework developed by EdTrust for evaluating whether state financial-aid programs meet ten student-centered criteria related to access, affordability, eligibility, and grant design.
* [Edu-QuRating pipeline and Edu-QuRater scorers](edu-qurating-pipeline-and-edu-qurater-scorers.md) - A software and trained-model package developed by Garrod et al.
* [EduBehaviors-kit](edubehaviors-kit.md) - EduBehaviors-kit is an open-source Python package developed by Julian Bernado et al.
* [EduBehaviors-Studio](edubehaviors-studio.md) - EduBehaviors-Studio is a self-hosted web application developed by Julian Bernado et al.
* [EducaSim](educasim.md) - EducaSim is a web-based LLM-agent simulation environment developed by Cameron Mohne and colleagues for teacher-training role-play practice with simulated small-group students.
* [Education Corps in-person high-dosage literacy tutoring program model](education-corps-in-person-high-dosage-literacy-tutoring-program-model.md) - Education Corps runs an in-person, high-dosage literacy tutoring programme that trains community tutors and supports them with Learning Coaches to provide small-group instruction for below-benchmark K–5 students, especially in grades K–3.
* [Educational Quality and Improvement Profile (EQuIP)](educational-quality-and-improvement-profile-equip.md) - A multi-indicator school reporting and accountability instrument proposed by Adams et al.
* [Educational Survey Test](educational-survey-test.md) - A 30-minute composite elementary-school achievement test assembled from eight standard educational tests by Pintner and colleagues to measure attainment across major curriculum subjects using age and grade norms.
* [EduClaw-Bench](educlaw-bench.md) - EduClaw-Bench is a virtual benchmark environment developed by Lee et al.
* [EDUFRAMETRAP](eduframetrap.md) - EDUFRAMETRAP is a controlled pre-deployment benchmark created by Enkelejda Kasneci et al.
* [EduGage dataset](edugage-dataset.md) - A multimodal learning-engagement dataset released by Zikang Leng and colleagues, containing synchronized sensor streams, engagement-probe ratings, video metadata, and quiz responses from self-guided video-learning sessions for participant-independent engagement-modeling research.
* [EduGuard](eduguard.md) - EduGuard is a safe AI tutoring architecture that combines instructor-approved retrieval, pedagogical strategy selection, rubric-aware generation, claim verification, and overreliance control for programming education.
* [EduQwen 32B-RL1, EduQwen 32B-SFT, and EduQwen 32B-SFT-RL2](eduqwen-32b-rl1-eduqwen-32b-sft-and-eduqwen-32b-sft-rl2.md) - Singh et al.'s EduQwen family comprises open-source 32-billion-parameter pedagogical LLMs designed for customizable, auditable tutoring and educational-platform integration.
* [EduSim-LLM](edusim-llm.md) - EduSim-LLM is a zero-coding educational robotics platform developed by Shenqi Lu et al.
* [Edustories dataset](edustories-dataset.md) - A public dataset of 1,492 anonymized, expert-annotated teacher-written classroom case studies developed from Czech trainee teachers' teaching-practice experiences.
* [EduZone](eduzone.md) - EduZone is an automated adversarial evaluation framework released by Park et al.
* [EDvance at San Francisco State University](edvance-at-san-francisco-state-university.md) - A cohort-based early childhood teacher-preparation programme run by San Francisco State University's Marian Wright Edelman Institute through lower-division and working-educator PATH pathways.
* [EEG familiarity benchmark dataset and evaluation framework (3960 samples, 23 participants, two validation protocols)](eeg-familiarity-benchmark-dataset-and-evaluation-framework-3960-samples-23-participants-tw.md) - A dataset and benchmarking framework released by Isuru Nanayakkara et al.
* [EELE](eele.md) - The Environmental Education Learning Experience (EELE) is a field-trip curriculum package whose developers provide activities and curriculum-standards alignments for teachers.
* [Effective Practice Incentive Community (EPIC) incentive program](effective-practice-incentive-community-epic-incentive-program.md) - A New Leaders for New Schools programme that offers incentives to educators in eligible schools showing significant student achievement gains and supports documenting and disseminating their practices.
* [Effective Practice Incentive Community (EPIC) school award program](effective-practice-incentive-community-epic-school-award-program.md) - A school-level award programme operated through three partner organizations that uses school data to determine eligibility and recognizes eligible schools with EPIC awards.
* [Eight proposed CRESST assessment tools](eight-proposed-cresst-assessment-tools.md) - A suite of eight assessment tools proposed by the National Center for Research on Evaluation, Standards, and Student Testing (CRESST) to measure learning processes and performance through concept mapping, simulation, outlining, problem solving, and related tasks.
* [Eight-scale instrument for classifying teaching style](eight-scale-instrument-for-classifying-teaching-style.md) - A behavioral-recording and rating instrument developed by Garfunkel for describing classroom atmosphere and teaching style across eight bipolar scales.
* [Eight-test ancient-history recall scale with weighted score cards](eight-test-ancient-history-recall-scale-with-weighted-score-cards.md) - An assessment developed by Sackett for measuring high-school and university learners’ recall of ancient-history facts through eight timed tests with weighted scoring.
* [EISEL](eisel.md) - EISEL is an in-house LMS learning tool developed for the study that provides chapter-based question pools, randomized practice sets, immediate correctness feedback, and repeated practice for undergraduate MIS and information-technology learners.
* [Eksploratoryòm](eksploratoryom.md) - Eksploratoryòm is Suzanne Cole et al.'s Haitian Creole digital science curriculum for early-grade learners, using story-based, culturally relevant interactive lessons that integrate STEM, literacy, and social-emotional learning.
* [EL Education](el-education.md) - EL Education's integrated English Language Arts curriculum and professional-learning program, developed and provided by EL Education for schools and teachers.
* [EL Education's Teacher Potential Project (TPP)](el-educations-teacher-potential-project-tpp.md) - A programme run by EL Education that combines a standards-aligned English language arts curriculum with embedded professional development for teachers.
* [El Paso Collaborative for Academic Excellence](el-paso-collaborative-for-academic-excellence.md) - A University of Texas at El Paso and El Paso-area school-district initiative that combined standards, intensive teacher professional development, coaching, and teacher-preparation changes to improve K–college achievement.
* [ELBench](elbench.md) - ELBench is a four-module, 2,939-item benchmark developed by Yilin Jiang et al.
* [Electrically instrumented mirror-drawing apparatus](electrically-instrumented-mirror-drawing-apparatus.md) - A Wells (1915) psychological-laboratory apparatus that uses a mirrored star-tracing task, electrical error counting, and elapsed-time measurement to assess motor learning performance.
* [Elementary On-Track framework](elementary-on-track-framework.md) - Chicago Public Schools' Elementary On-Track framework uses GPA and attendance thresholds to organize support tiers for younger students.
* [Elevate](elevate.md) - Elevate is a software-enabled continuous-improvement platform developed by PERTS and UCCSR that helps educators measure, disaggregate, track, and improve learning conditions over time.
* [ELEVATE (Efficient LLM Education with Virtual Avatar Teaching Engine)](elevate-efficient-llm-education-with-virtual-avatar-teaching-engine.md) - A three-stratum framework developed by Lorenzo Stacchio et al.
* [ELEVATE Engine](elevate-engine.md) - A streaming, queue-based client-server software engine developed by Lorenzo Stacchio et al.
* [Elevate Math summer program](elevate-math-summer-program.md) - Elevate Math is a Silicon Valley Education Foundation summer mathematics support programme that combines a Common Core–aligned curriculum, blended instruction, Khan Academy practice, college-readiness activities, and professional development for its teachers and assistants.
* [Ellevation Education student-level EL records database](ellevation-education-student-level-el-records-database.md) - A longitudinal database constructed with Ellevation Education containing individual English-learner proficiency, assessment, demographic, grade-level, and reclassification records from 2016–17 to 2023–24 across four states.
* [ELLM/Plus curriculum](ellmplus-curriculum.md) - ELLM/Plus is a literacy-focused comprehensive curriculum for young children from low-income families, comprising theme-based instructional packets, children’s books, support materials, and home/school activities.
* [ELS:2002 longitudinal database](els2002-longitudinal-database.md) - A nationally representative U.S.
* [Embodied simulated-patient training application for ACT-oriented psychotherapy practice with spoken dialogue](embodied-simulated-patient-training-application-for-act-oriented-psychotherapy-practice-wi.md) - A Unity application developed by Pascal Riachi et al.
* [Emergent writing assessment battery of conceptual, procedural, and generative subtests for preschoolers](emergent-writing-assessment-battery-of-conceptual-procedural-and-generative-subtests-for-p.md) - Puranik et al.'s individually administered assessment battery measures preschoolers' conceptual, procedural, and generative emergent-writing skills through structured writing and language tasks.
* [Emerging Technology Adoption Framework](emerging-technology-adoption-framework.md) - A living edtech evaluation and procurement framework developed by Digital Promise and Educator CIRCLS, organizing stakeholder questions and criteria across initial evaluation, adoption, and post-adoption stages.
* [eMINTS](emints.md) - A professional-development and coaching programme run by the eMINTS National Center that helps teachers integrate technology and inquiry-based learning into curriculum and instruction.
* [Emotion Socialization Observation Scale (ESOS)](emotion-socialization-observation-scale-esos.md) - A structured classroom-observation assessment developed by Veraksa and colleagues to rate kindergarten teachers' reactions to children's emotions, modeling of emotional expressiveness, and teaching about emotions.
* [Energizers](energizers.md) - Energizers is a classroom-based physical-activity programme developed for elementary students and implemented during the school day to increase activity and support on-task behaviour.
* [Energy Skate Park: Basics (ESPB)](energy-skate-park-basics-espb.md) - A PhET Interactive Simulations physics simulation that lets learners explore conservation of energy and energy relationships by manipulating a skater, track, mass, and friction.
* [ENGAGE](engage.md) - ENGAGE is a Unity-based game learning environment that teaches computational thinking to middle school students through an immersive underwater storyworld, visual programming tasks, and progressive challenge levels.
* [Enhanced Core Reading Instruction](enhanced-core-reading-instruction.md) - Enhanced Core Reading Instruction is a structured literacy intervention programme with professional development materials for teachers, interventionists, and literacy leaders.
* [Enhanced Proactive Reading](enhanced-proactive-reading.md) - A 120-lesson small-group early literacy, language arts, and English language development curriculum for first-grade English language learners experiencing reading difficulties, developed for delivery in daily sessions by teachers.
* [Enhancing the Skills of Early Childhood Trainers training pack](enhancing-the-skills-of-early-childhood-trainers-training-pack.md) - A Bernard van Leer Foundation training-pack curriculum, co-published with UNESCO, that combines early childhood development theory with experiential participatory methods for trainer-of-trainers courses or self-learning.
* [Entonasyon Değerlendirme Formu](entonasyon-degerlendirme-formu.md) - A 44-item, seven-point intonation-assessment form developed by Yakup Aksoy that converts cent-level deviations in violin students’ target notes into categorical accuracy scores.
* [Environmental Education Efficacy Belief Instrument (EEEBI)](environmental-education-efficacy-belief-instrument-eeebi.md) - Developed by Sia (1992), the EEEBI is a 23-item Likert-type assessment of preservice teachers’ personal environmental-education teaching efficacy and outcome expectancy beliefs.
* [EOS Opportunity Chart](eos-opportunity-chart.md) - A data-reporting tool developed by Equal Opportunity Schools that helps schools and districts compare disaggregated advanced-course seats, enrollments, and success with their student demographics.
* [EOS Student Insight Card](eos-student-insight-card.md) - Equal Opportunity Schools' assessment tool compiles students' interests, aspirations, motivation, and trusted-staff recommendations to inform access to advanced coursework.
* [EPIC Charter School Consortium school-and-teacher-effectiveness measurement system](epic-charter-school-consortium-school-and-teacher-effectiveness-measurement-system.md) - A Mathematica Policy Research measurement system used by the EPIC charter school consortium to identify effective schools and teachers across its member charter schools.
* [EPIGAME (The Fuzzy Chronicles)](epigame-the-fuzzy-chronicles.md) - A digital puzzle game developed by Martinez-Garza et al.
* [EPIQ-AI Readiness Framework](epiq-ai-readiness-framework.md) - A framework developed by Sangwa et al.
* [Epistemic violence-freedom scale](epistemic-violence-freedom-scale.md) - A 29-item Likert-scale instrument developed by Kartal et al.
* [Epistemological Beliefs Assessment for Physical Sciences (EBAPS)](epistemological-beliefs-assessment-for-physical-sciences-ebaps.md) - EBAPS is a 30-item assessment instrument developed to measure physical-science students' epistemological beliefs across five non-orthogonal dimensions using prescribed weighted axis scores.
* [ePSRL Management System](epsrl-management-system.md) - A purpose-built electronic personalised self-regulated learning management system developed by Khiat and colleagues to support learners' planning, monitoring, appraisal, reflection, and learning analytics throughout the learning process.
* [Equatio and Mathspace](equatio-and-mathspace.md) - Equatio is Texthelp's digital mathematics equation editor and accessibility tool, while Mathspace is a digital workspace for working with equations, shapes, and freehand drawings; teachers and students use them in mathematics instruction.
* [Equitized Syllabus](equitized-syllabus.md) - The Equitized Syllabus is a shared syllabus template developed and standardized by BMCC for adoption across course sections and partner colleges.
* [Equity-centered, vendor-accountable edtech evaluation system model](equity-centered-vendor-accountable-edtech-evaluation-system-model.md) - A proposed community-college edtech evaluation and procurement framework from Van Nostrand et al.
* [ERWC](erwc.md) - The Expository Reading and Writing Course is a curriculum and associated teacher professional-learning and coaching programme developed and implemented to support secondary students' expository reading and writing.
* [ERWC 3.0 curriculum](erwc-30-curriculum.md) - A California State University-developed college-preparatory English language arts curriculum comprising 67 selectable modules for grade 11–12 courses, with integrated reading, writing, discussion, English Language Development, and Universal Design for Learning features.
* [Essential 0-5 Survey](essential-0-5-survey.md) - The Essential 0-5 Survey, created in partnership with Start Early, gathers teacher and parent perceptions to assess six organizational conditions in early-childhood education programs and provide leaders with actionable data.
* [Essentials for Community School Transformation Framework](essentials-for-community-school-transformation-framework.md) - A Community Schools Forward framework that extends the traditional four community-school pillars with belonging, safety, and care, and rigorous community-connected classroom instruction to guide whole-child school transformation.
* [Estonian reflective-writing AI-feedback corpus of 2988 homework triplets from 283 students](estonian-reflective-writing-ai-feedback-corpus-of-2988-homework-triplets-from-283-students.md) - A restricted corpus created by Andres Karjus et al.
* [eSylvan](esylvan.md) - eSylvan was Sylvan Learning Centers' early Internet-based tutoring environment for delivering its reading, writing, and mathematics curriculum through teacher-led online sessions.
* [Ethical AI and Workforce Readiness (EAWR) micro-credential framework](ethical-ai-and-workforce-readiness-eawr-micro-credential-framework.md) - A proposed micro-credential framework by İsmail Şan et al.
* [Ethics Training Agents](ethics-training-agents.md) - A web-based multi-human, multi-agent LLM discussion system developed by Seo et al.
* [Evaluating the Relationships Between Poverty and School Performance interactive data visualization gallery](evaluating-the-relationships-between-poverty-and-school-performance-interactive-data-visua.md) - An interactive data-visualization gallery released and operated by NWEA Research to let users explore relationships between school poverty, achievement, and growth in the report's sample.
* [Every Learner Everywhere Lighthouse Institution Program and Technical Assistance Model](every-learner-everywhere-lighthouse-institution-program-and-technical-assistance-model.md) - A Gates Foundation-funded Every Learner Everywhere initiative in which a network of organizations helps participating colleges implement adaptive courseware in gateway courses through technical assistance, communities of practice, subgrants, and research support.
* [EveryDay Intervention](everyday-intervention.md) - A school- and district-partnered attendance intervention that analyzes attendance data and mails personalized absence reports to parents or guardians of students at risk of chronic absenteeism.
* [Everyday Mathematics®](everyday-mathematics.md) - Everyday Mathematics® is a comprehensive pre-K–6 mathematics curriculum developed by the University of Chicago School Mathematics Project, combining teacher guides, student materials, manipulatives, games, technology, and repeated exposure to mathematical content.
* [Evidence to Insights (e2i) Coach](evidence-to-insights-e2i-coach.md) - A free online platform offered by the Regional Educational Laboratory Mid-Atlantic that helps schools and teachers use their own data to identify effective remote-learning strategies and refine practice.
* [Expeditionary Learning](expeditionary-learning.md) - A whole-school educational model developed and supported by EL Education that organizes schools around experiential, project-based learning and related schoolwide practices.
* [Experience Corps](experience-corps.md) - Experience Corps is a community-based programme that places adults aged 55 and older in elementary schools to tutor and mentor at-risk students, assist teachers, and lead enrichment activities.
* [Expert-annotated clinical consultation corpus of 207 sessions and 4,815 messages with four annotation dimensions](expert-annotated-clinical-consultation-corpus-of-207-sessions-and-4815-messages-with-four.md) - Yang et al.'s dataset contains 207 medical-student clinical history-taking sessions and 4,815 human–AI dialogue turns annotated by clinical experts for message fidelity, dialogue intent, scaffolding need, and OSCE performance.
* [Expert-annotated Hungarian reflective essay dataset](expert-annotated-hungarian-reflective-essay-dataset.md) - A corpus of approximately 1,954 Hungarian reflective essays from Early Childhood Education students, expert-annotated on a four-level reflection scale by Zsolt Csibi and colleagues.
* [Expert-vetted assessment suite of GUI-oriented vibe-coding tasks with scoring criteria](expert-vetted-assessment-suite-of-gui-oriented-vibe-coding-tasks-with-scoring-criteria.md) - An expert-designed assessment suite of natural-language GUI-oriented vibe-coding tasks with predefined scoring criteria and rubrics, developed by the article's international subject-matter-expert panel for tertiary students.
* [Expository Reading and Writing Course (ERWC)](expository-reading-and-writing-course-erwc.md) - The Expository Reading and Writing Course is a college-preparatory English language arts curriculum developed by the California State University system for secondary students, with modules that integrate rhetorical reading, writing, revision, and language study.
* [Extended 36-trial Red-Blue Test](extended-36-trial-red-blue-test.md) - A computer-based Stroop/Day-Night-adapted assessment for early primary school children that measures inhibitory control through 36 rule-reversal color-naming trials scored by accuracy.
* [Extended labelled dataset of 345 OER sentences with ethnic-bias annotations from 193 students](extended-labelled-dataset-of-345-oer-sentences-with-ethnic-bias-annotations-from-193-stude.md) - A dataset released by Josmario Albuquerque and colleagues containing 345 contextualized OER sentences annotated by 193 higher-education students for perceived ethnic bias, with contextual metadata, aggregate labels, bias scores, and reproducibility code.

#### F {: #letter-f }

* [Faces task](faces-task.md) - A computerized behavioral task developed by Bialystok, Craik, and Ryan that measures response suppression, inhibitory control, and task switching through speeded responses to gaze and eye-color stimuli.
* [Facing History and Ourselves](facing-history-and-ourselves.md) - A character-education curriculum developed and delivered by Facing History & Ourselves that uses historical events, especially the Holocaust, to help middle and high school students examine identity, moral reasoning, and civic responsibility.
* [FACTRIA (Framework of Factors in the Analytical process and the data Context for Responsible Institutional Analytics)](factria-framework-of-factors-in-the-analytical-process-and-the-data-context-for-responsibl.md) - A flexible, evidence-informed framework proposed by Marques et al.
* [FACTRIA-aware chatbot](factria-aware-chatbot.md) - A web-based chatbot developed by Marques et al.
* [Faculty diversity pipeline programs (State Doctoral Scholars Program, STRIDE, California Pre-Doctoral Program)](faculty-diversity-pipeline-programs-state-doctoral-scholars-program-stride-california-pre.md) - A set of initiatives providing doctoral mentoring, networking, financial or preparatory support, and hiring-committee workshops to diversify the professoriate, run by the Southern Regional Education Board, Florida International University, and the University of California system respectively.
* [Failure Free Reading](failure-free-reading.md) - Failure Free Reading is a scripted language-development and reading-intervention program for struggling Kindergarten–grade 12 readers, combining teacher instruction, talking software, workbook exercises, and independent reading activities.
* [Fall 2020 MAP Growth remote-versus-in-person comparability dataset](fall-2020-map-growth-remote-versus-in-person-comparability-dataset.md) - A dataset of fall 2020 NWEA MAP Growth assessment scores from more than 535,000 K–8 students in 147 districts, assembled and analyzed by Megan Kuhfeld and colleagues to compare remotely and in-person administered tests.
* [Family Child Care Apprenticeship (FCC Apprenticeship)](family-child-care-apprenticeship-fcc-apprenticeship.md) - A job-embedded apprenticeship pathway run by the SEIU Educator Training Center with community-college and local partner sites to provide family child care providers with coursework, coaching, advising, and financial support toward California Child Development Permits.
* [Family Science Fun Guide](family-science-fun-guide.md) - A digital family science curriculum guide developed for the Early Science with Nico and Nor program, offering playful home and outdoor activities and conversation support for caregivers.
* [FAST](fast.md) - Families and Schools Together (FAST), developed and implemented by Families and Schools Together, Inc., is a multifamily group programme that strengthens parenting, family relationships, and connections among families, schools, and communities.
* [Fast ForWord®](fast-forword.md) - Fast ForWord Language is a computer-based language and reading intervention program developed and marketed by Scientific Learning Corporation for learners needing support with English language proficiency and reading skills.
* [Fast Track](fast-track.md) - Fast Track is a comprehensive, longitudinal intervention developed by the Conduct Problems Prevention Research Group to support children at risk for conduct problems through coordinated academic, behavioral, social, peer, and family services.
* [FASTalk](fastalk.md) - FASTalk is a text-message family-engagement programme that sends multilingual, at-home literacy activities to families to support learning partnerships between schools and historically underserved households.
* [FastPerson](fastperson.md) - FastPerson is a multimodal video-to-video summarization system developed by Kazuki Kawamura that generates condensed lecture videos while preserving the original speaker's voice and allowing chapter-level switching between summary and full versions.
* [FEEDTYPE: a benchmark of teacher and LLM feedback annotated for feedback focus across three writing domains](feedtype-a-benchmark-of-teacher-and-llm-feedback-annotated-for-feedback-focus-across-three.md) - A released corpus of paragraph-level teacher- and LLM-generated writing feedback with feedback-focus annotations, scores, and student revisions across composition, ESL, and fiction courses, created by Norah Almousa et al.
* [FEIP](feip.md) - A Heising-Simons Foundation-funded public-private partnership that builds parents' and professionals' capacity for family engagement and promotes educational outcomes for low-income immigrant children from birth through age 8 in California's San Mateo and Santa Clara counties.
* [FH2T](fh2t.md) - From Here to There (FH2T) is a digital game developed as a supplemental learning intervention for middle- and high-school students to practice algebraic expressions and equation solving through direct manipulation of mathematical notation.
* [Fifteen-country access and depth profile dataset (Table 2)](fifteen-country-access-and-depth-profile-dataset-table-2.md) - A dataset compiled by Adrian-Marius Dumitran that codes programming-language policy and AI-literacy access across fifteen national upper-secondary education systems for comparative curriculum and governance analysis.
* [finaldataset.dat](finaldatasetdat.md) - A publicly downloadable OSF-hosted study dataset containing item-level self-concept and comparison-related belief measures, intervention indicators, and class identifiers, released with Mplus analysis syntaxes by the study authors.
* [Find the Fit](find-the-fit.md) - Find the Fit is an enhanced college-advising programme used within Upward Bound that combines personalized planning materials and student text messaging with advisor training webinars.
* [Fine-tuned Gemma E2B offline deployment with dashboard and conversational interfaces](fine-tuned-gemma-e2b-offline-deployment-with-dashboard-and-conversational-interfaces.md) - Chenguang Pan et al.'s offline educational-intervention-effect explanation system, combining a LoRA-adapted Gemma E2B model with dashboard and conversational interfaces for parents, students, counselors, and other stakeholders.
* [First Step to Success](first-step-to-success.md) - First Step to Success is a behavior-coaching programme developed for children at risk of aggressive or antisocial behavior, combining screening, classroom intervention, and HomeBase parent training.
* [First-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses from a math learning platform](first-of-its-kind-real-world-dataset-of-student-dialogues-and-question-responses-from-a-ma.md) - A Duan et al.
* [Five considerations framework for engaging educational partners in school climate improvement](five-considerations-framework-for-engaging-educational-partners-in-school-climate-improvem.md) - An Ojetunde et al.
* [Five EdTech Quality Indicators](five-edtech-quality-indicators.md) - A framework developed by 1EdTech, CAST, CoSN, Digital Promise, InnovateEDU, ISTE+ASCD, and SETDA for evaluating AI-enabled edtech against safety, evidence, inclusion, usability, and interoperability criteria.
* [Five fundamental elements of a high-quality education system](five-fundamental-elements-of-a-high-quality-education-system.md) - A policy framework from Oakes et al.
* [Five-Component Framework for Systemic School Turnaround](five-component-framework-for-systemic-school-turnaround.md) - TenSquare's framework guides boards, school leaders, and turnaround partners in transforming leadership, talent, teaching and learning, culture, and operations and finance in low-performing charter schools.
* [Five-construct dichotomous questionnaire measuring teacher AI readiness in green energy education](five-construct-dichotomous-questionnaire-measuring-teacher-ai-readiness-in-green-energy-ed.md) - A five-construct, 25-item binary questionnaire developed by Riandi Riandi et al.
* [Five-goal state policy framework for increasing educator workforce diversity](five-goal-state-policy-framework-for-increasing-educator-workforce-diversity.md) - An Education Trust framework that assesses and guides state policy conditions for recruiting, hiring, supporting, and retaining a racially diverse educator workforce.
* [Five-item pre-instruction AI perception intake survey](five-item-pre-instruction-ai-perception-intake-survey.md) - A study-developed anonymous Qualtrics survey that measures bioscience trainees' baseline AI perceptions and related intake characteristics before an RCR course.
* [Five-task emergent writing assessment protocol with feature-based scoring system](five-task-emergent-writing-assessment-protocol-with-feature-based-scoring-system.md) - A researcher-developed preschool emergent-writing assessment protocol comprising five writing tasks and task-tailored feature-based scoring, created by Puranik et al.
* [Five-year longitudinal cohort dataset of 4,228 students assessed up to three times per year comparing ever-SPED and never-SPED growth](five-year-longitudinal-cohort-dataset-of-4228-students-assessed-up-to-three-times-per-year.md) - A research dataset assembled and analyzed by Angela Johnson (2021) that follows 4,228 students from kindergarten through fourth grade with repeated math and reading assessments to compare growth by special-education status and season.
* [Five-year Twitter corpus (14,201 tweets) with SetFit sentiment classifier and BERTopic modelling pipeline](five-year-twitter-corpus-14201-tweets-with-setfit-sentiment-classifier-and-bertopic-modell.md) - A researcher-created dataset and analysis pipeline comprising 14,201 English-language U.S.
* [Flashlight360](flashlight360.md) - Flashlight360 is a browser-based platform for educators and administrators to monitor and assess multilingual students' speaking and writing progress, providing scoring and feedback across defined language-skill areas.
* [FLIGHT](flight.md) - FLIGHT is a Florida school-based mentoring and college-access programme supported by staff workshops, dedicated mentoring and advocacy activities, scholarships, and public-private funding.
* [Flight SOS](flight-sos.md) - Flight SOS is an interactive video English-as-a-foreign-language program co-produced by Gale et al.
* [Flowcode](flowcode.md) - An AI-powered web-based creative-coding environment developed by Tseng et al.
* [Fluency Formula™](fluency-formula.md) - A Scholastic supplemental curriculum for grades 1–6 that provides brief daily lessons and materials designed to develop word-recognition automaticity, decoding accuracy, and oral reading expressiveness.
* [FLUX smartphone ambulatory assessment application](flux-smartphone-ambulatory-assessment-application.md) - A dedicated smartphone application developed by the Technology Based Assessment Group at DIPF for the FLUX project to administer working-memory, sleep, and tiredness assessments to elementary-school children in daily-life settings.
* [Focus on High Schools](focus-on-high-schools.md) - A Boston Public Schools reform programme that reorganized comprehensive high schools into smaller learning communities and reformed English and language arts programmes.
* [For Our Babies campaign](for-our-babies-campaign.md) - A U.S.
* [FORAP (Framework for Organizing Reusable and Adaptable PjBL Projects)](forap-framework-for-organizing-reusable-and-adaptable-pjbl-projects.md) - A framework developed by Suleiman et al.
* [Forca Libras](forca-libras.md) - Forca Libras is an AI-powered computer-vision hangman game developed within the GAIA project to support Brazilian Sign Language learning for hearing students and Portuguese literacy for deaf students.
* [Force and Motion Microworld (FMM)](force-and-motion-microworld-fmm.md) - A suite of three computer simulation programs developed by Tao et al.
* [Force Concept Inventory (FCI)](force-concept-inventory-fci.md) - A standardized multiple-choice assessment developed by David Hestenes, Malcolm Wells, and Gregg Swackhamer to measure introductory students’ conceptual understanding of Newtonian mechanics.
* [FoundationalASSIST dataset](foundationalassist-dataset.md) - FoundationalASSIST is a dataset of ASSISTments mathematics interactions containing complete question text, actual student responses, distractor selections, and Common Core knowledge-component alignments for developing and evaluating educational AI and knowledge-tracing models.
* [Foundations for Young Adult Success](foundations-for-young-adult-success.md) - A UChicago Consortium framework that organizes developmental conditions and experiences supporting young people's successful transition to adulthood.
* [Four cornerstones of a fair and effective school funding system: adequacy, equity, transparency, and responsibility](four-cornerstones-of-a-fair-and-effective-school-funding-system-adequacy-equity-transparen.md) - An EdBuild-developed framework, summarized by The Education Trust–Midwest, for evaluating and reforming state school-funding systems through adequacy, equity, transparency, and responsibility.
* [Four Domains Comprehensive Assessment of Leadership for Learning (CALL) survey and feedback system](four-domains-comprehensive-assessment-of-leadership-for-learning-call-survey-and-feedback.md) - A leadership-team survey, diagnostic, and feedback-reporting system developed for schools and districts using the Four Domains framework to identify leadership-practice needs and guide improvement planning.
* [Four Domains for Rapid School Improvement](four-domains-for-rapid-school-improvement.md) - A Center on School Turnaround framework that organizes practices for state, district, and school leaders pursuing rapid school improvement.
* [Four equity pillars framework for federal assessment policy](four-equity-pillars-framework-for-federal-assessment-policy.md) - An Ed Trust framework that organizes priorities for equitable statewide summative assessment policy around high expectations, inclusive assessments, timely actionable results, and meaningful interpretation of results.
* [Four Pillars of Opportunity framework](four-pillars-of-opportunity-framework.md) - Cook-Harvey et al.'s framework organizes equitable ESSA implementation around access to higher-order learning, multiple equity measures, resource equity, and evidence-based interventions.
* [Four-Element Family Engagement Framework](four-element-family-engagement-framework.md) - A named family-engagement improvement framework or playbook that organizes work around capacity, process, infrastructure, and outcomes for adoption by schools and education organizations.
* [Four-element framework for restorative practices implementation](four-element-framework-for-restorative-practices-implementation.md) - This is a guide developed by its authors or issuing organization to help schools and other organizations implement restorative practices.
* [Four-metric faculty equity grading framework](four-metric-faculty-equity-grading-framework.md) - A four-metric framework developed by Bitar et al.
* [Four-pillar competency rubric](four-pillar-competency-rubric.md) - A rubric developed by Smith et al.
* [Four-year Copilot comment-specification dataset from an undergraduate OOP course](four-year-copilot-comment-specification-dataset-from-an-undergraduate-oop-course.md) - A reconstructed longitudinal dataset of anonymized student comments, Copilot-generated Java code, and test results from four offerings of an undergraduate object-oriented programming activity.
* [FOXGLOVE](foxglove.md) - FOXGLOVE is a parallel dataset of sentence-anchored, goal-labeled instructor and LLM writing feedback with urgency rankings and expert quality ratings, created from twelfth-grade argumentative essays by Yijun Liu and colleagues.
* [Fraction Face-Off!](fraction-face-off.md) - Fraction Face-Off!
* [Framework for Leadership](framework-for-leadership.md) - Pennsylvania's tool for evaluating the effectiveness of school principals and assistant principals, developed and administered by the Pennsylvania Department of Education.
* [Framework for Leading Next Generation Science Standards Implementation](framework-for-leading-next-generation-science-standards-implementation.md) - A leadership framework developed by Stiles et al.
* [Framework for Powerful Learning with Emerging Technology](framework-for-powerful-learning-with-emerging-technology.md) - A three-principle framework developed by its authors to guide educators, caregivers, and learners in using emerging technologies through learner-centered, evidence-based, and skill-building practices.
* [Framework for Teaching](framework-for-teaching.md) - The Charlotte Danielson Framework for Teaching is a framework developed by Charlotte Danielson for evaluating and supporting classroom teaching through observable components across four domains and four performance levels.
* [Frequentist-based Representative Diversity (FRD) index](frequentist-based-representative-diversity-frd-index.md) - A proposed index by Chan et al.
* [Freshman OnTrack](freshman-ontrack.md) - A school early-warning indicator and dashboard system that combines student attendance, grades, discipline, and momentum data to monitor freshman on-track status and guide support.
* [Freshman OnTrack indicator](freshman-ontrack-indicator.md) - A research-based early-warning measurement developed and used by the University of Chicago Consortium and Chicago Public Schools to identify ninth-grade students at risk of not graduating.
* [Freshman Success](freshman-success.md) - Freshman Success is a school-based ninth-grade monitoring and support programme that uses real-time reports to help staff keep students on track during the freshman year.
* [FTW toolkit for running Future Technology Workshops](ftw-toolkit-for-running-future-technology-workshops.md) - A workshop implementation package that provides the structure, materials, and session instructions for running Future Technology Workshops, enabling facilitators to conduct participatory design with children or adults without specialist software-design training.
* [Fueling Brains](fueling-brains.md) - A Fueling Brains early-childhood program that integrates executive-function development, guided and open-ended play, hands-on learning, and ongoing assessment into pre-kindergarten instruction.
* [Functional Aspects of Working Memory Test (FAWMT)](functional-aspects-of-working-memory-test-fawmt.md) - A computerized 45-minute assessment adapted by Bedyńska et al.
* [Funder reflection and action tool](funder-reflection-and-action-tool.md) - A structured organizational self-assessment tool developed by Meg Long and colleagues to help funders evaluate whether their strategic intent, culture, and capacity support place-based systems-change investing.
* [Future Forward](future-forward.md) - Future Forward is a branded early-literacy tutoring and family-engagement programme implemented by participating schools and sites for struggling students in kindergarten through grade 3.
* [Future Ready Talent Framework](future-ready-talent-framework.md) - A conceptual framework synthesized by Pretti et al.

#### G {: #letter-g }

* [GAI-A (Generative Artificial Intelligence in Architecture) interface with Collage Tool](gai-a-generative-artificial-intelligence-in-architecture-interface-with-collage-tool.md) - GAI-A is a React.js and Flask-based generative-AI architecture design interface developed by Leman Figen Gül and colleagues that converts user-created image collages into cohesive architectural renderings.
* [GAIDE: A Guiding Framework for AI-Integrated Design for Educators](gaide-a-guiding-framework-for-ai-integrated-design-for-educators.md) - Song et al.'s GAIDE framework guides K–12 teachers through AI understanding, iterative collaborative design and prototyping, and reflection on ethical application when creating AI-powered learning technologies.
* [Galaxy Math](galaxy-math.md) - Galaxy Math is an AIR-disseminated, space-themed one-to-one tutoring programme in which paraprofessionals provide at-risk first graders with frequent sessions focused on addition and subtraction.
* [Game Performance Assessment Instrument (GPAI)](game-performance-assessment-instrument-gpai.md) - The GPAI is a physical-education observational assessment instrument used to code students' in-game skill execution and tactical performance during live games or recordings.
* [Game-based assessment of persistence (GAP)](game-based-assessment-of-persistence-gap.md) - Ventura et al.'s game-based assessment measures learner persistence from log-derived time spent on unsolved problems during gameplay.
* [Gator Mentoring](gator-mentoring.md) - Gator Mentoring is a school-wide, full-year initiative that pairs at-risk middle-school students with adult staff mentors for bi-weekly support and guidance.
* [GBSRI](gbsri.md) - GBSRI is a genre-based, self-regulated writing-instruction framework developed by the article’s authors for EFL writing instruction.
* [GED, HiSET, and TASC](ged-hiset-and-tasc.md) - GED Testing Service, ETS, and Data Recognition Corporation provide these standardized high school equivalency assessments, which states use to award secondary-school equivalency credentials to individuals who did not graduate from high school.
* [GEMS® Space Science Sequence](gems-space-science-sequence.md) - A grades 3–5 space-science curriculum developed by the Lawrence Hall of Science at the University of California, Berkeley, comprising four units and 24 class sessions with teacher guides and materials.
* [GenAI Education Reddit Corpus and 17-Theme Taxonomy](genai-education-reddit-corpus-and-17-theme-taxonomy.md) - A GitHub-released corpus and taxonomy by Pelin Yüce et al.
* [GenAI Reliance Types Scale (GenAI-RTS)](genai-reliance-types-scale-genai-rts.md) - A 20-item self-report scale developed by Hossain et al.
* [GenAI Usage Behavior Scale](genai-usage-behavior-scale.md) - A 15-item, five-point Likert self-report scale developed by Song Yafeng et al.
* [GenAI-Learning-Outcomes GitHub repository](genai-learning-outcomes-github-repository.md) - A repository released by Jingwei Yi et al.
* [GenAI-SRL scale and GenAI-SRL-12 short form](genai-srl-scale-and-genai-srl-12-short-form.md) - A self-report assessment developed by Xiaoqi Wang et al.
* [GenAICanHarmLearning anonymized dataset and code repository](genaicanharmlearning-anonymized-dataset-and-code-repository.md) - A GitHub release by Bastani Hamsa et al.
* [GenARch](genarch.md) - GenARch is a GenAI-assisted multi-user extended-reality pipeline developed by Xiao et al.
* [Generalized Intelligent Framework for Tutoring (GIFT)](generalized-intelligent-framework-for-tutoring-gift.md) - GIFT is a software framework for authoring, integrating, and adapting computer-based tutoring and training environments, developed and maintained by the GIFT community.
* [Generative AI Assessment Literacy Scale (GAA-LS)](generative-ai-assessment-literacy-scale-gaa-ls.md) - Nie et al.'s 18-item five-dimension self-report scale measures higher education students' perceived capacity to use generative AI responsibly in assessment-related tasks.
* [Generative AI Literacy Assessment Test (GLAT)](generative-ai-literacy-assessment-test-glat.md) - A 20-item multiple-choice performance assessment developed by Jin et al.
* [Generative AI Student Feedback Literacy Scale (GenAI-SFLS)](generative-ai-student-feedback-literacy-scale-genai-sfls.md) - A 39-item Chinese-language self-report scale developed by Cui et al.
* [GENESIS framework](genesis-framework.md) - A five-pillar framework proposed by Ahangama (2026) for guiding GenAI-era assessment reform in Australian ICT tertiary institutions at AQF levels 7–9.
* [Geniventure](geniventure.md) - Geniventure is a game-based learning environment developed for middle- and high-school introductory genetics instruction, using virtual drakes, scaffolded challenges, hints, and interaction tracing.
* [GeoGebra](geogebra.md) - GeoGebra is a free dynamic mathematics software platform developed and maintained by GeoGebra for interactive work across algebra, geometry, calculus, and spreadsheets.
* [Geometry Reasoning and Proof Test (GRPT) with perception and attitude questionnaire](geometry-reasoning-and-proof-test-grpt-with-perception-and-attitude-questionnaire.md) - A study-developed assessment package combining a geometry reasoning and proof test with a five-point questionnaire measuring students’ perceptions and attitudes toward AI-supported geometry instruction.
* [Georgia GKIDS Readiness Check](georgia-gkids-readiness-check.md) - A brief kindergarten readiness assessment in Georgia's yearlong GKIDS system, combining direct performance tasks and teacher observations across social-emotional learning, English language arts, and mathematics.
* [GeoVAD-Bench](geovad-bench.md) - A diagnostic benchmark created by Zhitong Dong et al.
* [GeoWeave](geoweave.md) - A post-training framework developed by Zhitong Dong et al.
* [Gephi](gephi.md) - Gephi is an open-source network analysis and visualization platform developed and maintained by the Gephi community and contributors.
* [German Test Anxiety Inventory (PAF)](german-test-anxiety-inventory-paf.md) - A 20-item self-report questionnaire measuring worry, interference, lack of confidence, and arousal facets of test anxiety in students.
* [Getting Ready](getting-ready.md) - Getting Ready is a University of Nebraska–Lincoln program that trains early childhood educators in evidence-based family-partnership strategies and coaches them in collaborative goal-setting through home visits, conferences, and informal interactions.
* [GGMnonreg](ggmnonreg.md) - GGMnonreg is an R package that implements non-regularized Gaussian graphical-modeling methods for low-dimensional data, developed by Donald Williams.
* [GO Tutor Corps](go-tutor-corps.md) - GO Tutor Corps is a school-based high-impact mathematics tutoring programme that provides individualized or small-group tutoring, structured sessions, and progress monitoring for students needing additional academic support.
* [Go-Lab ecosystem](go-lab-ecosystem.md) - The Go-Lab ecosystem, including the Graasp authoring and implementation platform and Golabz repository, is a technology environment developed and maintained to help teachers create and use inquiry-based STEM learning spaces.
* [Go-Lab online teacher community and Inquiry Learning Spaces](go-lab-online-teacher-community-and-inquiry-learning-spaces.md) - Go-Lab is an online ecosystem and teacher community that supports the creation, sharing, reuse, and refinement of inquiry-based learning spaces and resources, developed and maintained by the Go-Lab Initiative.
* [Go-Learn-Grow](go-learn-grow.md) - The Go-Learn-Grow toolkit is a set of practical materials produced for the Regional Educational Laboratory Mid-Atlantic to help districts, schools, and early childhood providers improve pre-kindergarten and kindergarten attendance.
* [Go/No-Go and Nebraska Barnyard inhibition and working-memory tasks](gono-go-and-nebraska-barnyard-inhibition-and-working-memory-tasks.md) - A computerized assessment battery combining a fish/shark Go/No-Go inhibition task and the Nebraska Barnyard complex-span working-memory task for preschool children.
* [GoCPS](gocps.md) - GoCPS is Chicago Public Schools' online universal-enrollment platform, operated by CPS to centralize high-school applications, program rankings, deferred-acceptance matching, offers, and waitlists.
* [Gold and ultra-gold benchmark subsets](gold-and-ultra-gold-benchmark-subsets.md) - Croteau et al.
* [Good Behavior Game](good-behavior-game.md) - A classroom behavior-management programme originally developed at Johns Hopkins University in which students work in teams and receive recognition for meeting classroom behavior expectations, with implementations including PAX and AIR versions.
* [Google Expeditions](google-expeditions.md) - A Google programme that supplied schools with virtual-reality field-trip kits, content, equipment, and teacher training for conducting virtual expeditions.
* [Gorski's equity literacy approach](gorskis-equity-literacy-approach.md) - A framework developed by Paul Gorski for helping educators recognize, analyze, and respond to inequity through equity-oriented knowledge, skills, dispositions, and action.
* [GPT Tutor](gpt-tutor.md) - GPT Tutor is a GPT-4 chat-based mathematics tutoring interface developed and studied by Bastani Hamsa et al.
* [GPT-2 Output Detector Demo](gpt-2-output-detector-demo.md) - A language-analysis tool developed by OpenAI that estimates whether submitted text resembles AI-generated writing, particularly GPT-2-style output.
* [GRAD Partnership Student Success Systems](grad-partnership-student-success-systems.md) - A framework developed by the GRAD Partnership for organizing secondary-school supports around relationships, holistic data, strategic improvement actions, and student-centered mindsets.
* [Graded 80-word opposites-test list with difficulty point values from .5 to 2.0 totaling 100 points](graded-80-word-opposites-test-list-with-difficulty-point-values-from-5-to-20-totaling-100.md) - A historical verbal-association and word-knowledge assessment developed by Harry A.
* [GradeOpt](gradeopt.md) - GradeOpt is a multi-agent LLM coding framework developed by Copur-Gencturk et al.
* [GRAIL combined dataset of three classroom experiments on non-interactive teaching in optics](grail-combined-dataset-of-three-classroom-experiments-on-non-interactive-teaching-in-optic.md) - A combined dataset of three randomized classroom experiments with German secondary physics students comparing restudy with voice-message explanation after instruction on converging lenses, produced by Lachner et al.
* [Graph4Learn (G4L) intelligent tutoring system](graph4learn-g4l-intelligent-tutoring-system.md) - A Python-based intelligent tutoring web application developed by Csépányi-Fürjes et al.
* [Graphical Languages in Mathematics (GLIM) test](graphical-languages-in-mathematics-glim-test.md) - A 36-item assessment developed by Lowrie and colleagues to measure Grades 4–6 students' decoding of six graphical languages in mathematics, including number lines, graphs, maps, and diagrams.
* [Grasha-Riechmann Learning Style Inventory](grasha-riechmann-learning-style-inventory.md) - A 60-item Likert-scale assessment developed by Anthony Grasha to measure students' social-interaction learning-style preferences across independent-dependent, avoidant-participant, and collaborative-competitive dimensions; this study used a Turkish adaptation.
* [Green Dot Public Schools](green-dot-public-schools.md) - A nonprofit charter-school programme and school-design model run by Green Dot Public Schools that creates small community high schools organized around teacher support, autonomous leadership, college preparation, comprehensive student services, family engagement, and replicability.
* [Group and representation tool (GRT)](group-and-representation-tool-grt.md) - The Group and representation tool (GRT), developed by Erkens, Bodemer, and Hoppe, transforms students' written text into variables to support heterogeneous group formation and cognitive group-awareness visualization.
* [GrouPer](grouper.md) - GrouPer is an AI-based student-grouping recommendation tool developed for teachers that uses unsupervised clustering of assessment responses to support formative analysis and assignment of adapted learning activities.
* [Grow Network](grow-network.md) - The Grow Network is a reporting platform operated by the Grow Network that transforms grades 3–9 assessment results into customized print and online reports, instructional resources, and parent-facing explanations for educators, principals, and families.
* [GROW project](grow-project.md) - An expert-consensus framework developed by the GROW project that guides primary mathematics teachers through diagnosing needs, setting differentiated goals, differentiating instruction and practice, and evaluating progress.
* [GROW teacher professional development programme](grow-teacher-professional-development-programme.md) - A year-long professional development programme run by educational consultants and school-based project coaches to develop primary teachers' competency in differentiated mathematics instruction through team meetings, coaching, lesson observation, and school-level implementation planning.
* [Grow Your Own Illinois program](grow-your-own-illinois-program.md) - An Illinois state-funded programme that supports community members and prospective high school candidates in becoming certified teachers through scholarships and partnerships among school districts, preparation programmes, and community-based organizations.
* [GroweLab](growelab.md) - GroweLab is engage2learn's all-in-one coaching and talent-development platform for facilitating and documenting educator coaching and tracking professional-growth data and outcomes.
* [GSA](gsa.md) - A teacher professional-development system that guides educators in videotaping, coding, and reviewing their own classroom teaching to identify strengths and areas for change.
* [Guidance-based ChatGPT-assisted Learning Aid (GCLA)](guidance-based-chatgpt-assisted-learning-aid-gcla.md) - GCLA is a ChatGPT-assisted learning aid developed by Lee et al.
* [Guide to Measuring Implementation: The 3Us](guide-to-measuring-implementation-the-3us.md) - A Mathematica practical guidance resource that helps implementing organizations decide when and how to measure implementation indicators and use the findings.
* [Guide to Setting Targets for Research Questions](guide-to-setting-targets-for-research-questions.md) - A Mathematica practical guide prepared for the Gates Foundation to help organizations and research partners set and use targets in evidence-building work.
* [Guiding Principles for Enacting SoLD-Aligned Teacher Preparation framework](guiding-principles-for-enacting-sold-aligned-teacher-preparation-framework.md) - A Darling-Hammond et al.

#### H {: #letter-h }

* [Harmonized cross-national PISA 2018–2022 country-level dataset with learning environment and career aspiration indicators](harmonized-cross-national-pisa-20182022-country-level-dataset-with-learning-environment-an.md) - A balanced longitudinal country-level dataset assembled by Popa et al.
* [Hazel Prover](hazel-prover.md) - Hazel Prover is a web-based classroom proof assistant developed as an extension to the Hazel live typed functional programming environment for teaching equational and inductive reasoning about typed functional programs.
* [HDR VC brachytherapy immersive VR training platform](hdr-vc-brachytherapy-immersive-vr-training-platform.md) - A Meta Quest 3 and edge-cloud-based virtual-reality platform developed by Ronghua Xu et al.
* [Head Start](head-start.md) - Head Start is a federally funded U.S.
* [Head-Toes-Knees-Shoulders (HTKS) task](head-toes-knees-shoulders-htks-task.md) - A brief, game-like direct assessment developed for young children that measures behavioral self-regulation through rule reversal, attention, working-memory, and inhibitory-control tasks.
* [Headsprout Early Reading](headsprout-early-reading.md) - Headsprout Early Reading is an Internet-based supplemental early-literacy curriculum developed by Headsprout, using adaptive animated lessons, stories, flashcards, and readers to teach foundational reading skills.
* [Healthy Harlem](healthy-harlem.md) - Healthy Harlem is a school-based health and wellness programme whose Get Fit and Prevention components promote students' physical fitness, nutrition knowledge, and physical-activity self-efficacy.
* [Healthy Minds Program (HMP)](healthy-minds-program-hmp.md) - Healthy Minds Innovations' Healthy Minds Program is a free smartphone-based meditation app that delivers structured audio lessons and guided practices for cultivating well-being skills.
* [Healy Picture Completion Test](healy-picture-completion-test.md) - A standardized performance assessment in which children complete pictures and receive score-, time-, and age-level classifications, administered and scored using Pintner and Anderson procedures.
* [HEARTS](hearts.md) - A whole-school, trauma-informed programme developed by the University of California, San Francisco and implemented in San Francisco Unified School District schools through tiered supports, staff training, consultation, and student interventions.
* [HEC Health Equity Framework](hec-health-equity-framework.md) - A co-developed health-equity framework with implementation supports that organizes actions to improve care and disrupt intersecting systems of oppression in healthcare.
