* [Heggerty's Bridge to Reading](heggertys-bridge-to-reading.md) - A teacher-led foundational skills curriculum from Heggerty that combines explicit phonics and phonemic-awareness lessons for daily kindergarten through grade 2 instruction.
* [HelpCoach](helpcoach.md) - A research-developed in situ chat-interface add-on by Hyoungwook Jin and colleagues that monitors and scaffolds students’ targeted help-seeking during AI-supported problem solving.
* [Here and Now Story Book](here-and-now-story-book.md) - A collection of stories for young children based on their everyday activities and interests, published by E.
* [Hero Elementary](hero-elementary.md) - Hero Elementary is a multiplatform K–2 science and literacy curriculum programme offering themed activity playlists for use in formal and informal learning settings.
* [Hierarchical Diagnostic Reasoning (HDR)](hierarchical-diagnostic-reasoning-hdr.md) - A structured case-based assessment proposed by Takahashi et al.
* [High School Redirection](high-school-redirection.md) - High School Redirection is an alternative high-school programme model for students at high risk of noncompletion, implemented through small schools offering remedial instruction, mentoring, childcare, independent study, and accelerated credit accumulation.
* [Higher Vocational Students' Perceived Learning Outcomes Scale](higher-vocational-students-perceived-learning-outcomes-scale.md) - A 5-point Likert self-report scale developed by Song Yafeng et al.
* [HIP Taxonomy](hip-taxonomy.md) - A taxonomy and implementation tool developed by IUPUI for designing, implementing, and scaling ePortfolio-based high-impact learning experiences.
* [Holding onto the GREEN Zone](holding-onto-the-green-zone.md) - A Reilly et al.
* [Hong Kong Knowledge-Building Teachers Network (KBTN)](hong-kong-knowledge-building-teachers-network-kbtn.md) - A Hong Kong Ministry of Education-supported teacher network that connects policy, peer support, CSCL research, knowledge-building theory, and classroom technology to support teachers adopting knowledge-building pedagogy.
* [Hoot Reading](hoot-reading.md) - Hoot Reading is a proprietary-platform, video-based one-to-one literacy tutoring programme delivered by trained Hoot Reading teachers in partnership with schools and districts.
* [Hour of Code](hour-of-code.md) - The Hour of Code is a Code.org initiative offering one-hour introductory computer-science activities for K–12 students and implemented by schools and other participating organizations.
* [How I Know project](how-i-know-project.md) - WestEd's How I Know project framework provides five synergistic practices for school leaders guiding formative-assessment implementation and scaling.

#### I {: #letter-i }

* [I CAN Learn](i-can-learn.md) - I CAN Learn® is a self-paced, mastery-based computer mathematics curriculum developed by JRL Enterprises, Inc., with interactive lessons, placement-based learning paths, practice, quizzes, and classroom tracking tools.
* [I-BEST](i-best.md) - A Washington State Board for Community and Technical Colleges programme that integrates adult basic education with occupational training through team teaching and learner support services.
* [i-Ready](i-ready.md) - i-Ready is Curriculum Associates' digital learning platform combining computer-adaptive Diagnostic assessments with personalized Reading and Mathematics instruction.
* [i-Ready Personalized Instruction](i-ready-personalized-instruction.md) - An online personalized reading-instruction program for grades K–8 that uses i-Ready Diagnostic data to assign lessons and monitor progress, developed and provided by Curriculum Associates.
* [I3P](i3p.md) - A programme run by UNAL's National Directorate of Pedagogical Innovation that supports collaborative pedagogical innovation projects intended to foster Professional Learning Communities across campuses and faculties.
* [iASK (Iowa Assessment of Skills and Knowledge for reading)](iask-iowa-assessment-of-skills-and-knowledge-for-reading.md) - A computer-based reading assessment developed to measure struggling students’ word-recognition knowledge and automaticity through tasks such as picture matching, rhyme selection, and auditory–visual verification.
* [IB DP](ib-dp.md) - The IB DP is an internationally developed upper-secondary curriculum and credential programme run by the International Baccalaureate for students preparing for higher education.
* [ICNALE](icnale.md) - ICNALE is a controlled learner corpus of argumentative English essays from Asian EFL learners and native English speakers, created and maintained by the International Corpus Network of Asian Learners of English.
* [ICNALE Global Rating Archive (GRA) dataset](icnale-global-rating-archive-gra-dataset.md) - A publicly available dataset derived from ICNALE, released by Stefano Bannò and colleagues, containing L2 English learner essays and speeches with holistic and analytic ratings from an English-as-a-Lingua-Franca assessment perspective.
* [Ideal pathways system](ideal-pathways-system.md) - A college-and-career pathways framework proposed by Rebeca Shackleford and colleagues that coordinates CTE, work-based learning, postsecondary opportunities, credentials, and continuous counseling for adolescents and young adults.
* [IDEAS](ideas.md) - A NASA-associated professional-development initiative that used a four-day teacher academy and two years of follow-up support to help K–12 educators integrate astronomy and space-science web resources and computer technology into teaching.
* [IELCE](ielce.md) - A federally supported adult-education programme administered through state and local providers that combines English-language literacy instruction with civics education for adult learners, including immigrants.
* [IELTS Writing Scored Essays Dataset](ielts-writing-scored-essays-dataset.md) - A publicly available Kaggle dataset of 787 Academic IELTS Task 2 essays with official examiner-assigned band scores, used for automated writing-assessment research.
* [IF-AT](if-at.md) - A commercial scratch-and-reveal multiple-choice assessment form made by Epstein Educational Enterprises that provides immediate item-level correctness feedback and supports partial-credit scoring.
* [Ignite Reading](ignite-reading.md) - Ignite Reading is a virtual literacy tutoring programme that provides daily one-to-one foundational-reading sessions for young learners through trained tutors and a structured instructional sequence.
* [IKS-Instruct dataset](iks-instruct-dataset.md) - A released multilingual dataset of 24,795 instruction-response pairs for fine-tuning and evaluating language models on Indian Knowledge Systems pedagogy, created and released by Shwetha Singaravelu and colleagues.
* [iLAP (Interactive Learning and Assessment Platform)](ilap-interactive-learning-and-assessment-platform.md) - An online platform developed to support teaching and assessment of Liberal Studies in Hong Kong schools through assignment submission, rubric development, assessment implementation, and performance monitoring.
* [Illinois Community College Board (ICCB) data via the Illinois Longitudinal Data System (ILDS)](illinois-community-college-board-iccb-data-via-the-illinois-longitudinal-data-system-ilds.md) - A statewide administrative dataset maintained by the Illinois Community College Board and accessed through the Illinois Longitudinal Data System, containing enrollment, credential, program, and credit records for students at Illinois public two-year colleges.
* [Illinois Kindergarten Individual Development Survey (KIDS)](illinois-kindergarten-individual-development-survey-kids.md) - A statewide teacher-administered kindergarten readiness assessment used in Illinois public schools, based on the Desired Results Developmental Profile–Kindergarten, with required professional development and coaching for implementation.
* [Illinois Monetary Award Program (MAP) grant](illinois-monetary-award-program-map-grant.md) - Illinois's need-based higher-education grant programme, administered by the Illinois Student Assistance Commission to help eligible Illinois residents afford in-state postsecondary education.
* [Illinois principal preparation redesign](illinois-principal-preparation-redesign.md) - A statewide Illinois policy and programme redesign for preparing and certifying school principals through selective admissions, competency-based coursework, internships, and performance assessments.
* [Imagine Español](imagine-espanol.md) - Imagine Español is Imagine Learning's personalized Spanish language and literacy curriculum for students in grades Pre-K–6, supporting dual-language, transitional bilingual, and world-language programs.
* [Imagine IM](imagine-im.md) - Imagine IM is a print and digital K–12 core mathematics curriculum developed by Imagine Learning and certified by Illustrative Mathematics for problem-based, student-centered instruction.
* [Imagine Language & Literacy](imagine-language-literacy.md) - Imagine Language & Literacy is Imagine Learning’s web-based adaptive supplemental literacy program for students from pre-kindergarten through sixth grade.
* [Imagine Math](imagine-math.md) - Imagine Math is Imagine Learning's adaptive digital supplemental mathematics learning platform for Pre-Kindergarten through Geometry, offering scaffolded instruction and on-demand support from certified teachers.
* [Imagine Math Facts](imagine-math-facts.md) - Imagine Learning's Imagine Math Facts is a digital 3D game-based program for practicing addition, subtraction, multiplication, and division to build arithmetic fluency and automaticity.
* [IMPROV framework](improv-framework.md) - Chen's framework guides learning-science researchers, designers, educators, and practitioners through six interconnected action areas for infrastructuring and sustaining educational innovations while building practitioner-led design capacity.
* [Improving Chicago Schools: The Students Speak, 1997 and Improving Chicago Schools: The Teachers' Turn, 1997](improving-chicago-schools-the-students-speak-1997-and-improving-chicago-schools-the-teache.md) - A pair of student and teacher surveys administered by the Consortium on Chicago School Research to measure views of school environment, classroom learning, parent involvement, school governance, and teachers' professional work life.
* [Improving Chicago's Schools biannual survey](improving-chicagos-schools-biannual-survey.md) - A biannual Consortium on Chicago School Research survey that collects students' reports on school experiences and educators' reports on instruction and working conditions across Chicago Public Schools.
* [INCLUD-ED project](includ-ed-project.md) - A European research project that identified Successful Educational Actions, including dialogic leadership and interactive groups, for inclusive school transformation and community participation.
* [Indiana College Core](indiana-college-core.md) - The Indiana Commission for Higher Education's Indiana College Core programme enables high school students to earn transferable college credits, supported by the My College Core interactive planning tool and counselor guidance.
* [Indiana grant and scholarship programs (21st Century Scholars, Frank O'Bannon, Mitch Daniels Early Graduation, Workforce Ready)](indiana-grant-and-scholarship-programs-21st-century-scholars-frank-obannon-mitch-daniels-e.md) - Indiana state financial-aid programmes that provide grants or scholarships to eligible students pursuing postsecondary education or workforce credentials.
* [Indicators-of-distress framework](indicators-of-distress-framework.md) - A National Charter School Resource Center framework that organizes leading signs of charter-school decline across six domains to help leaders, boards, authorizers, and support organizations identify risks and target interventions.
* [Individual-participant-data meta-analysis datasets of examination scores and passing rates disaggregated by minoritized-group status](individual-participant-data-meta-analysis-datasets-of-examination-scores-and-passing-rates.md) - An openly available collection of individual-participant examination-score and passing-rate records disaggregated by minoritized-group or socioeconomic status, released by Theobald et al.
* [Information Studies Research Accelerator (ISRA)](information-studies-research-accelerator-isra.md) - A first-year residential living-learning community programme run by its host institution to introduce undergraduate students to information studies research.
* [INJECT Exercise Platform (IXP)](inject-exercise-platform-ixp.md) - An open-source platform developed by the INJECT team for delivering tabletop exercise scenarios, coordinating team interactions, and recording activity data for assessment.
* [InkSurvey](inksurvey.md) - InkSurvey is free, web-based software developed for educators that collects keyed or digital-ink student responses in real time and enables anonymous display to a class.
* [InsideTrack Coaching](insidetrack-coaching.md) - InsideTrack© Coaching is a postsecondary student-support programme run by InsideTrack that provides proactive, personalized coaching through multiple communication channels to help students overcome academic and non-academic barriers to persistence and graduation.
* [INSIGHTS into Children's Temperament](insights-into-childrens-temperament.md) - INSIGHTS into Children's Temperament is an evidence-based intervention programme that equips educators, parents, and young children to understand and respond to children's temperament through professional development, classroom sessions, and facilitated parent lessons.
* [InSitu (InSituations) instrument](insitu-insituations-instrument.md) - A mobile-based 17-item self-report instrument introduced by Pöysä et al.
* [Insta-Reviewer](insta-reviewer.md) - Insta-Reviewer is a research-developed software system that extracts salient sentences from student project reports and uses a fine-tuned BART model to generate textual feedback.
* [Instruction Study](instruction-study.md) - A Chicago Public Schools district-wide dataset created by Stevens et al.
* [Instructional coherence framework: four student-centered approaches share commitments to identity, agency, equity, and community](instructional-coherence-framework-four-student-centered-approaches-share-commitments-to-id.md) - A framework developed by Browning et al.
* [Instructional Content Emphasis Instrument–Revised (ICE-R)](instructional-content-emphasis-instrumentrevised-ice-r.md) - A multidimensional classroom-observation instrument developed by Swanson et al.
* [Instructional Conversations and Literature Logs](instructional-conversations-and-literature-logs.md) - A programme developed by William Saunders and Claude Goldenberg for English language learners that combines teacher-facilitated small-group instructional conversations with literature-log writing and sharing.
* [Instructional Leadership Corps (ILC)](instructional-leadership-corps-ilc.md) - A statewide California teacher-led professional learning initiative run by the Stanford Center for Opportunity Policy in Education, the California Teachers Association, and the National Board Resource Center to develop teacher leadership and support instructional shifts.
* [Instrumental Enrichment](instrumental-enrichment.md) - Instrumental Enrichment is Reuven Feuerstein’s structured cognitive-intervention program, designed to create mediated-learning situations that support cognitive modifiability and is implemented by educators or trained mediators.
* [Interaction Equivalency Theorem Indicator](interaction-equivalency-theorem-indicator.md) - A Miyazoe et al.
* [Interactive evidence and gap maps and an open web database of AIHEd reviews built with EPPI Mapper and EPPI Visualiser](interactive-evidence-and-gap-maps-and-an-open-web-database-of-aihed-reviews-built-with-epp.md) - A public database and interactive evidence-gap-map resource created by Bond et al.
* [Interactive evidence and gap maps of motivation regulation research, built with EPPI-Mapper, with the underlying dataset released on OSF](interactive-evidence-and-gap-maps-of-motivation-regulation-research-built-with-eppi-mapper.md) - An OSF-released dataset and EPPI-Mapper interactive visualisation created by Maike Trautner et al.
* [Intermediate Mechanics Tutorials](intermediate-mechanics-tutorials.md) - A University of Maine curriculum under development for second-year mechanics courses in which students first encounter differential equations, with associated materials made available through the project.
* [Internationals Network for Public Schools](internationals-network-for-public-schools.md) - The Internationals Network for Public Schools framework guides a network of schools serving recent immigrant and English-learner students through five principles covering collaboration, experiential learning, language–content integration, local autonomy, and a common learning model.
* [Interpretive Spelling Test (IST)](interpretive-spelling-test-ist.md) - The Interpretive Spelling Test is a spelling-to-dictation assessment developed by Niolaki et al.
* [IntroEduAI](introeduai.md) - IntroEduAI is an educational platform developed to deliver programming assignments, automatically evaluate student responses with large language models against instructor-authored rubrics, and generate response-level feedback and assignment-level reports.
* [Inventory of Learning Styles (ILS)](inventory-of-learning-styles-ils.md) - A self-report assessment developed by Jan Vermunt to measure higher-education students' processing and regulation strategies, conceptions of learning, and learning orientations.
* [Inventory of Perceived Study Environment Extended (IPSEE)](inventory-of-perceived-study-environment-extended-ipsee.md) - A 56-item student questionnaire, with a parallel teacher version, developed by Karin D.
* [Inventory of School Motivation (ISM)](inventory-of-school-motivation-ism.md) - A school-motivation measurement instrument developed by McInerney and used to assess motivation across cultural groups, with cross-cultural validation and measurement-invariance testing required for new populations.
* [Investigations in Number, Data, and Space; Math Expressions; Saxon Math; and Scott Foresman-Addison Wesley Mathematics](investigations-in-number-data-and-space-math-expressions-saxon-math-and-scott-foresman-add.md) - Four published elementary mathematics curricula evaluated comparatively by Agodini et al.
* [IPS-EDP model](ips-edp-model.md) - A seven-principle instructional-design framework developed by Boetje et al.
* [IQB Trends in Student Achievement large-scale assessment data (IQB-BT 2021 and IQB-BT 2022)](iqb-trends-in-student-achievement-large-scale-assessment-data-iqb-bt-2021-and-iqb-bt-2022.md) - German large-scale educational assessment data produced by the Institute for Educational Quality Improvement (IQB), containing achievement scores and contextual questionnaires for representative student cohorts before and during the COVID-19 pandemic.
* [iRCL](ircl.md) - i-Ready Classroom Mathematics is Curriculum Associates’ core K–8 mathematics curriculum integrating print and digital instructional materials, practice tools, hands-on resources, and professional learning supports.
* [IRLA](irla.md) - The Independent Reading Level Assessment (IRLA) is a reading assessment framework used as a potential screening tool within Response to Intervention.
* [Is Your State Prioritizing Social, Emotional, and Academic Development? 50-state scan](is-your-state-prioritizing-social-emotional-and-academic-development-50-state-scan.md) - A 50-state policy-scan data resource released by Ed Trust in partnership with CASEL that compiles publicly available state policies so educators, advocates, and policymakers can compare support for students’ social, emotional, and academic development.
* [ISD-Agent-Bench](isd-agent-bench.md) - ISD-Agent-Bench is a benchmark dataset and evaluation harness created by YoungHoon Jeon et al.
* [iSELF professional development program](iself-professional-development-program.md) - iSELF is a professional-development programme developed for primary teachers that combines SRL theory training, lesson-planning support, coached lesson observation, and reflective feedback to promote explicit, integrated SRL instruction.

* [ITAS conversational instructor analytics agent](itas-conversational-instructor-analytics-agent.md) - ITAS is a conversational analytics agent that helps instructors and content developers query privacy-preserving aggregate learning-deployment data in natural language.

* [IUPUI ePortfolio HIP taxonomy: four attributes of high-impact ePortfolio practice at three ascending levels of impact](iupui-epportfolio-taxonomy-four-attributes.md) - The taxonomy identifies four attributes of high-impact ePortfolio practice: \"1.
* [IXL Math](ixl-math.md) - IXL Learning's IXL Math is a personalized mathematics learning platform with adaptive skills, tutorials, diagnostics, analytics, and classroom-support tools for learners from PreK through Calculus.

* [JOBSTART](jobstart.md) - JOBSTART is a nonresidential alternative education and occupational-training programme for disadvantaged high school dropouts, combining GED preparation, occupational skills training, support services, and job placement assistance.

* [KBDeX (Knowledge Building Discourse Explorer)](kbdex-knowledge-building-discourse-explorer.md) - KBDeX is a social network analysis tool developed by Oshima and colleagues for visualizing and analyzing collaborative ideas and discourse in knowledge-building communities.
* [KDD Cup 2010 Bridge to Algebra dataset](kdd-cup-2010-bridge-to-algebra-dataset.md) - A Carnegie Learning–donated dataset distributed through the PSLC DataShop containing large-scale student interaction records from mathematics curriculum practice for educational data-mining and knowledge-tracing research.
* [Keller et al. (2021) harmonized six-cycle PISA top-performing student dataset](keller-et-al-2021-harmonized-six-cycle-pisa-top-performing-student-dataset.md) - A harmonized individual-participant dataset covering six PISA cycles and 82 countries, with accompanying analysis protocols, R code, and materials released by the study authors through OSF.
* [Kentucky Academic Standards for Computer Science](kentucky-academic-standards-for-computer-science.md) - A framework issued by the Kentucky Department of Education that defines K–8 computer-science learning standards, including Data & Analysis and Impacts of Computing strands.
* [KG-grounded benchmark of 10,836 solution-feedback pairs for step-level LLM tutoring evaluation](kg-grounded-benchmark-of-10836-solution-feedback-pairs-for-step-level-llm-tutoring-evaluat.md) - A benchmark dataset released by Tahreem Yasir et al.

* [KiVa, Olweus Bullying Prevention Program (OBPP), NoTrap!, and ViSC](kiva-olweus-bullying-prevention-program-obpp-notrap-and-visc.md) - Four branded school-based anti-bullying programmes run by their respective developers and implementers for primary and secondary schools, using whole-school, classroom, individual, peer, online, and train-the-trainer interventions.

* [Knower-LevelEstimater.xls](knower-levelestimaterxls.md) - A Microsoft Excel spreadsheet tool by Negen et al.
* [Knowledge Building](knowledge-building.md) - A pedagogical framework associated with Scardamalia and Bereiter that organizes inquiry, dialogic discourse, and collaborative creation and refinement of community knowledge, with students sharing responsibility and teachers facilitating.
* [Knowledge Catalyser](knowledge-catalyser.md) - A computer-supported discussion forum developed by Gerry Stahl and colleagues for small-group knowledge-building discussions with tools for voting on, annotating, critiquing, and revising postings.
* [Knowledge Connections Analyzer (KCA)](knowledge-connections-analyzer-kca.md) - A web-based tool developed by Yang et al.
* [Knowlify AI video generation platform](knowlify-ai-video-generation-platform.md) - Knowlify is an AI video-generation platform that creates editable explainer videos from natural-language prompts and source documents.
* [KOKU](koku.md) - An NHS-approved tablet-delivered falls-prevention programme co-developed with older adults and falls-prevention experts, combining progressive strength-and-balance exercises with eCoach guidance, safety information, reminders, progress tracking, and health-literacy games for older adults.
* [Kolibri](kolibri.md) - Kolibri is Learning Equality's open-source, offline-first learning platform for delivering educational content and supporting learning in low-connectivity settings.
* [Korean alphabet writing assessment for handwriting legibility](korean-alphabet-writing-assessment-for-handwriting-legibility.md) - A short-range copying assessment that measures preschool children's handwriting legibility by having them reproduce Korean alphabet letters in designated boxes; it was used by Seo S-M (2018).
* [KP-Lab](kp-lab.md) - The KP-Lab toolset, developed by the European KP-Lab project, is a suite of digital tools for creating knowledge artefacts, coordinating collaborative knowledge-creation processes, supporting networked communities, and enabling reflection.
* [KPE](kpe.md) - KPE is a KP-Lab collaborative knowledge-creation platform that provides shared workspaces and tools for planning, producing, organizing, annotating, versioning, commenting on, and reflecting on shared knowledge objects.

* [KT4EQG](kt4eqg.md) - KT4EQG is a personalized question-generation system combining knowledge tracing, large-language-model generation, reinforcement learning, and an alignment verifier, developed by Xinyi Gao et al.
* [KTST](ktst.md) - An open-source code repository released by Kai Neubauer and colleagues implementing knowledge tracing set transformers, their set-based interaction embeddings, and learnable ALiBi attention for research in knowledge tracing and intelligent tutoring systems.
* [Kutti AI](kutti-ai.md) - Kutti AI is a React Native/Expo learning-companion application developed by Kadharmoideen Fadurudeen that delivers curriculum instruction, spoken questions, speech-based responses, difficulty detection, and feedback through audio, with offline-capable and multilingual support.
* [KVEC micro-credentials](kvec-micro-credentials.md) - A Kentucky Valley Educational Cooperative professional-learning programme of free, competency-based micro-credentials for rural K–12 educators, created through a grant partnership with Digital Promise and hosted on the Digital Promise Micro-credential Platform.
* [Kwara-STEM AI Tutor](kwara-stem-ai-tutor.md) - A localized AI diagnostic tutoring tool developed for STEM practical instruction that provides students with real-time feedback and correction during tasks.
* [KWiK](kwik.md) - KWiK is a teacher-coaching and computer-assisted learning programme developed and evaluated by Philip Oreopoulos and colleagues to support mastery-based mathematics practice in elementary and middle schools.

#### L {: #letter-l }

* [Labelled corpus of 4,217 discussion posts](labelled-corpus-of-4217-discussion-posts.md) - A dataset created by Gorgun et al.

* [LEAGUE framework](league-framework.md) - A six-pillar institutional review framework developed by S.
* [League of Innovative Schools](league-of-innovative-schools.md) - A Digital Promise membership network that brings together school districts for cross-district collaboration, partnerships, professional learning, and sharing of innovative practices.
* [LEAP (Learning with AI about Physics)](leap-learning-with-ai-about-physics.md) - LEAP is a web platform developed for teachers to author physics tasks with tailored LLM instructions and deliver automated formative feedback to students.
* [Learn.Plan.Succeed.](learnplansucceed.md) - Learn.Plan.Succeed.
* [LearnAdapt Praxis](learnadapt-praxis.md) - LearnAdapt Praxis is a LearnAdapt web application that structures adult workplace-learning projects through the stages Frame, Learn, Build, Validate, and Transfer.
* [LearnAI Framework](learnai-framework.md) - A university-developed two-layer framework combining wide-scale AI-readiness exposure with customized co-creation support for mixed-ability learners.

* [Learning Genie](learning-genie.md) - Learning Genie is an AI-supported platform made by Learning Genie for preschool through grade 12 instructional planning, documentation, assessment, portfolios, differentiation, and family communication.

* [Learning Studios](learning-studios.md) - Learning Studios is a network of technology-equipped classrooms operated through HP and Microsoft's Reinvent the Classroom initiative, providing hardware, design-project guides, teacher training, professional development, and an online learning community.

* [Lectura Proactiva](lectura-proactiva.md) - Lectura Proactiva, distributed as Intervenciones tempranas de la lectura, is a bilingual one-to-small-group reading intervention for Spanish-speaking early readers, delivered by certified teachers.

* [Lessons in Character](lessons-in-character.md) - Lessons in Character is a 24-lesson character-education curriculum for elementary and middle school students, developed for classroom use through weekly themes, stories, writing activities, and projects.
* [Level of Adoption (LoA) survey](level-of-adoption-loa-survey.md) - An online collaborative self-report survey developed by Orr et al.
* [Leveled Literacy Intervention](leveled-literacy-intervention.md) - Leveled Literacy Intervention is a Fountas & Pinnell literacy intervention programme providing daily small-group tutoring for struggling readers, typically in kindergarten through grade 2.
* [Levels of Use Interview](levels-of-use-interview.md) - A focused branching interview developed by Loucks (1977) that assigns overall and category-specific Levels of Use ratings, including Not Doing and No Information classifications.
* [Lexia Academy](lexia-academy.md) - Lexia Academy is Lexia Learning’s self-paced eLearning platform for educators, providing professional-development courses and resources for literacy instruction, blended learning, and implementation of Lexia products.
* [Lexia Core5 Reading](lexia-core5-reading.md) - Lexia Learning's Lexia Core5 Reading is a blended digital literacy programme for pre-K–5 students that provides personalized foundational-reading instruction, embedded assessment, and teacher support resources.
* [Lexia English](lexia-english.md) - Lexia English Language Development is a blended English-language-development programme from Lexia Learning for K–6 English Learners, combining student-directed digital lessons with teacher-led instruction and progress data.

* [Lexia Reading](lexia-reading.md) - Lexia Reading is a computerized supplemental phonics and reading-skills practice program with adaptive independent practice and web-based progress reporting, developed and provided by Lexia Learning.
* [Liberatory Space framework for Black student thriving](liberatory-space-framework-for-black-student-thriving.md) - A framework developed for Long Beach Unified School District's Black Community to guide the design of environments that promote Black students' belonging, identity affirmation, agency, and achievement.
* [Library of Congress DBPH talking-book audio service network](library-of-congress-dbph-talking-book-audio-service-network.md) - A Library of Congress Division for the Blind and Physically Handicapped service network that distributes recorded books and magazines, lending equipment and providing reader support through regional, state, local, and volunteer organizations.
* [LINCS certification track program in supply chain management](lincs-certification-track-program-in-supply-chain-management.md) - A TAACCCT-funded supply-chain management certification initiative developed by a Broward College-led nine-college consortium with industry and university partners for early-career professionals.
* [Lindamood Phoneme Sequencing (LiPS)](lindamood-phoneme-sequencing-lips.md) - A branded phonological-awareness and decoding intervention developed by Lindamood-Bell Learning Processes for emergent and struggling readers, including students with dyslexia.
