* [Linked dataset of institutional GenAI policies and CS course syllabi from U.S. R1 universities](linked-dataset-of-institutional-genai-policies-and-cs-course-syllabi-from-us-r1-universiti.md) - A research dataset created by Amrita Ganguly et al.
* [Linked five-year student-level administrative dataset from Pittsburgh Public Schools, Propel Schools, and Allegheny County DHS](linked-five-year-student-level-administrative-dataset-from-pittsburgh-public-schools-prope.md) - A five-year linked student-level administrative dataset assembled by the study team from Pittsburgh Public Schools, Propel Schools, and the Allegheny County Department of Human Services for analyzing and predicting near-term academic risks.
* [Linked Learning Alliance two-tiered certification system](linked-learning-alliance-two-tiered-certification-system.md) - A certification framework run by the Linked Learning Alliance to evaluate and support secondary-school Linked Learning pathways combining academics, career technical education, work-based learning, and student support services.
* [LinkedIn skill network dataset: 2.4 million US workers, 16,753 skills, 556,206 directed edges, 12 skill domains](linkedin-skill-network-dataset-24-million-us-workers-16753-skills-556206-directed-edges-12.md) - A Carpanelli et al.
* [Linking Learning to Careers](linking-learning-to-careers.md) - A Vermont Division of Vocational Rehabilitation programme that provides high school students with disabilities individualized career planning, integrated work-based learning, college exploration, team-based guidance, assistive technology support, and transportation funding.
* [LiquidText](liquidtext.md) - LiquidText is a tablet-based multiple-document reading and sensemaking application developed by LiquidText that lets learners view, annotate, link, and synthesize information across documents.
* [Literacy Boost](literacy-boost.md) - Literacy Boost is Save the Children's early-literacy intervention programme, including teacher professional development and classroom-support components for young learners.
* [Literacy Express](literacy-express.md) - A preschool curriculum package for three- to five-year-old children that integrates oral language, emergent literacy, mathematics, science, general knowledge, and socioemotional development through thematic units and daily group activities.
* [Literacy First](literacy-first.md) - Literacy First is an early-literacy tutoring programme run by Literacy First that provides trained tutors and individualized daily sessions for kindergarten through grade 2 students who are reading below grade level.
* [Literary axiom dataset: 1,455 Chinese-language records with typed mappings and relationships](literary-axiom-dataset-1455-chinese-language-records-with-typed-mappings-and-relationships.md) - Qiang Liu et al.'s dataset provides 1,455 Chinese-language literary-axiom records with typed mappings to literary works and typed relationships for literary scholarship and computational-creativity research.
* [Little Books](little-books.md) - Little Books is a Good Year Books curriculum resource consisting of reproducible, illustrated beginning-reading books for interactive reading by parents with children or teachers with students.
* [LittleCurriculum](littlecurriculum.md) - LittleCurriculum is an 88B-token K–5-filtered pretraining corpus created by Fanfei Li et al.
* [LittleLearner](littlelearner.md) - LittleLearner is a 5B-parameter language model trained from scratch by Fanfei Li et al.
* [LlamaLo](llamalo.md) - LlamaLo is a dialogue chatbot fine-tuned by Yifat Ben-David Kolikant et al.
* [LLM-queries](llm-queries.md) - A GitHub repository released by Keith et al.
* [LLMersion-1](llmersion-1.md) - Guo et al.'s open-source local language-learning software prototype integrates synchronized listening, reading, pronunciation, conversation, and writing practice, with the companion LLMersion Narrator for producing narrated audio and read-along video.
* [LLMs Unplugged](llms-unplugged.md) - A freely available CC BY-NC-SA collection of unplugged lessons, instructor notes, printable handouts, and optional n-gram booklet software tools created and hosted by Ben Swift for teaching language-model fundamentals and AI literacy.
* [Local Control Funding Formula (LCFF)](local-control-funding-formula-lcff.md) - California's statewide K–12 school-finance framework, administered through state education authorities, that distributes base, supplemental, and concentration funding to support more equitable funding based on student need.
* [Localized faculty AI literacy scale based on UNESCO's 2024 AI Competency Framework for Teachers](localized-faculty-ai-literacy-scale-based-on-unescos-2024-ai-competency-framework-for-teac.md) - A 15-item, five-point Likert self-report scale developed by Liu et al.
* [Long Beach Early Learning Hub](long-beach-early-learning-hub.md) - An online child-care and early-learning enrollment and assistance-finding platform developed collaboratively and hosted by the Mayor's Fund for Long Beach.
* [Longitudinal middle-school ITS log dataset with weekly engagement targets](longitudinal-middle-school-its-log-dataset-with-weekly-engagement-targets.md) - A longitudinal dataset of web-based intelligent tutoring system activity from 425 middle-school mathematics students, providing timestamped interaction logs and weekly practice-effort and skill-mastery targets for learning-analytics research.
* [Longitudinal SEL dataset from a large urban district with about 3,000 students per timepoint](longitudinal-sel-dataset-from-a-large-urban-district-with-about-3000-students-per-timepoin.md) - A four-year longitudinal dataset of students' social-emotional learning scores used to model SEL status and growth as predictors of ninth-grade academic risk.
* [Longitudinal sociometric and video-coded teacher behavior dataset from 1,420 fifth-grade students in 56 classes](longitudinal-sociometric-and-video-coded-teacher-behavior-dataset-from-1420-fifth-grade-st.md) - A research dataset assembled by Hendrickx et al.
* [Longitudinal student-level dataset on entry, exit, attrition, and replacement in KIPP middle schools](longitudinal-student-level-dataset-on-entry-exit-attrition-and-replacement-in-kipp-middle.md) - A longitudinal student-level dataset assembled by Ira Nichols-Barrer and colleagues to track entry, exit, attrition, and replacement in KIPP middle schools and compare student flows with nearby district-run schools.
* [Looking in Classrooms](looking-in-classrooms.md) - A textbook on effective teaching and classroom observation authored by Jere Brophy and Thomas Good for undergraduate and graduate education students.
* [Loss and Momentum Framework for community college student progression](loss-and-momentum-framework-for-community-college-student-progression.md) - A framework associated with Pathways Analysis that organizes community-college progression into connection, entry, progress, and completion stages and links common loss points to institutional momentum strategies.
* [Louisiana middle-school communications industrial arts curriculum guide (draft)](louisiana-middle-school-communications-industrial-arts-curriculum-guide-draft.md) - A Louisiana state curriculum guide for grades 6–9 that organizes communications-focused industrial arts instruction in drafting, electricity-electronics, graphic arts, and photography around performance objectives, activities, and resources.
* [Lovaas Model](lovaas-model.md) - An intensive early behavioral intervention programme developed at UCLA and delivered through the Lovaas Institute, combining one-to-one discrete-trial instruction, peer play, classroom inclusion, generalization activities, and parent training.
* [Lowell's 25-test primary-grade group intelligence battery in booklet form](lowells-25-test-primary-grade-group-intelligence-battery-in-booklet-form.md) - A 25-test group intelligence assessment battery developed by Lowell for first- through third-grade pupils and used to identify children needing individual Binet testing.
* [LT_NLP_SET_pub](lt-nlp-set-pub.md) - A GitHub repository maintained by the review authors containing the code, study lists, prompts, codebooks, frozen response bases, and adjudication materials needed to audit and reconstruct the SET-NLP evidence map.
* [LUCA browser-based office simulation](luca-browser-based-office-simulation.md) - LUCA is a browser-based office-simulation and assessment environment developed by Sabrina Ludwig and colleagues, providing simulated office tools, authoring of authentic work scenarios, embedded assessments, and activity logging for business education.
* [LUDIA](ludia.md) - LUDIA is a browser-based, private, multilingual AI thought-partner tool developed by Stark et al.
* [Lumen One Introduction to Statistics](lumen-one-introduction-to-statistics.md) - Lumen Learning's online statistics courseware replaces or supplements textbooks with segmented multimedia content, interactive practice, study supports, and instructor-facing engagement and intervention tools.
* [LumiNote](luminote.md) - LumiNote is an instructor-facing Unity3D and Meta Quest 3 VR system developed by Danxuan Liang and colleagues that uses GPT-4o and Whisper to convert spoken teaching intent into reviewable spatial annotations, demonstrations, and language support for stage-lighting education.

#### M {: #letter-m }

* [M&E Checklist](me-checklist.md) - The M&E Checklist is a resource developed by Ruggiero et al.
* [M&E Checklist for evidence-building with priority-community stakeholders](me-checklist-for-evidence-building-with-priority-community-stakeholders.md) - A project-developed checklist from the Evaluating the Development of Middle Years Math Teaching and Learning Solutions project, prepared for the Gates Foundation, that guides solution teams through stakeholder-collaborative evidence building and testing.
* [M&E Checklist Phase 1](me-checklist-phase-1.md) - A Mathematica-developed, Gates Foundation-supported checklist that guides stakeholders through designing, refining, and testing education solutions using a theory of change.
* [M&E Checklist Phase 2](me-checklist-phase-2.md) - A Mathematica practical guidance document prepared for the Gates Foundation that guides education-solution developers through refining an implemented solution and building descriptive implementation evidence.
* [M&E Needs Inventory](me-needs-inventory.md) - A self-assessment tool developed by Megan Shoji and colleagues for organizations to evaluate their measurement and evaluation capacity and identify priorities for external research-partner support.
* [M&E Plan Template](me-plan-template.md) - The Measurement and Evaluation (M&E) Plan Template is a roadmap described by Place et al.
* [M&E Reporting Template](me-reporting-template.md) - A Mathematica reporting template that guides organizations, research partners, and funders in producing concise, accessible summaries of study approaches, findings, and next steps.
* [M&E toolkit](me-toolkit.md) - A monitoring and evaluation toolkit that helps practitioners generate and tailor evidence for solutions at different stages of development.
* [M-PLANS (Motivation - Planning Lessons to Activate eNgagement in Science)](m-plans-motivation-planning-lessons-to-activate-engagement-in-science.md) - M-PLANS is a multi-week professional-learning programme and toolkit developed by Harris et al.
* [M-PLANS reference toolkit and classroom-based quick tools](m-plans-reference-toolkit-and-classroom-based-quick-tools.md) - M-PLANS is a teacher-facing framework and toolkit that provides motivation-principle look-fors, planning questions, aligned activities, talk moves, and just-in-time classroom supports for middle-school science instruction.
* [MACE program](mace-program.md) - Developed by Eveline M.
* [Mad City Mystery](mad-city-mystery.md) - Mad City Mystery is a place-based augmented-reality environmental-science curriculum/game developed by Squire and colleagues for students to investigate environmental mysteries and construct scientific arguments.
* [MAESTRO v3.0.0 pretraining corpus and custom 4,320-recording student dataset](maestro-v300-pretraining-corpus-and-custom-4320-recording-student-dataset.md) - The MAESTRO corpus is a public collection of MIDI-aligned solo-piano performances, while Zhu et al.
* [Magma Math](magma-math.md) - Magma Math is a browser- and iPad-based digital mathematics learning platform developed and operated by Magma Math for students in grades 4–8.
* [Magnetic Reading](magnetic-reading.md) - Magnetic Reading is a print-based supplemental grades 3–5 reading-comprehension curriculum developed by Great Minds, with grade-level texts, educator resources, and scaffolds for whole-class and differentiated instruction.
* [Magnetic Reading Foundations](magnetic-reading-foundations.md) - Magnetic Reading Foundations is a curriculum from Magnetic Reading that provides daily K–2 instruction in foundational reading skills through digital lessons, assessments, and interactive tutorials for differentiation.
* [MAI-AI: Metacognitive Awareness Inventory for Artificial Intelligence](mai-ai-metacognitive-awareness-inventory-for-artificial-intelligence.md) - A 29-item AI-centered Likert self-report instrument developed by Pau Benazet i Montobbio et al.
* [MAIC platform](maic-platform.md) - An online learning environment developed by the article's authors that combines course-preparation workflows, multi-agent classrooms, customizable classmate agents, and large-model learning-analytics tools for instructors and university students.
* [Major's diagnostic procedure for detecting mental debility in youth](majors-diagnostic-procedure-for-detecting-mental-debility-in-youth.md) - A multi-part diagnostic procedure attributed to Major that uses mathematical problems, discrimination questions, school-learning tests, psychoanalytic tests, and medical examination to assess youth suspected of mental debility.
* [Maker Learning Leadership Framework](maker-learning-leadership-framework.md) - A Digital Promise framework that helps K–12 leadership teams establish a maker-learning vision, build a supporting culture, and develop sustainable maker-learning programs.
* [Making Sense of Decoding and Spelling (MSDS)](making-sense-of-decoding-and-spelling-msds.md) - MSDS is a structured adult basic-education curriculum developed by Alamprese et al.
* [MALA (Modular Artificial Learning Assistance)](mala-modular-artificial-learning-assistance.md) - A GPT-4o-based modular chatbot architecture developed by Gabelmann et al.
* [MalAlgoLib](malalgolib.md) - MalAlgoLib, developed by Naiming Liu et al., is a graph-based library that generates linear-equation problems with correct and misconception-driven step-by-step solution traces for algebra learning and tutoring research.
* [Mandatory-random student drug testing](mandatory-random-student-drug-testing.md) - A school-based programme that subjects participating high-school students to random drug testing, typically implemented and evaluated at the school level.
* [Mangomon](mangomon.md) - Mangomon is a CEFR-based role-playing gamification mobile application developed by a Thai software company with Thammasat University's Language Institute for out-of-class business English vocabulary learning.
* [MAP Accelerator](map-accelerator.md) - Khan Academy MAP Accelerator is a mastery-based mathematics learning platform developed by Khan Academy in partnership with NWEA for students in grades 3–8.
* [MAP Growth](map-growth.md) - MAP Growth is NWEA's computer-adaptive assessment system, with national achievement and growth norms and reporting tools such as the MAP Growth National Dashboard.
* [MAP Growth Course-Specific assessments](map-growth-course-specific-assessments.md) - MAP Growth course-specific tests are NWEA's variable-length computerized adaptive mathematics and science assessments for Algebra, Geometry, Integrated Math, and Biology/Life Science, administered during the school year to measure course-specific achievement and growth.
* [MAP Growth Goal Explorer](map-growth-goal-explorer.md) - MAP Growth Goal Explorer is an NWEA software tool that helps teachers, families, and students set fall-to-spring growth goals and compare projected scores with academic benchmarks.
* [MAP Growth grades 1–2 achievement dataset (spring 2019 and spring 2022 samples)](map-growth-grades-12-achievement-dataset-spring-2019-and-spring-2022-samples.md) - A research dataset assembled by Kuhfeld et al.
* [MAP Growth interim computer adaptive assessment system](map-growth-interim-computer-adaptive-assessment-system.md) - NWEA's MAP Growth interim computer-adaptive assessment system delivers variable-length assessments several times a year and uses adaptive item selection and scoring to measure student achievement and growth.
* [MAP Growth math assessment data corpus](map-growth-math-assessment-data-corpus.md) - A large corpus of NWEA MAP Growth mathematics assessment data, including standard and accessible test administrations, used to study item accessibility and quality for students with and without visual impairments.
* [MAP Growth National Dashboard](map-growth-national-dashboard.md) - NWEA's dashboard software product that presents national student achievement and growth trend data from MAP Growth assessments.
* [MAP Growth norms](map-growth-norms.md) - MAP Growth norms are NWEA's nationally normed assessment benchmarks that report student achievement percentiles and conditional growth comparisons using multilevel growth models.
* [MAP Growth retest dataset for evaluating E-M scoring accuracy](map-growth-retest-dataset-for-evaluating-e-m-scoring-accuracy.md) - A research dataset assembled from archived NWEA MAP Growth mathematics and reading RIT-score records for students who completed closely timed retests, used to evaluate effort-moderated scoring accuracy.
* [MAP Growth Spanish Math and Reading assessments](map-growth-spanish-math-and-reading-assessments.md) - NWEA's computer-adaptive Spanish-language Math and Reading assessments measure student achievement and growth across specified grades and testing windows, using MAP Growth scales, norms, and blueprints where applicable.
* [MAP Growth Specialty School National Dashboard](map-growth-specialty-school-national-dashboard.md) - A NWEA data dashboard reporting growth results for U.S.
* [MAP Reading Fluency](map-reading-fluency.md) - MAP Reading Fluency is NWEA's online adaptive assessment for pre-K through grade 5 reading skills and oral reading fluency, with benchmark testing, progress monitoring, and an optional K–3 dyslexia screener in English and Spanish.
* [MAP Reading Fluency Dyslexia Screener](map-reading-fluency-dyslexia-screener.md) - The MAP Reading Fluency Dyslexia Screener is an NWEA assessment that combines reading-skill scores with Rapid Automatized Naming to identify students at risk of dyslexia.
* [MAP Reading Fluency Foundational Skills assessment](map-reading-fluency-foundational-skills-assessment.md) - An NWEA English early-literacy assessment for Grades K–3 measuring phonological awareness, phonics and word recognition, and language comprehension, with achievement and growth norms.
* [Maryland Concentration of Poverty (CoP) entitlement grant program](maryland-concentration-of-poverty-cop-entitlement-grant-program.md) - A Maryland state grant programme established by the Blueprint for Maryland’s Future that funds essential community-school staff and per-pupil supports in high-poverty public schools.
* [Maryland Teacher and Principal Evaluation System](maryland-teacher-and-principal-evaluation-system.md) - A statewide educator-evaluation programme combining professional-practice ratings with student-growth measures, administered by the Maryland State Department of Education.
* [Massachusetts DESE early literacy screening assessment dataset](massachusetts-dese-early-literacy-screening-assessment-dataset.md) - A multi-year dataset of K–3 early literacy screener results collected and maintained by the Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education from participating grantee schools and districts.
* [Massachusetts DESE statewide K–3 early literacy screening dataset (2023/24)](massachusetts-dese-statewide-k3-early-literacy-screening-dataset-202324.md) - A Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education dataset of K–3 early literacy screening results collected from participating schools and districts to identify students at risk of reading difficulty and guide early intervention.
* [Massachusetts early literacy screening assessment dataset linked with MCAS results](massachusetts-early-literacy-screening-assessment-dataset-linked-with-mcas-results.md) - A Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education dataset of K–3 early literacy screening results linked to state MCAS results for longitudinal analysis.
* [Massachusetts early literacy screening assessment dataset, 2022/23](massachusetts-early-literacy-screening-assessment-dataset-202223.md) - A Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education dataset of K–3 early literacy screening scores collected from participating grantee schools and districts during the 2022/23 school year.
* [Massachusetts early literacy screening assessment longitudinal database](massachusetts-early-literacy-screening-assessment-longitudinal-database.md) - A longitudinal database built by WestEd for the Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education linking grantee K–3 early literacy universal-screening scores with SIMS, MCAS, ACCESS for ELLs, and school- and district-level data.
* [Massachusetts K–3 early literacy screening longitudinal dataset (2020/21–2023/24)](massachusetts-k3-early-literacy-screening-longitudinal-dataset-202021202324.md) - WestEd’s longitudinal dataset for the Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education links K–3 early-literacy screening results with SIMS demographics, MCAS, ACCESS for ELLs, and school- and district-level contextual data to monitor literacy performance and reading risk.
* [MASTER-TPD prototype tool](master-tpd-prototype-tool.md) - A prototype design-support tool implementing the Training Program Design phase of the MASTER method, developed by Verstegen et al.
* [Math 180](math-180.md) - Math 180 is Scholastic's targeted mathematics intervention programme combining adaptive student software with teacher-facilitated instruction for students striving to reach grade-level proficiency.
* [Math Corps](math-corps.md) - Math Corps is an intensive summer mathematics camp for middle school students founded and operated by Wayne State University, with replication sites supported through NSF funding.
* [Math Flash](math-flash.md) - A computer-assisted mathematics practice program that presents addition and subtraction facts for timed retrieval practice with visual representations, adaptive pacing, corrective repetition, feedback, and rewards.
* [Math Garden single-digit multiplication error dataset and 14-category error classification](math-garden-single-digit-multiplication-error-dataset-and-14-category-error-classification.md) - A research dataset and 14-category taxonomy of single-digit multiplication errors collected from the Math Garden computerized arithmetic practice environment and analyzed by Savi et al.
* [Math Mysteries](math-mysteries.md) - A teacher-developed, AI-powered, storytelling-based gamified mathematics learning platform for middle school students, created using Google AI Studio by the Song et al.
* [Math with Matt](math-with-matt.md) - Math with Matt is a CTAT-based intelligent tutoring system developed by Rief et al.
* [Math-gender Misconceptions Questionnaire short version (MGMQ-s)](math-gender-misconceptions-questionnaire-short-version-mgmq-s.md) - A short self-report questionnaire developed by Dersch, Thacker, and Eitel that measures in-service and student teachers' empathizing-systemizing and girls' compensation misconceptions using agreement and certainty responses.
* [Mathbot](mathbot.md) - Mathbot is an AI-based personalized elementary mathematics learning platform developed to adapt instruction and feedback to individual learners while incorporating students' interests into fraction problems.
* [Mathematica's M&E approach and associated toolkit](mathematicas-me-approach-and-associated-toolkit.md) - A Mathematica framework and toolkit that structures evidence gathering and decision-making across four phases of evidence-based solution development.
* [Mathematical Equivalence Assessment (MEA), 15-item adapted instrument](mathematical-equivalence-assessment-mea-15-item-adapted-instrument.md) - A 15-item adapted assessment developed by Şimşek et al.
* [Mathematical Imagery Trainer for Proportion](mathematical-imagery-trainer-for-proportion.md) - An embodied mathematics-learning environment developed by Dor Abrahamson and colleagues in which learners manipulate on-screen cursors to construct proportional relationships through staged visual and numerical overlays.
* [Mathematics Diagnostic Testing Project (MDTP)](mathematics-diagnostic-testing-project-mdtp.md) - MDTP is a mathematics diagnostic assessment project that provides topic-level mastery information for students and educators, including online results used to inform algebra placement decisions.
* [Mathematics-Related Beliefs Questionnaire](mathematics-related-beliefs-questionnaire.md) - The MRBQ is a 58-item, six-point Likert-scale questionnaire developed by Op ’t Eynde and colleagues to measure students’ beliefs about mathematics, themselves as mathematics learners, and their classroom social context.
* [MATHia](mathia.md) - MATHia is Carnegie Learning's adaptive mathematics intelligent tutoring system that analyzes students' step-by-step problem solving and provides personalized instruction and feedback toward mastery.
* [MAWSQ: Metacognitive Academic Writing Strategies Questionnaire](mawsq-metacognitive-academic-writing-strategies-questionnaire.md) - A 43-item, 7-point Likert self-report questionnaire developed by Teng and colleagues to assess EFL university students' metacognitive knowledge and regulation strategies in academic writing.
* [MCD-v2](mcd-v2.md) - MCD-v2 is a computerized adaptive cognitive diagnostic test developed by Vy Le et al.
* [McKinney-Vento Act](mckinney-vento-act.md) - A U.S.
* [MDTP Algebra Readiness test](mdtp-algebra-readiness-test.md) - A 45-item multiple-choice mathematics diagnostic assessment developed by the Mathematics Diagnostic Testing Project to measure students' algebra readiness across seven topic areas.
* [Measure C and the Oakland Children's Initiative](measure-c-and-the-oakland-childrens-initiative.md) - County and city early-childhood and education funding initiatives administered or implemented by First 5 Alameda and Oakland Promise to expand early learning access, improve provider conditions, support school readiness, and promote college persistence.
* [Measurement battery: Adaptability Scale–Short, ICAR Letter and Number Series, and MES-UC motivation/engagement items](measurement-battery-adaptability-scaleshort-icar-letter-and-number-series-and-mes-uc-motiv.md) - An online questionnaire battery assembled by Martin Andrew J.
* [Med2Lab](med2lab.md) - Med2Lab is a platform used in medical education to submit case vignettes, open-ended questions, rubrics, and deidentified student responses to GPT-4 for automated scoring and feedback.
* [MedEasy](medeasy.md) - MedEasy is a web-based multi-agent virtual standardized-patient platform developed by Zhiqi Gao et al.
* [MedGame Bench](medgame-bench.md) - A 5,000-case medical narrative-generation and story-direction benchmark created by Qian Wu et al.
* [MedGame dual-engine framework](medgame-dual-engine-framework.md) - A Qian Wu et al.
* [MEDS dataset of 28,000 LLM runs across four mathematics-education tasks](meds-dataset-of-28000-llm-runs-across-four-mathematics-education-tasks.md) - A publicly released dataset by Esposito et al.
* [MeduAI-SP](meduai-sp.md) - MeduAI-SP is a web-based multi-agent LLM standardized-patient training platform developed by Yang et al.
* [MEL-TS: a translingual set of mental effort and mental load self-rating items in six languages](mel-ts-a-translingual-set-of-mental-effort-and-mental-load-self-rating-items-in-six-langua.md) - A multilingual self-report instrument developed by Tino Endres et al.
* [Mellon Research Forum initiative on the value of a liberal arts education](mellon-research-forum-initiative-on-the-value-of-a-liberal-arts-education.md) - A Mellon Foundation research initiative that supports and organizes scholarly inquiry into the multiple outcomes of a liberal arts education.
* [Memdora](memdora.md) - Memdora is a cross-platform AI spaced-repetition software system developed and described by Ruiyang Zhang, offering browser-based capture, AI-generated study cards, and FSRS-6 scheduling for long-term retention.
* [MEME (Modeling and Evidence Mapping Environment)](meme-modeling-and-evidence-mapping-environment.md) - A networked collaborative modeling environment developed by Zhou et al.
* [Mentira](mentira.md) - Mentira is a place-based mobile game developed by Holden and Sykes that teaches Spanish sociopragmatic language use through collaborative crime-mystery interactions and contextual corrective feedback.
* [Mentor Tech](mentor-tech.md) - Mentor Tech is a Texas Tech University programme that supports underrepresented students through matched faculty mentoring, workshops, community connections, and scholarships.
* [Menu of Measures](menu-of-measures.md) - A curated resource developed by Mathematica and a panel of mathematics-instruction and measurement researchers for Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation grantees, listing valid, reliable, and accessible measures of middle-grades mathematics outcomes.
* [Metacognitive Awareness of Reading Strategies Inventory](metacognitive-awareness-of-reading-strategies-inventory.md) - MARSI is a self-report assessment developed by Mokhtari and Reichard to measure learners' awareness and use of global, problem-solving, and support reading strategies.
* [Metacognitive Interview Form for Younger and Older Students](metacognitive-interview-form-for-younger-and-older-students.md) - A semi-structured interview assessment developed by the North Central Regional Educational Laboratory (NCREL) to measure younger and older students' metacognitive reading-strategy use before, during, and after reading.
* [Metacognitive Interview Form for Younger Students](metacognitive-interview-form-for-younger-students.md) - An informal, semi-structured NCREL reading assessment interview that elicits younger students’ awareness and use of metacognitive strategies before, during, and after reading.
* [Metafora system](metafora-system.md) - Metafora is a computer-supported collaborative-learning system developed by Schwarz and colleagues that combines shared construction tools with an argumentation space for group inquiry, reflection, planning, and peer assessment.
* [MetaTutor](metatutor.md) - MetaTutor is a hypermedia-based intelligent tutoring system developed by educational technology researchers that uses pedagogical agents and self-regulated-learning tools to support learners studying scientific content.
* [MI Write](mi-write.md) - MI Write is Measurement Incorporated's automated writing assessment and feedback software used by schools to support writing instruction and student revision.
* [MiCareer Resource Hub](micareer-resource-hub.md) - MiCareer Resource Hub is a Michigan Consortium for Addressing Rural Education Expansion and Retention partnership initiative that connects educator-preparation institutions and rural school districts to help prospective and experienced educators earn teaching credentials and classroom experience.
* [Michigan Multi-Tiered System of Supports](michigan-multi-tiered-system-of-supports.md) - A framework developed and supported by the Michigan Multi-Tiered System of Supports Technical Assistance Center for designing and implementing Tier 2 academic and behavioral supports, including reading interventions.
* [Michigan's Great Start Readiness Program (GSRP)](michigans-great-start-readiness-program-gsrp.md) - A state-funded preschool programme operated by Michigan for four-year-old children with factors placing them at risk of educational failure, using specified quality standards, staffing requirements, and research-validated curricula.
* [Microanalytic SRL assessment tool with open-ended and closed-ended questions across the three SRL phases](microanalytic-srl-assessment-tool-with-open-ended-and-closed-ended-questions-across-the-th.md) - A study-developed assessment instrument by Laura Dörrenbächer-Ulrich and colleagues that measures college students’ metacognitive and motivational strategy use before, during, and after a learning task through open- and closed-ended questions.
* [MicroDYN](microdyn.md) - MicroDYN is Joachim Funke's scalable assessment system using linear structural-equation items to measure learners' exploration, mental-model externalization, and control of dynamic systems.
* [Milpitas Early Learning Transitions Model](milpitas-early-learning-transitions-model.md) - A Milpitas Unified School District programme funded by the Heising-Simons Foundation to strengthen supports for preschool children and families preparing for elementary school.
* [Mindfulness: A Practical Guide to Finding Peace in a Frantic World (M-FP)](mindfulness-a-practical-guide-to-finding-peace-in-a-frantic-world-m-fp.md) - An introductory, skills-based mindfulness curriculum developed from mindfulness-based cognitive therapy that sequences four themes across eight sessions and includes daily home practice for general adult learners.
* [MindMatters school-based mental health promotion programme](mindmatters-school-based-mental-health-promotion-programme.md) - MindMatters is a comprehensive whole-school mental-health and resilience promotion programme originally developed in Australia and implemented in Swiss schools by Radix.
* [Mindset Kit](mindset-kit.md) - Mindset Kit is a free, research-informed set of growth-mindset teaching and intervention materials developed by the Project for Education Research That Scales (PERTS) for educators and institutions.
* [Mindsets mathematics program](mindsets-mathematics-program.md) - Mindsets is a middle-school mathematics curriculum program built around collaborative, hands-on, challenge-based tasks aligned with the Standards for Mathematical Practice and supported by teacher professional development and implementation assistance.
* [Minnesota North Star Promise Scholarship, NSP+, and Minnesota State Grant](minnesota-north-star-promise-scholarship-nsp-and-minnesota-state-grant.md) - Minnesota state postsecondary financial-aid programs that provide tuition and need-based support to eligible residents, including eligible undocumented students, administered by the state.
* [Minority Teachers of Illinois scholarship program](minority-teachers-of-illinois-scholarship-program.md) - A state-run scholarship program that supports students of color enrolled in Illinois teacher-licensure programs to recruit a more racially diverse teaching workforce.
* [Mirenda's four-aspect framework for classroom participation](mirendas-four-aspect-framework-for-classroom-participation.md) - A framework devised by Mirenda for teachers to assess a child's classroom participation across integration, academic participation, independence, and social participation.
* [MISE](mise.md) - MISE's nine-component systemic reform strategy is a district-level framework for improving science teaching through coordinated leadership, curriculum, assessment, professional development, and instructional-support systems.
* [Missouri Teacher Career Ladder Program](missouri-teacher-career-ladder-program.md) - A Missouri state teacher-compensation programme that provides bonuses for specified additional professional work and responsibilities.
* [Missouri teacher equity plan](missouri-teacher-equity-plan.md) - Missouri's teacher equity plan is a state-run programme framework, developed and implemented by the Missouri Department of Elementary and Secondary Education, that uses shortage prediction to target recruitment and preparation in high-need areas.
* [MITCA method](mitca-method.md) - A homework-implementation framework developed by Vieites and colleagues that specifies five conditions—Varied, Specific, Worthwhile, Weekly, and Evaluated—for using primary-school homework to support self-regulated learning.
* [Mixture Achievement Test (MAT)](mixture-achievement-test-mat.md) - A 17-item multiple-choice chemistry achievement test developed by Kingir (2013) to assess ninth-grade students' understanding of mixtures, including classification, solutions, solubility, and separation.
* [MoBa genotyped mother-father-child trio sample (N = 40,879)](moba-genotyped-mother-father-child-trio-sample-n-40879.md) - A restricted-access Norwegian Mother, Father and Child Cohort Study dataset containing genotype data for 40,879 mother-father-child trios, administered by the Norwegian Institute of Public Health for research on intergenerational and child-development outcomes.
* [Mobl21](mobl21.md) - Mobl21 is a mobile learning platform that teachers use to create and manage multimedia study materials, while students use it for note-taking, self-paced review, tests, and quizzes.
* [Model of Essential Supports for Student Learning](model-of-essential-supports-for-student-learning.md) - A school-improvement framework developed by the Consortium on Chicago School Research that organizes seven organizational capacities and practices intended to support ambitious student learning.
* [Modern Classroom Project](modern-classroom-project.md) - The Modern Classroom Project is a teacher-created instructional model run by the Modern Classroom Project organization for self-paced, competency-based classroom instruction.
* [Modified 5E lesson design planning template](modified-5e-lesson-design-planning-template.md) - This is a lesson-planning framework adapted by WestEd's K–12 Alliance for the Montebello project, modifying the 5E Instructional Model with concept, teacher, and student columns and embedded evaluation.
* [Modified Intermodal Preferential Looking Paradigm (IPLP) word-learning task with novel word/object pairings](modified-intermodal-preferential-looking-paradigm-iplp-word-learning-task-with-novel-wordo.md) - A researcher-modified preferential-looking assessment developed and used by Houston et al.
* [MoFaCTS](mofacts.md) - MoFaCTS is an online drill-trial problem-authoring and deployment platform that administers instructional and assessment activities, scores responses, and delivers corrective feedback.
* [MoMa (Motivation in Mathematics)](moma-motivation-in-mathematics.md) - A 90-minute classroom-based mathematics relevance and motivation intervention developed by Gaspard et al.
* [Montessori education system](montessori-education-system.md) - An educational programme developed by Maria Montessori that uses mixed-age groupings, prepared environments, specialized materials, and trained teachers to support self-directed learning from birth through adolescence.
* [Montevidisco](montevidisco.md) - An interactive video-based Spanish-language conversational simulation created by Edward Schneider and Junius Bennion in which learners navigate branching encounters in a Mexican village and record spoken responses for evaluation.
* [MOOC Replication Framework](mooc-replication-framework.md) - MORF, described by Gardner (2018), is a framework that supports controlled, privacy-preserving replication of predictive analyses across MOOC studies, courses, and datasets.
* [Moodle Workshop module](moodle-workshop-module.md) - Moodle's Workshop activity module supports the distribution, anonymous peer assessment, and teacher assessment of learner submissions using defined assessment criteria and grading schemes.
* [Morphology+](morphology.md) - IMSE's Morphology+ is a structured literacy curriculum and educator-training program developed by the Institute for Multisensory Education for grades 3–5 morphology, vocabulary, and comprehension instruction.
* [MotiBo](motibo.md) - MotiBo is an interactive digital storytelling system designed by Fung et al.
* [Motivated Strategies for Learning Questionnaire (MSLQ)](motivated-strategies-for-learning-questionnaire-mslq.md) - The MSLQ is Pintrich and colleagues' self-report assessment of students' motivation and learning-strategy use, comprising motivation and self-regulated-learning scales for college and high-school learners.
* [Motivation and Engagement Scale (MES)](motivation-and-engagement-scale-mes.md) - A 44-item self-report assessment developed by Andrew J.
* [Motivation for Incidental Vocabulary Learning Scale (MIVLS), Incidental Vocabulary Learning Anxiety Scale (IVLAS), and Mastery of Incidental Vocabulary Learning Strategies Scale (MIVLSS)](motivation-for-incidental-vocabulary-learning-scale-mivls-incidental-vocabulary-learning-a.md) - A set of three Zhao et al.
* [Motivational Framework for Culturally Responsive Teaching (MFCRT)](motivational-framework-for-culturally-responsive-teaching-mfcrt.md) - A framework developed by Wlodkowski and Ginsberg for designing culturally responsive instruction through the motivational conditions of inclusion, attitude, meaning, and competence.
* [MPathic-VR virtual human conversation simulation](mpathic-vr-virtual-human-conversation-simulation.md) - MPathic-VR is a computer-based virtual-human conversation simulation for healthcare learners, developed for practicing and assessing advanced intercultural and interprofessional communication skills.
* [Mr. Vetro simulation framework](mr-vetro-simulation-framework.md) - A student-facing, Agentsheets-based collaborative cardiovascular-system simulation developed by Gerry Stahl and colleagues for K–12 students to explore physiological parameters and complex-system causality.
* [MRIM scale](mrim-scale.md) - A 19-item, 6-point Likert self-report assessment developed by Chenghui Chen for measuring planning, monitoring, and evaluating of interactive metadiscourse strategies in L2 argumentative writing among first-year non-English major university EFL students.
* [MSCEIT-YV Emotion Regulation Ability subtest](msceit-yv-emotion-regulation-ability-subtest.md) - A standardized youth assessment subtest that measures adolescents’ ability to judge the effectiveness of alternative actions for achieving emotion-regulation goals, developed as part of the Mayer, Salovey, and Caruso Emotional Intelligence Test—Youth Version.
* [Multi-measure metacognition assessment paradigm combining MAI, confidence judgments, and trial-by-trial AUROC2](multi-measure-metacognition-assessment-paradigm-combining-mai-confidence-judgments-and-tri.md) - A study-developed assessment paradigm by Terneusen et al.
* [Multi-Payer School-Linked Fee Schedule](multi-payer-school-linked-fee-schedule.md) - A California optional programme administered through Carelon that enables enrolled local educational agencies and designated school-linked providers to obtain reimbursement from commercial and Medi-Cal managed care plans for student mental health and substance use disorder services.
* [Multi-scale portfolio of 14 adoption-ready computing PjBL project packages](multi-scale-portfolio-of-14-adoption-ready-computing-pjbl-project-packages.md) - A portfolio developed by Suleiman et al.
* [Multi-season math and reading score dataset for over seven million K-8 students (2016-17)](multi-season-math-and-reading-score-dataset-for-over-seven-million-k-8-students-2016-17.md) - A dataset of NWEA MAP Growth mathematics and reading scores collected in fall, winter, and spring of  the 2016–17 school year for more than seven million kindergarten through grade 8 students.
* [Multi-site longitudinal student-level charter school dataset covering eight states and cities](multi-site-longitudinal-student-level-charter-school-dataset-covering-eight-states-and-cit.md) - A longitudinal student-level dataset assembled by Ron Zimmer and colleagues covering charter-school students in Chicago, Denver, Milwaukee, Philadelphia, San Diego, Florida, Ohio, and Texas for comparative charter-school research.
* [Multi-strategy test (MST)](multi-strategy-test-mst.md) - The Multi-strategy test (MST) is an open-response, scenario-based assessment instrument developed by Schuster et al.
* [Multi-Tier Systems of Support (MTSS)](multi-tier-systems-of-support-mtss.md) - Multi-Tier Systems of Support is a tiered framework that schools, districts, and state education agencies use to coordinate academic, behavioral, and social-emotional supports for all students.
* [Multidisciplinary project dataset on signaling in multimedia learning with up to 400 participants from 15 classes](multidisciplinary-project-dataset-on-signaling-in-multimedia-learning-with-up-to-400-parti.md) - A research dataset from a multidisciplinary multimedia-learning project involving up to 400 students in 15 classes, with measures of prior knowledge, learning outcomes, ratings, usability, log data, and delayed retention.
* [Multilingual Assessment Instrument of Narratives (MAIN)](multilingual-assessment-instrument-of-narratives-main.md) - MAIN is a multilingual narrative assessment developed by an international research team to evaluate children's narrative production and comprehension through story-telling, story-retelling, or model-story tasks.
* [Multimodal mathematics item-response corpus: 970 item models, 4,848 items, 13.88 million responses](multimodal-mathematics-item-response-corpus-970-item-models-4848-items-1388-million-respon.md) - A corpus of multimodal multiple-choice mathematics items and 13.88 million student responses created by Christopher Ormerod et al.
* [Multimodal OBE examination-answer dataset of 485 scanned submissions with criterion-level rubric marks](multimodal-obe-examination-answer-dataset-of-485-scanned-submissions-with-criterion-level.md) - A restricted research dataset created by Jahangir Alam SM et al.
* [Multiple Episode Protocol Analysis (MEPA)](multiple-episode-protocol-analysis-mepa.md) - A computer program developed by Erkens et al.
* [Multiple-Text Strategy Inventory (MTSI)](multiple-text-strategy-inventory-mtsi.md) - A 15-item questionnaire developed by Ivar Bråten and colleagues to assess undergraduates' self-reported accumulation and cross-text elaboration strategies immediately after reading multiple texts.
* [MUSE: a 12-task benchmark of artistic image understanding for situated education](muse-a-12-task-benchmark-of-artistic-image-understanding-for-situated-education.md) - MUSE is a benchmark dataset developed by Luyao Zhu et al.
* [MuTSE](mutse.md) - MuTSE is a web application developed by Rares-Alexandru Roșcan et al.
* [My Math Academy](my-math-academy.md) - My Math Academy is a game-based adaptive mathematics learning platform for pre-K through grade 2, developed and operated by Age of Learning.
* [My Reading Academy](my-reading-academy.md) - My Reading Academy is an adaptive game-based foundational reading curriculum for pre-K through grade 2, providing phonemic-awareness, phonics, vocabulary, fluency, and comprehension instruction.
* [My School, My Voice survey](my-school-my-voice-survey.md) - CCSR's My School, My Voice product combines teacher and student school-climate survey responses into 31 school-level measures with comparative trend reports and item-level details.
* [MyDigitalHand (MDH)](mydigitalhand-mdh.md) - MyDigitalHand is a ticketing platform that manages student help requests and teaching-staff interactions during office hours, as used in the studied CS2 course.
* [myHeggerty](myheggerty.md) - myHeggerty is Heggerty's digital platform providing optional resources for teachers' planning, professional learning, and classroom instruction.
* [myPLACE Online](myplace-online.md) - A Philadelphia Mayor's Commission on Literacy online cohort learning programme and shared electronic registration platform for low-literate adult learners, used across the Commission's partner agencies.
* [MyTeachingPartner-Secondary (MTP-S)](myteachingpartner-secondary-mtp-s.md) - MyTeachingPartner–Secondary is a University of Virginia Center for Advanced Study of Teaching and Learning professional-development programme that combines a video library of effective teacher–student interactions with individualized web-based coaching for secondary teachers.

#### N {: #letter-n }

* [NADL (Numerical Activities of Daily Living) standardized battery](nadl-numerical-activities-of-daily-living-standardized-battery.md) - A standardized battery developed by Vigna Giulia et al.
* [NAO humanoid robot with Choregraphe programming environment](nao-humanoid-robot-with-choregraphe-programming-environment.md) - SoftBank Robotics' NAO humanoid robot and Choregraphe visual programming environment enable developers to create multimodal behaviors using voice, vision, touch, and movement for educational and other applications.

* [National Education Longitudinal Survey (NELS)](national-education-longitudinal-survey-nels.md) - A nationally representative longitudinal education dataset collected and maintained by the National Center for Education Statistics, tracking students from eighth grade through postsecondary outcomes.
* [National Graduate Attributes Project eight-factor systemic framework](national-graduate-attributes-project-eight-factor-systemic-framework.md) - A framework developed by the National Graduate Attributes Project for universities to improve graduate-attribute achievement by addressing eight interacting institutional factors, including assessment, curriculum, staff development, and quality assurance.
* [National Guard Youth ChalleNGe Program](national-guard-youth-challenge-program.md) - A National Guard-run residential programme for youth ages 16–18 who have left or been expelled from high school, combining GED preparation with quasi-military training and leadership, citizenship, service, life-skills, fitness, health, job-skills, and academic components.

* [National Network of Partnership Schools (NNPS)](national-network-of-partnership-schools-nnps.md) - A Johns Hopkins University network that supports schools and districts in developing and sustaining family and community partnership programmes through institutes, workshops, professional-learning series, and facilitation.
* [National Research Council group tests of general intelligence](national-research-council-group-tests-of-general-intelligence.md) - A proposed pair of group-administered general-intelligence test series developed by a National Research Council committee for large-scale school administration and scoring.
* [National Student Clearinghouse (NSC)](national-student-clearinghouse-nsc.md) - National Student Clearinghouse data are a nonprofit repository of postsecondary enrollment, completion, and related records voluntarily contributed by participating institutions and used for education research and reporting.
* [National Study of Online Charter Schools](national-study-of-online-charter-schools.md) - A national research dataset assembled through a survey of online charter-school principals and U.S.
* [National Study of Online Charter Schools principal survey](national-study-of-online-charter-schools-principal-survey.md) - A nationwide survey dataset collected by Mathematica Policy Research from 127 online charter school principals to describe online charter schools' operations and instructional approaches.
* [Nature of Science Scale (NOSS)](nature-of-science-scale-noss.md) - The Nature of Science Scale (NOSS), developed by Özgelen (2013), is a 30-item instrument for measuring views of the nature of science among learners and teachers.
* [NBA Math Hoops](nba-math-hoops.md) - NBA Math Hoops is a Learn Fresh programme developed in partnership with the NBA and WNBA that uses an interactive basketball board game, real player statistics, and supporting math lessons to teach elementary and middle-grade mathematics.
* [NCES restricted-use individual-level dataset of seven million US student observations across 160 survey waves](nces-restricted-use-individual-level-dataset-of-seven-million-us-student-observations-acro.md) - A restricted-use NCES dataset combining approximately seven million US student observations from 160 psychometrically linked mathematics and reading survey waves for longitudinal achievement analysis.
* [NCLB eleven-component comprehensive school reform framework](nclb-eleven-component-comprehensive-school-reform-framework.md) - A framework specified by the No Child Left Behind Act that organizes comprehensive K–12 school reform around eleven coordinated components, including evidence-based methods, professional development, measurable goals, family and community involvement, external assistance, evaluation, and resource co
* [NCS capacity-building model](ncs-capacity-building-model.md) - A whole-school improvement framework developed and implemented by the Network for College Success to build school staff capacity through professional learning networks, data use, coaching, and distributed leadership.
* [NCTE Transcript dataset](ncte-transcript-dataset.md) - A publicly available dataset of U.S.
* [Nebraska's AQuESTT Dual State Accountability System](nebraskas-aquestt-dual-state-accountability-system.md) - A state accountability framework operated by the Nebraska Department of Education alongside the federal ESSA accountability system to evaluate and guide Nebraska schools and districts.
* [Nemo Explain Visualizer (nev)](nemo-explain-visualizer-nev.md) - Nemo Explain Visualizer is a free, open-source web-based visual explanation and debugging tool developed for the Nemo Datalog reasoner, with interactive trace, tree, and fact-table views.
* [Net.Create](netcreate.md) - Net.Create is a network-visualization platform developed by Craig and colleagues that enables students to co-construct representations of historical events and relationships for collaborative reasoning.
* [Networks for School Improvement (NSI)](networks-for-school-improvement-nsi.md) - A Gates Foundation programme that supports networks of schools in using continuous improvement to improve outcomes for students who are Black, Latino, or experiencing poverty.
* [Neurone](neurone.md) - Neurone is a web-based platform that supports online inquiry by helping students search texts, select and contextualize snippets, evaluate sources, and compose integrated writing.
* [NeuroRacer](neuroracer.md) - NeuroRacer is a custom-designed three-dimensional video game developed by Anguera and colleagues that combines perceptual discrimination and visuomotor driving tasks for cognitive-control assessment and training.
* [Nevada MRI menu of more rigorous interventions for CSI schools](nevada-mri-menu-of-more-rigorous-interventions-for-csi-schools.md) - The Nevada Department of Education, with Region 15 Comprehensive Center support, developed this evidence-based menu to help LEAs and Comprehensive Support and Improvement schools identify and implement interventions addressing persistent low or stagnant academic performance.
* [Nevada's GRAD score](nevadas-grad-score.md) - A machine-learning-generated student at-risk indicator run through Nevada's Infinite Campus system and used by the Nevada Department of Education in its school funding formula.
* [New Chance](new-chance.md) - New Chance is a two-phase education-and-employment programme developed and implemented by MDRC and partner sites for young welfare mothers who had dropped out of school.
* [New Hampshire Teacher Candidate Assessment of Performance (NH TCAP)](new-hampshire-teacher-candidate-assessment-of-performance-nh-tcap.md) - A teacher-candidate performance assessment adapted from the Performance Assessment for California Teachers and implemented by a consortium of New Hampshire higher-education institutions to support candidate learning and assess readiness to teach.
* [New Heights](new-heights.md) - A school-based support programme for expectant and parenting teens, evaluated in Washington, DC, to improve students’ educational outcomes.
* [New Jersey statewide principal evaluation system](new-jersey-statewide-principal-evaluation-system.md) - New Jersey's statewide system for evaluating principals, combining principal-practice and leadership instruments with measures of student achievement, implemented statewide beginning in 2013/14.
* [New Leaders](new-leaders.md) - New Leaders runs a selective principal-residency and ongoing support programme that prepares and develops school leaders, particularly for high-need schools.
* [New Leaders for New Schools (NLNS)](new-leaders-for-new-schools-nlns.md) - A leadership-development programme run by New Leaders for New Schools that prepares and supports urban public-school principals through intensive training, a mentored residency, seminars, and leadership coaching.
* [New Mexico Community Schools Act grant program](new-mexico-community-schools-act-grant-program.md) - A New Mexico Public Education Department-administered grant programme that funds community-school coordinators, planning, implementation, and technical assistance for participating public schools.
* [New paradigm measuring exploratory curiosity in preschool children as threshold of desired uncertainty](new-paradigm-measuring-exploratory-curiosity-in-preschool-children-as-threshold-of-desired.md) - A behavioral assessment paradigm developed by Jirout et al.
* [New Teacher Center](new-teacher-center.md) - A New Teacher Center framework for districtwide instructional-coaching implementation that combines tracking coaching activities, guiding coaches, and engaging district leaders.
* [New York City middle school student safety survey panel dataset](new-york-city-middle-school-student-safety-survey-panel-dataset.md) - A panel dataset compiled from New York City Department of Education middle-school student safety surveys, containing annual safety perceptions for more than 340,000 students across more than 700 schools from 2007 to 2010.
* [New York's APPR teacher and principal evaluation system](new-yorks-appr-teacher-and-principal-evaluation-system.md) - New York's Annual Professional Performance Review (APPR) is a statewide educator-evaluation framework established by New York education officials and the teachers union that combines student-growth measures with classroom observations.
* [Newly developed SRL strategy knowledge test with 25 paired strategy comparisons](newly-developed-srl-strategy-knowledge-test-with-25-paired-strategy-comparisons.md) - A 25-paired-comparison assessment developed by Laura Dörrenbächer-Ulrich et al.
* [Newly developed three-item External Mandate scale](newly-developed-three-item-external-mandate-scale.md) - A three-item self-report scale developed by Li et al.
* [Newton's Playground](newtons-playground.md) - A research-developed 2D educational physics video game from the Florida State University research team in which learners draw simple machines to solve qualitative Newtonian-physics puzzles.
* [Next Generation School Accountability initiative](next-generation-school-accountability-initiative.md) - Utah's state school-accountability framework, administered by the Utah State Board of Education, that includes growth and equity indicators such as the Lowest Quartile measure.
* [NextGen Educators Program](nextgen-educators-program.md) - A Connecticut educator-pipeline programme run through a partnership between the state education department and Central Connecticut State University that places teacher candidates in K–12 classrooms with professional mentors.
* [NFTE entrepreneurship courses](nfte-entrepreneurship-courses.md) - A semester- or year-long entrepreneurship curriculum developed and delivered by the Network for Teaching Entrepreneurship (NFTE), culminating in student business plans, presentations, and competitions.
* [NFTE entrepreneurship education program](nfte-entrepreneurship-education-program.md) - NFTE's entrepreneurship education program, developed and run by the Network for Teaching Entrepreneurship, uses project-based and experiential instruction to develop entrepreneurial-mindset skills alongside small-business-management knowledge.
* [NFTE Startup Summer](nfte-startup-summer.md) - NFTE's Startup Summer is an intensive business-startup programme that supports selected high-school students from under-resourced communities through boot camp, coaching, funding, and a public pitch event.
* [NGSS District Implementation Guide](ngss-district-implementation-guide.md) - A guide developed by Achieve to help education leaders plan, support, and sustain implementation of the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS).
* [NGSS Implementation Leadership Framework](ngss-implementation-leadership-framework.md) - A framework for guiding leaders and leadership development in implementing the Next Generation Science Standards, developed for the NGSS education community.
* [NGSS-aligned middle school and high school science benchmarks](ngss-aligned-middle-school-and-high-school-science-benchmarks.md) - Two publicly released synthetic benchmark datasets created by Schroeder et al.
* [NIH Intramural Loan Repayment Program](nih-intramural-loan-repayment-program.md) - A set of National Institutes of Health loan-repayment programmes run by NIH to attract and retain biomedical researchers in its intramural laboratories and clinics.
* [NIH Undergraduate Scholarship Program](nih-undergraduate-scholarship-program.md) - A scholarship programme run by the National Institutes of Health that supports talented undergraduate science students from disadvantaged backgrounds and encourages biomedical research careers.
* [Nine tool innovation tasks spanning three paradigms and three tool types](nine-tool-innovation-tasks-spanning-three-paradigms-and-three-tool-types.md) - A validated nine-task battery developed by Guo et al.
* [Non-Language Mental Test](non-language-mental-test.md) - A group intelligence test developed by Pintner and colleagues for school children from grade II upward, designed to assess mental ability while minimizing reading and language demands and to support fairer comparisons for non-English-speaking and deaf children.
* [Non-visual story-based behavioral ToM task](non-visual-story-based-behavioral-tom-task.md) - A publicly available non-visual, story-based theory-of-mind assessment developed by Richardson et al.
* [North Carolina Generative AI Implementation Recommendations](north-carolina-generative-ai-implementation-recommendations.md) - A framework issued by North Carolina to guide K–12 generative-AI implementation, including shared levels and disclosure recommendations for AI-assisted assessment work.
* [North Lawndale Cluster Initiative (NLCI)](north-lawndale-cluster-initiative-nlci.md) - AUSL's foundation-funded initiative coordinated schools, pre-k, professional development, mentoring, social-emotional supports, transition services, and college counseling across a North Lawndale school cluster from 2013–14 through 2018–19.
* [Northside Achievement Zone (NAZ)](northside-achievement-zone-naz.md) - A place-based Promise Neighborhood initiative serving a defined area of North Minneapolis through coordinated early-childhood, education, and family-support services.
* [Novel data set linking fight participants for discipline disparity analysis](novel-data-set-linking-fight-participants-for-discipline-disparity-analysis.md) - A research dataset constructed by Nathan Barrett et al.
* [NSF proposal analytic dataset](nsf-proposal-analytic-dataset.md) - An analytic dataset assembled by Fesler et al.
* [NSMQ Riddles](nsmq-riddles.md) - A benchmark dataset created by George Boateng and colleagues containing 1,840 scientific and mathematical riddles from Ghana's National Science and Maths Quiz for evaluating automated question-answering systems.
* [NTool](ntool.md) - NTool is an online, newsgroup-reader-based asynchronous communication tool developed by Pieter J.
* [Number Rockets](number-rockets.md) - Number Rockets is a scripted Tier 2 small-group mathematics tutoring program for first-grade students struggling in math, delivered by tutors in supplemental 40-minute sessions.
* [Number Sets Test](number-sets-test.md) - A speeded assessment developed by Moore et al.
* [Number system knowledge test (32 items)](number-system-knowledge-test-32-items.md) - A 32-item oral and written assessment developed by Cowan et al.
* [NWEA COVID-19 MAP Growth data visualization](nwea-covid-19-map-growth-data-visualization.md) - An interactive NWEA Research dashboard that visualizes state-level MAP Growth achievement and projected proficiency data for the 2020–2021 school year to inform understanding of pandemic-related learning impacts and recovery.
* [NWEA MAP Growth 2022–23 achievement dataset](nwea-map-growth-202223-achievement-dataset.md) - A dataset assembled by NWEA from MAP Growth assessments for approximately 6.7 million U.S.
* [NWEA MAP Growth assessment and longitudinal achievement database](nwea-map-growth-assessment-and-longitudinal-achievement-database.md) - A longitudinal database of student achievement and growth scores collected through NWEA's MAP Growth assessments and used for large-scale educational analysis.
* [NWEA professional learning modules](nwea-professional-learning-modules.md) - NWEA's professional-learning module suite trains educators to use MAP Growth reports, interpret assessment information, set growth goals, and apply broader formative-assessment practices.
* [NWEA science achievement trend analysis dataset](nwea-science-achievement-trend-analysis-dataset.md) - A dataset and technical brief produced by NWEA researchers containing MAP Growth science achievement and growth trends across seven school years to examine pandemic-era learning patterns.
* [NWEA state dashboard](nwea-state-dashboard.md) - A NWEA dashboard that summarizes state-level MAP Growth achievement and growth trends and related COVID-19 learning-impact analyses for education stakeholders.

#### O {: #letter-o }

* [OASIS-based AI Anxiety Scale (5-item, Chinese-adapted)](oasis-based-ai-anxiety-scale-5-item-chinese-adapted.md) - A five-item Chinese-adapted self-report scale developed by Duan et al.
* [OATutor (Open Adaptive Tutor)](oatutor-open-adaptive-tutor.md) - OATutor is an open-source adaptive tutoring platform developed by the OATutor Project for delivering online practice problems, learner hints, and instructional interaction data.
