* [Occupational Aspiration Scale](occupational-aspiration-scale.md) - A self-report instrument developed by Haller et al.
* [Ocsai and confidence Python libraries for confidence-aware LLM scoring](ocsai-and-confidence-python-libraries-for-confidence-aware-llm-scoring.md) - Python libraries released by Organisciak et al.
* [Odyssey Math](odyssey-math.md) - Odyssey® Math is a web-based K–8 mathematics curriculum and individualized learning platform developed by Compass Learning®, combining diagnostic assessment, formative assessment, and teacher-configurable learning paths.
* [OER Evidence Hub and OER Knowledge Cloud](oer-evidence-hub-and-oer-knowledge-cloud.md) - The OER Evidence Hub, developed by the Knowledge Media Institute and The Open University's Institute of Educational Technology, and the OER Knowledge Cloud are digital infrastructures for interrogating the open-education movement and collecting, preserving, and disseminating its research documents.
* [OG+](og.md) - IMSE's OG+ is a Structured Literacy foundational-skills curriculum developed by the Institute for Multi-Sensory Education (IMSE) for whole-class, small-group, and one-to-one reading and spelling instruction in grades K–5.
* [OGAP Math](ogap-math.md) - OGAP Math is a professional learning system that supports K–8 mathematics teachers in using research-based learning progressions, student-work analysis, item banks, and frameworks to guide instruction.
* [OGAP Professional Learning](ogap-professional-learning.md) - OGAP Professional Learning is a mathematics professional-development programme run by the Ongoing Assessment Project (OGAP) for educators across developmental reasoning strands.
* [Ogive percentile norms charts for seven tests (Figures I-VII)](ogive-percentile-norms-charts-for-seven-tests-figures-i-vii.md) - Bingham's set of percentile-norm charts for seven Dartmouth freshman mental-ability and performance tests, used to convert test scores into ranks within the norm group.
* [Ohio AI Toolkit](ohio-ai-toolkit.md) - The Ohio Department of Education and Workforce's web-based toolkit guides Ohio policymakers, teachers, and parents through AI-in-education policy development and implementation while curating related resources.
* [Ohio LEAP and California Cal-Learn](ohio-leap-and-california-cal-learn.md) - LEAP, run by Ohio, and Cal-Learn, run by California, are state welfare programs that use attendance, academic-performance, and completion bonuses or sanctions to support teen parents' school participation.
* [Ohio's School Quality and Student Success (SQSS) Accountability System](ohios-school-quality-and-student-success-sqss-accountability-system.md) - Ohio's statewide accountability framework, administered by the Ohio Department of Education and Workforce, includes the Early Literacy Indicator to monitor K–3 literacy outcomes alongside other school-quality and student-success measures.
* [Ohio’s Dyslexia Guidebook](ohios-dyslexia-guidebook.md) - A framework and implementation guide produced by the Ohio Department of Education and Workforce for universal literacy screening, dyslexia screening, and related instructional supports in Ohio schools.
* [Oklahoma Teacher and Leader Effectiveness Evaluation System](oklahoma-teacher-and-leader-effectiveness-evaluation-system.md) - Oklahoma's statewide system for evaluating teachers and school leaders, including value-added measures estimated from student test scores and related teacher and student information.
* [Ollie](ollie.md) - Ollie is a gaze-informed GPT-4o storytelling assistant developed by Wu et al.
* [OmniEdu capability-oriented instruction-tuning corpus](omniedu-capability-oriented-instruction-tuning-corpus.md) - The OmniEdu corpus is a curated, audited, diverse instruction-tuning dataset for education-focused language models, created by Hao Liang et al.
* [On Biodiversity's Tracks](on-biodiversitys-tracks.md) - On Biodiversity's Tracks is a Greenpeace-developed virtual-reality learning application that lets secondary students explore biodiversity and environmental topics in immersive virtual environments.
* [On the Rise Academy](on-the-rise-academy.md) - A Detroit Public Schools Community District alternative teacher-certification and recruitment programme that prepares local community members and school support staff through coaching, observations, and supported school placements.
* [Once Early-Reading Program](once-early-reading-program.md) - Once Early-Reading Program is a school-embedded early-literacy tutoring programme that provides scripted, laptop-delivered one-to-one structured-literacy sessions by trained paraprofessionals for PreK through first-grade students.
* [Online Experimental Psychological System (OPES) computerized cognitive test battery](online-experimental-psychological-system-opes-computerized-cognitive-test-battery.md) - A Web-based assessment system and battery of computerized cognitive and mathematical tasks developed for administering psychological and achievement measures to learners.
* [Online figurative assessment tool measuring six inductive reasoning processes](online-figurative-assessment-tool-measuring-six-inductive-reasoning-processes.md) - A domain-general computer-based figurative test for fifth-grade students that measures six inductive reasoning processes through 54 items in six formats, administered via the eDia platform.
* [OnRamps](onramps.md) - OnRamps is a University of Texas dual-credit programme in which high school teachers deliver UT-designed and supported college-level courses to high school students, who may choose whether to accept the resulting college credit.
* [OnYourMark](onyourmark.md) - OnYourMark is a virtual, individualized, high-dosage early-literacy tutoring programme run by OnYourMark in partnership with schools for kindergarten through grade 2 students.
* [Open audit dataset of 1,800 LLM history-tutor responses across five personas](open-audit-dataset-of-1800-llm-history-tutor-responses-across-five-personas.md) - Alexis Popovici et al.'s open corpus of 1,800 API-generated history-tutor responses across four LLMs, five student personas, and three prompt types for researchers and auditors evaluating educational AI bias.
* [Open CC-BY 4.0 dataset of digital self-efficacy responses (N=243) on Zenodo](open-cc-by-40-dataset-of-digital-self-efficacy-responses-n243-on-zenodo.md) - A fully anonymized dataset of demographic variables and Likert-scale digital self-efficacy responses from 243 secondary students, released with a comprehensive codebook by Nicolas Rodriguez-Alvarez et al.
* [Open Court Reading](open-court-reading.md) - Open Court Reading© is a K–6 comprehensive reading curriculum published by McGraw-Hill Education that provides systematic literacy instruction, decodable texts, anthologies, workbooks, and teacher materials.
* [Open dataset of student-chatbot interaction logs, code snapshots, and knowledge and self-regulation measures](open-dataset-of-student-chatbot-interaction-logs-code-snapshots-and-knowledge-and-self-reg.md) - A supplementary open dataset released by Hyoungwook Jin et al.
* [Open executable module library of six Jupyter notebooks for AI in power systems](open-executable-module-library-of-six-jupyter-notebooks-for-ai-in-power-systems.md) - An open library released by Junjie Yin et al.
* [Open Learning Analytics Platform](open-learning-analytics-platform.md) - An open learning-analytics software platform developed by the article's authors for educational stakeholders to implement, visualize, and integrate learning-analytics indicators.
* [Open Learning Initiative (OLI)](open-learning-initiative-oli.md) - Open Learning Initiative is Carnegie Mellon University's online courseware and learning-platform project, offering openly available and credit-bearing courses with automated instructional and assessment features.
* [Open online dataset and statistical analyses of the mutual-modelling emotions study](open-online-dataset-and-statistical-analyses-of-the-mutual-modelling-emotions-study.md) - A GitHub repository released by Dillenbourg et al.
* [Open Practices Disclosure form](open-practices-disclosure-form.md) - A journal-run disclosure and badge framework that authors use to document public data, materials, and preregistered research plans for recognition of open scientific practices.
* [Open Science Framework analytic script generating and analyzing the simulated data (osf.io/9p83z)](open-science-framework-analytic-script-generating-and-analyzing-the-simulated-data-osfio9p.md) - An R analytic script released by Sorjonen et al.
* [Open Source Physics (OSP) toolkit](open-source-physics-osp-toolkit.md) - A free, open-source physics software toolkit, including Easy Java Simulations and Tracker, developed for creating, customizing, analyzing, and sharing computer models and video-based investigations.
* [Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD)](open-university-learning-analytics-dataset-oulad.md) - OULAD is an open-source dataset maintained for learning-analytics research, containing demographic, virtual learning-environment activity, and performance data from adult learners at The Open University.
* [Open-source adversarial stress-testing platform for RPLAs with FastAPI backend and React dashboard](open-source-adversarial-stress-testing-platform-for-rplas-with-fastapi-backend-and-react-d.md) - An open-source platform developed by Saqib Shouqi et al.
* [Open-source data and scripts for R and Mplus for comparing cross-lagged models](open-source-data-and-scripts-for-r-and-mplus-for-comparing-cross-lagged-models.md) - A reusable release of educational research data, technical notes, and analysis scripts for comparing cross-lagged models, provided by Fernando Núñez-Regueiro and colleagues.
* [Open-source database of SRL constructs and multimodal indicators](open-source-database-of-srl-constructs-and-multimodal-indicators.md) - A Zenodo-hosted database that organizes self-regulated-learning constructs and multimodal indicators from 142 studies for use by SRL researchers and learning-analytics practitioners.
* [Open-source modular AI math tutoring platform](open-source-modular-ai-math-tutoring-platform.md) - An open-source modular platform developed by Jarosław A.
* [Open-source PyTorch DKT implementation](open-source-pytorch-dkt-implementation.md) - An open-source PyTorch implementation created by the article's authors for fitting Deep Knowledge Tracing to DataShop-format data and using it in online knowledge-tracing settings.
* [Opening the Doors to College](opening-the-doors-to-college.md) - A Chicago Public Schools Department of Postsecondary Education framework specifying grade-level steps and recommendations for students preparing for college.
* [OpenLearnLM Benchmark dataset](openlearnlm-benchmark-dataset.md) - OpenLearnLM is an open benchmark dataset created by Unggi Lee et al.
* [Openly available EEG and behavioral datasets and replicable experimental framework for human-AI interaction studies](openly-available-eeg-and-behavioral-datasets-and-replicable-experimental-framework-for-hum.md) - Khurana et al.'s openly released Kaggle datasets contain raw EEG recordings and behavioral ratings from high-school students who completed AI-assisted problem-solving tasks, supporting reuse in human-AI interaction research.
* [Openly released TTX assessment resources](openly-released-ttx-assessment-resources.md) - A publicly licensed bundle from V.
* [Openly shared student and teacher measures from the Social Perspective Taking professional development study](openly-shared-student-and-teacher-measures-from-the-social-perspective-taking-professional.md) - Student and teacher measurement instruments from Hunter Gehlbach et al.'s professional-development study, publicly deposited in OSF for reuse and replication.
* [OpenSciEd](openscied.md) - OpenSciEd is a freely available, Creative Commons–licensed middle-school science curriculum developed to address the Next Generation Science Standards.
* [OpenSciEd lesson-idea dataset of 49 lesson plans and 8820 LLM-simulated student ideas](openscied-lesson-idea-dataset-of-49-lesson-plans-and-8820-llm-simulated-student-ideas.md) - A dataset created by Nguyen et al.
* [Operation Outbreak](operation-outbreak.md) - Operation Outbreak is a hybrid educational disease-transmission simulation programme that uses a Bluetooth-enabled mobile application and live social interaction to help students investigate contagion and public-health concepts.
* [Opiq 6th-grade mathematics interaction dataset from Estonia](opiq-6th-grade-mathematics-interaction-dataset-from-estonia.md) - An interaction-log dataset collected from the Opiq digital learning platform, containing time-ordered 6th-grade mathematics student–item records for educational data-mining and knowledge-tracing research.
* [Opportunity Culture](opportunity-culture.md) - Opportunity Culture is a school redesign and staffing framework developed by Public Impact that uses tiered teaching roles to expand the reach of excellent teachers and strengthen teacher leadership.
* [OPTEL](optel.md) - OPTEL is an observation protocol for teachers of English learners, developed and implemented by the California Department of Education with support from R15CC.
* [Organization Test](organization-test.md) - A standardized sentence-rearrangement assessment developed by Greene for children in grades three through eight to measure logical organization within sentences.
* [Orientation discrimination task](orientation-discrimination-task.md) - A computerized adaptive psychophysical assessment in which children judge relative segment orientation to estimate visual-processing discrimination thresholds.
* [OSF repository](osf-repository.md) - An OSF-hosted replication package from Zambrano et al.
* [Otis Absolute Point Scale (Scale I and Scale II) for group measurement of intelligence](otis-absolute-point-scale-scale-i-and-scale-ii-for-group-measurement-of-intelligence.md) - Arthur S.
* [Otis's absolute grade of intelligence scale with coefficient of brightness](otiss-absolute-grade-of-intelligence-scale-with-coefficient-of-brightness.md) - An intelligence assessment scale proposed by Arthur S.
* [Ottoman Empire face-to-face debate corpus with hand-coded transactivity](ottoman-empire-face-to-face-debate-corpus-with-hand-coded-transactivity.md) - A research dataset of 76 recorded dyadic debates by male undergraduates on the causes of the Ottoman Empire's decline, with transcribed clause-level speech and hand-coded transactivity annotations, collected by Gweon et al.
* [Oxford COVID-19 Government Response Tracker (OxCGRT)](oxford-covid-19-government-response-tracker-oxcgrt.md) - A publicly available dataset produced by the University of Oxford that records daily government COVID-19 policy responses, including stringency and school-closure measures, across countries.

#### P {: #letter-p }

* [Paas 9-point mental effort rating scale](paas-9-point-mental-effort-rating-scale.md) - A single-item nine-point subjective rating scale developed by Paas for measuring perceived mental effort invested in completing a learning or problem-solving task.
* [Paired self-report and objective-based AI literacy assessment instruments for K–12 teachers](paired-self-report-and-objective-based-ai-literacy-assessment-instruments-for-k12-teachers.md) - A psychometrically validated scenario-based multiple-choice test and Likert self-report scale developed to measure K–12 teachers' AI literacy across knowledge, application, evaluation and creation, and ethics.
* [Paired synthetic evaluation datasets: a 2,482-case edge-case benchmark and a 570-case human-reviewed egregious-failure set](paired-synthetic-evaluation-datasets-a-2482-case-edge-case-benchmark-and-a-570-case-human.md) - A dataset pair created by Chris Rohlfs et al.
* [PALMA longitudinal dataset of math achievement and self-beliefs in Bavarian secondary schools](palma-longitudinal-dataset-of-math-achievement-and-self-beliefs-in-bavarian-secondary-scho.md) - The PALMA project dataset tracks mathematics achievement and self-beliefs across six measurement waves among secondary-school students in Bavarian schools, using standardized tests, grades, and student surveys.
* [Panamath](panamath.md) - Panamath, the Psychophysical Assessment of Numerical Approximation, is a free software tool developed by the Halberda Lab for administering non-symbolic numerical comparison tasks and recording accuracy and response time in children and other learners.
* [ParaTutor](paratutor.md) - ParaTutor is a multi-agent LLM-based home math word-problem tutoring system developed by LUO Lan and colleagues that supports parent–child tutoring with phase-specific scaffolding and interactive visual grounding.
* [PARE-M (Persistent Agentic Research Environment Measurement) v0.1](pare-m-persistent-agentic-research-environment-measurement-v01.md) - A measurement framework introduced by Anas H.
* [Parent Teacher Home Visits (PTHV)](parent-teacher-home-visits-pthv.md) - Parent Teacher Home Visits (PTHV) is a family-engagement programme run by the Parent Teacher Home Visits organization in which educators and families build relationships through structured voluntary home visits.
* [Parental Engagement of Families from Latino Backgrounds / Participación Educativa de Familias Latinas (PEFL)](parental-engagement-of-families-from-latino-backgrounds-participacion-educativa-de-familia.md) - A 65-item Spanish- or English-language parent-report assessment developed by McWayne et al.
* [ParentCorps](parentcorps.md) - ParentCorps is a family-engagement and social-emotional learning program that equips early-childhood educators and families through structured professional development, materials, and facilitator support, including the ParentCorps Portal.
* [Parenting Relationship Questionnaire—Preschool Form (PRQ) involvement subscale](parenting-relationship-questionnairepreschool-form-prq-involvement-subscale.md) - An eight-item parent-report subscale that measures the frequency of parents’ positive engagement activities with and knowledge of their preschool-aged children.
* [ParentPowered READY4K!](parentpowered-ready4k.md) - ParentPowered READY4K!
* [Parents’ Evaluation of Aural/oral performance of Children (PEACH) questionnaire](parents-evaluation-of-auraloral-performance-of-children-peach-questionnaire.md) - The PEACH is an interview-administered questionnaire developed by Ching and colleagues that uses parent observations and normative data to assess everyday functional communication in children with hearing loss and evaluate amplification effectiveness.
* [Partners for Algorithmic Literacy (PAL)](partners-for-algorithmic-literacy-pal.md) - A Purdue University students-as-partners programme in which undergraduate students and faculty collaborate over six weeks to integrate artificial intelligence into coursework and develop algorithmic literacy.
* [Partnership for 21st Century Skills (P21) framework](partnership-for-21st-century-skills-p21-framework.md) - A framework developed by the Partnership for 21st Century Skills that organizes core subjects, 21st-century content, learning and thinking skills, ICT literacy, and life skills for K–12 education and promotes integrated assessment.
* [Passport to Literacy](passport-to-literacy.md) - Passport to Literacy is a semi-scripted small-group reading intervention programme for K–5 students reading below grade level, using explicit instruction in foundational reading, comprehension, and vocabulary skills.
* [Passport to Teaching](passport-to-teaching.md) - The American Board for Certification of Teacher Excellence's Passport to Teaching is an examination-based teacher-certification programme intended to identify competent teachers regardless of their preparation route.
* [Pathblazer](pathblazer.md) - Pathblazer is a supplemental online mathematics and reading intervention programme for struggling K–8 learners, delivered and supported by its programme provider for use by schools and classroom teachers.
* [PATHS (Promoting Alternative THinking Strategies)](paths-promoting-alternative-thinking-strategies.md) - The Promoting Alternative THinking Strategies (PATHS) curriculum is a classroom-delivered social-emotional learning program developed for teaching emotional understanding, communication, friendship skills, self-control, and social problem solving.
* [Pathway to Academic Success Project](pathway-to-academic-success-project.md) - The Pathway to Academic Success Project is a professional-development and coaching programme for grades 6–12 ELA teachers serving English learners, combining teacher training, coaching, and instructional materials.
* [Pathway to Planetary Health (PPH)](pathway-to-planetary-health-pph.md) - A six-dimension practice framework developed by MacKenzie-Shalders et al.
* [PAUSE framework](pause-framework.md) - A Vanderbilt University framework that guides recruitment and hiring teams through self-reflection and bias-interruption strategies.
* [PAX Good Behavior Game](pax-good-behavior-game.md) - PAX Good Behavior Game is a branded classroom behavior-management programme run by PAXIS Institute that uses team-based behavior monitoring, rewards, teacher training, manuals, timers, and parent materials.
* [PCEX expert-bundled Python dataset](pcex-expert-bundled-python-dataset.md) - A dataset produced from the PCEX programming-learning system containing 123 introductory Python programs organized into 49 expert-labeled bundles of worked examples and related code-completion challenges.
* [Peabody Picture Vocabulary Test—Third Edition (PPVT-III)](peabody-picture-vocabulary-testthird-edition-ppvt-iii.md) - A researcher-administered, norm-referenced picture-based assessment developed by Dunn and Dunn for measuring receptive vocabulary in children and other examinees.
* [PECAS Mediator](pecas-mediator.md) - A research-prototype remote-instruction tool developed by Chang et al.
* [PedaCo: a dual gatekeeping human-AI video co-creation system](pedaco-a-dual-gatekeeping-human-ai-video-co-creation-system.md) - PedaCo is a human-AI collaborative software system that helps educators generate and evaluate instructional videos using CTML-informed script review and automated post-synthesis metrics.
* [PEER Model](peer-model.md) - The PEER Model is an evidence-based framework introduced and empirically tested by Krstić et al.
* [Peer nomination procedure](peer-nomination-procedure.md) - A peer-report sociometric assessment developed by Moreno and adapted by Coie et al.
* [Peer-Assisted Learning Strategies (PALS)](peer-assisted-learning-strategies-pals.md) - A commercially distributed, scripted peer-assisted reading and literacy curriculum for kindergarten through upper elementary grades, developed by researchers including Patricia G.
* [PeerWise](peerwise.md) - PeerWise is a freely available online tool developed at the University of Auckland that enables students to author, answer, rate, comment on, and follow peers' multiple-choice questions.
* [PEFK (Prosocial and Effective Facilitation in Konversations)](pefk-prosocial-and-effective-facilitation-in-konversations.md) - A standardized, multi-domain compilation of online and oral discussion datasets created by Dimitris Tsirmpas and colleagues for benchmarking and analyzing facilitator interventions.
* [Pell Grant Graduation Rate Database and Institution Profile tool](pell-grant-graduation-rate-database-and-institution-profile-tool.md) - A publicly accessible database and profile tool created and maintained by The Education Trust that reports Pell and non-Pell graduation rates, enrollment shares, costs, and related institutional information for prospective students and policymakers.
* [PennGSE Data Science Methods for Digital Learning Platforms](penngse-data-science-methods-for-digital-learning-platforms.md) - A 17-week certificate programme run by the University of Pennsylvania Graduate School of Education, Digital Promise, and the University of Florida that provides hands-on training in data-science methods using digital-learning-platform datasets.
* [Pennsylvania Department of Education elementary school student school climate survey](pennsylvania-department-of-education-elementary-school-student-school-climate-survey.md) - A voluntary elementary student school-climate survey developed by the Pennsylvania Department of Education's Office for Safe Schools with REL Mid-Atlantic to help Pennsylvania schools measure climate and identify areas needing support.
* [Pennsylvania Department of Education voluntary school climate survey](pennsylvania-department-of-education-voluntary-school-climate-survey.md) - A voluntary statewide school-climate survey developed and administered by the Pennsylvania Department of Education's Office for Safe Schools in partnership with REL Mid-Atlantic to measure school climate and identify schools needing support.
* [Pennsylvania teacher evaluation pilot dataset](pennsylvania-teacher-evaluation-pilot-dataset.md) - A dataset covering teacher-observation and value-added evaluation measures for 6,676 teachers across 269 Pennsylvania districts during the 2012–2013 pilot and comparison period, assembled for the Pennsylvania teacher-evaluation pilot.
* [Penny](penny.md) - Penny is a GPT-4o-powered chatbot developed by Steve Woollaston and colleagues to support Japanese junior-high EFL learners' English writing practice through structured corrective feedback.
* [Penquiry V2](penquiry-v2.md) - Penquiry V2 is a pen-based interactive in-situ question-and-answer system developed for learners to query an LLM while studying digital PDF materials.
* [Pepper](pepper.md) - Pepper is a humanoid social-robot platform developed by SoftBank Robotics for multimodal social interaction and educational applications.
* [Perceived Social-Emotional Competence Scale (five dimensions)](perceived-social-emotional-competence-scale-five-dimensions.md) - A five-dimension, 1–7 student self-report scale developed by Collie for assessing adolescents’ perceived competence in social-emotional skills.
* [Perception of AI Utility Questionnaire](perception-of-ai-utility-questionnaire.md) - A 12-item Likert questionnaire developed by Suriá-Martínez et al.
* [PerFit](perfit.md) - PerFit is an R package that computes person-fit statistics for item-response and assessment data, developed for researchers conducting person-fit analysis.
* [Performance Assessment Resource Bank](performance-assessment-resource-bank.md) - A CCSSO collaboration with the Stanford Center for Assessment, Learning, and Equity (SCALE) and the Stanford Center for Opportunity Policy in Education (SCOPE) that provides reviewed curriculum-embedded performance tasks, units, rubrics, scored student work, and educator training protocols.
* [Performance Partnership Pilots for Disconnected Youth (P3)](performance-partnership-pilots-for-disconnected-youth-p3.md) - A federally supported initiative run by six U.S.
* [PERMA-based AI well-being scale for faculty in AI-integrated teaching](perma-based-ai-well-being-scale-for-faculty-in-ai-integrated-teaching.md) - A five-item Likert-scale instrument developed by Liu et al.
* [Persona Problem Builder (PPB)](persona-problem-builder-ppb.md) - A React and Flask web-based platform developed by Walkington et al.
* [PersonaPath benchmark for Knowledge-Centric learning path planning](personapath-benchmark-for-knowledge-centric-learning-path-planning.md) - A benchmark introduced by Yu Liu et al.
* [Persuasion Across Time and Space](persuasion-across-time-and-space.md) - A five-week, Common Core State Standards-aligned English language arts curriculum unit for intermediate-level middle school English learners, developed by Stanford University's Understanding Language initiative.
* [PERTS 15-item academic mindset survey with four subscales](perts-15-item-academic-mindset-survey-with-four-subscales.md) - A PERTS Likert-style student self-report assessment measuring individual mindset, classroom belonging, task relevance, and classroom-task self-efficacy in grades 3–8.
* [PhinetunEd](phinetuned.md) - A Phi-3-Mini language model fine-tuned by Tozadore et al.
* [Phonological Awareness Lessons](phonological-awareness-lessons.md) - Phonological Awareness Lessons is an explicit, systematic phonological-awareness curriculum for Pre-K through first grade, developed for use by teachers supporting foundational reading skills.
* [Physics Playground](physics-playground.md) - Physics Playground is a 2D computer-based physics learning game developed by Shute and colleagues in which students draw simple machines to solve problems while gameplay logs support stealth assessment of physics understanding and persistence.
* [PhysicsMate benchmark of 1,834 curriculum-grounded Bengali physics QA pairs](physicsmate-benchmark-of-1834-curriculum-grounded-bengali-physics-qa-pairs.md) - A benchmark dataset and accompanying knowledge graph of 1,834 Bengali Grade 9–10 physics question-answer pairs grounded in the official NCTB textbook, released by Rashid Azraf Jahin et al.
* [Picture Spelling Test with phonological plausibility scoring](picture-spelling-test-with-phonological-plausibility-scoring.md) - A researcher-designed picture-based spelling assessment with phonological-plausibility scoring, developed by Geers and Hayes for adolescents with prelingual deafness and cochlear implants.
* [Pinpoint Learning mock-exam dataset of 119,034 UK students across 13 national-level mock exams](pinpoint-learning-mock-exam-dataset-of-119034-uk-students-across-13-national-level-mock-ex.md) - A question-level GCSE mathematics mock-exam results dataset extracted from the Pinpoint Learning platform and collected from teacher-uploaded assessments.
* [Pintner's Mental Survey](pintners-mental-survey.md) - A standardized group mental-ability test consisting of six tests for school children ages 6–16, developed by Rudolf Pintner and normed on more than 3,000 children with percentile norms.
* [Pirate Math](pirate-math.md) - Pirate Math is a one-to-one mathematics tutoring programme for third-grade students with calculation and word-problem difficulties, delivered through structured sessions using counting-up and missing-number strategies with a treasure-map reward system.
* [PISA 2018 dataset on intercultural education and student psychosocial outcomes](pisa-2018-dataset-on-intercultural-education-and-student-psychosocial-outcomes.md) - A Programme for International Student Assessment 2018 dataset linking student-, school-, and country-level measures of intercultural education, teacher attitudes, and psychosocial outcomes across 58 countries, produced by the OECD.
* [PISA 2022 Global Crises Module (GCM) student questionnaire](pisa-2022-global-crises-module-gcm-student-questionnaire.md) - A PISA student questionnaire module administered by the OECD to measure 15-year-old students' learning conditions and experiences during COVID-19 school closures.
* [PIVOT](pivot.md) - PIVOT is a three-stage pedagogy-guided generative video tutoring framework developed by Xinchen Ma et al.
* [Playworks](playworks.md) - Playworks is a school-based programme run by the Playworks nonprofit that places trained full-time coaches in schools to organize inclusive, active play during recess and throughout the school day.
* [PLEDGE platform](pledge-platform.md) - PLEDGE is Greenfield Learning's online professional-learning platform, where participating educators complete screeners, set goals, access personalized content, and upload application artifacts.
* [PoETA](poeta.md) - PoETA is a curated toolbox framework developed by Lucciarini and Boniwell for integrating brief, stand-alone, flexible positive-psychology interventions into mainstream classrooms with minimal specialised training.
* [Point-hour ratio](point-hour-ratio.md) - A historical Ohio State University academic-standing index that weights registrar-recorded grades by credit hours and divides total points by total credited hours.
* [Polygence authentic learning dataset](polygence-authentic-learning-dataset.md) - A large anonymized multimodal dataset of more than 100,000 hours of longitudinal one-on-one project-based tutoring interactions between PhD-level mentors and K–12 or pre-college students, collected and maintained by Polygence.
* [Pooled correlational dataset of 288 independent samples (N = 47,121) with OSF repository](pooled-correlational-dataset-of-288-independent-samples-n-47121-with-osf-repository.md) - A pooled correlation-matrix dataset of Chinese-speaking children's cognitive-linguistic skills and word-reading outcomes, released by the study authors through an OSF repository.
* [Positive Action](positive-action.md) - A K–12 curriculum developed and delivered by Positive Action, Inc.
* [Positive Behavioral Interventions and Supports (PBIS)](positive-behavioral-interventions-and-supports-pbis.md) - PBIS is an evidence-based, multitiered framework used by schools to organize data, systems, and behavioral practices through universal, targeted, and individualized supports.
* [Post-secondary Attainment Index (PAI)](post-secondary-attainment-index-pai.md) - The University of Chicago Consortium on School Research's Post-secondary Attainment Index is a district-level indicator combining high-school graduation, college enrollment, and college-completion rates to estimate post-secondary attainment for CPS students.
* [PowerSchool Attendance Intervention](powerschool-attendance-intervention.md) - PowerSchool's attendance-intervention platform integrates attendance data into dashboards and supports educator-to-family messaging to help schools monitor and improve student attendance.
* [Practice-driven data](practice-driven-data.md) - A five-lesson, context-sensitive framework for using data in school improvement—prepare, focus, make meaning, strategize, and disrupt—developed through the University of Chicago’s partnership with Chicago Public Schools.
* [Pre-K Mathematics](pre-k-mathematics.md) - A supplemental preschool mathematics curriculum developed by Alice Klein and Prentice Starkey with Alma Ramirez and distributed by Pearson Scott Foresman.
* [Pre-K to Kindergarten Transitions Project (PKTP)](pre-k-to-kindergarten-transitions-project-pktp.md) - A BPI-staffed community-wide programme in Chicago's Altgeld-Riverdale neighborhood that coordinates cross-grade teacher learning, shared pre-K–kindergarten classroom practices, and family learning activities to support educational transitions.
* [Preprocessed English subset of the MARS dataset for multimodal teaching resource recommendation](preprocessed-english-subset-of-the-mars-dataset-for-multimodal-teaching-resource-recommend.md) - A preprocessed English-language subset of the MARS multimodal teaching-resource dataset, containing resource metadata and learner interaction logs for evaluating personalized recommendation models.
* [Preschool PATHS® Curriculum](preschool-paths-curriculum.md) - A social-emotional learning curriculum used within the REDI programme to develop preschoolers’ self-regulation, self-awareness, social problem-solving, and emotional control.
* [Pressey's new silent-reading test](presseys-new-silent-reading-test.md) - A silent-reading assessment devised by S.
* [Prevention and Get Fit](prevention-and-get-fit.md) - A school-based obesity-prevention programme combining universal nutrition and physical-activity activities with the targeted 12-week Get Fit intervention for students needing additional support.
* [Principal Supervisor Initiative (PSI)](principal-supervisor-initiative-psi.md) - A Wallace Foundation–launched initiative that supported six urban school districts in redefining principal supervision around developing principals as instructional leaders.
* [Principal time-use log dataset from approximately 100 elementary schools in eight districts](principal-time-use-log-dataset-from-approximately-100-elementary-schools-in-eight-district.md) - A research dataset collected by Matthew Johnson and colleagues from approximately 100 elementary schools across eight districts to document how principals allocate their work time.
* [Principals Pursuing Excellence (PPE)](principals-pursuing-excellence-ppe.md) - A two-year competency-based programme initiated by the New Mexico Public Education Department to develop and support turnaround principals, with trained mentors and leadership-focused training.
* [Prior Research Table](prior-research-table.md) - A Mathematica-developed documentation framework that helps education organizations record prior research on a solution and its priority community before planning future evaluations.
* [PRISM (Program for Research Into Self-Modifying Systems)](prism-program-for-research-into-self-modifying-systems.md) - A production-system interpreter developed by Pat Langley and Neches that augments LISP and lets users select among alternative architectural mechanisms and control options.
* [PRISMA-LLM 16-item universal reporting checklist](prisma-llm-16-item-universal-reporting-checklist.md) - PRISMA-LLM is a 16-item reporting framework proposed by Zabaleta et al.
* [PrivacyAI](privacyai.md) - PrivacyAI is an open-source GPT-4o-mini-based pipeline released by the authors for detecting, labeling, extracting, and verifying personally identifiable information in educational text.
* [Pro audito computer-based speechreading training (lippenlesen.ch)](pro-audito-computer-based-speechreading-training-lippenlesench.md) - A freely accessible web-based speechreading training developed by pro audito Schweiz, providing video instruction, flashcards, and exercises to practise visual speech cues and audiovisual speech comprehension in noise.
* [Prober.ai](proberai.md) - Prober.ai is a research-logging software platform that records consented participant sessions, events, drafts, challenges, unlocks, and reflection-coaching interactions for studying learning behavior.

* [Professional Learning Communities at Work (PLC at Work)](professional-learning-communities-at-work-plc-at-work.md) - Solution Tree's school-improvement programme organizes recurring cycles of educator collaboration, assessment, and targeted intervention around collective responsibility for student learning.

* [Progenitor X](progenitor-x.md) - Progenitor X is a narrative-based, turn-based puzzle game developed with a regenerative-medicine research team to teach middle and high school learners stem-cell science practices.
* [PROGFEED](progfeed.md) - PROGFEED is a longitudinal dataset released by Hasnain Heickal et al.

* [Progress Learning](progress-learning.md) - Progress Learning is a standards-aligned K–12 online instructional platform that provides individualized learning paths, assessments, assignments, diagnostics, and progress monitoring across subjects.
* [Project Altair](project-altair.md) - Project Altair is NWEA's project to enhance the constraint-based engine for delivering MAP Growth interim assessments with configurable exposure, routing, content-balance, termination, and invalidation rules.
* [Project Cal-Well](project-cal-well.md) - Project Cal-Well is a California Department of Education initiative, funded through a SAMHSA grant, that helps local educational agencies build sustainable school- and community-based mental health systems.
* [Project CRISS®](project-criss.md) - Project CRISS® is a professional-development programme run by Project CRISS that trains grades 3–12 teachers to embed reading, writing, and learning comprehension strategies in regular instruction.
* [Project LIFT](project-lift.md) - A public-private partnership run by Charlotte-Mecklenburg Schools and its philanthropic partners to implement Opportunity Culture staffing, professional development, and compensation structures in high-needs schools.
* [Project On-Track](project-on-track.md) - Project On-Track is a technology-supported, high-dosage small-group literacy tutoring programme for grades 1–3, run by participating schools in northeastern Tennessee using Amplify Reading mCLASS resources.

* [Project Topeka](project-topeka.md) - Project Topeka is an automated essay-scoring and automated writing-evaluation platform for middle-school argumentative writing, providing instructional materials, rubric-based AI feedback, and iterative student revision.

* [PROMISE (Promoting Readiness of Minors in Supplemental Security Income)](promise-promoting-readiness-of-minors-in-supplemental-security-income.md) - A federal demonstration initiative evaluated for the Social Security Administration that provided services intended to improve education, employment, and long-term self-sufficiency among youth ages 14–16 receiving Supplemental Security Income.
* [Promise Neighborhoods](promise-neighborhoods.md) - Promise Neighborhoods is a U.S.
* [PROMISE programs](promise-programs.md) - PROMISE programs are federally supported state-level initiatives that coordinate services for youth with disabilities receiving SSI and their families to improve educational and employment outcomes.

* [Prompt Equity Transformer (PET)](prompt-equity-transformer-pet.md) - An LLM-based accessibility middleware agent developed by Lier Jin et al.

* [PromptDecipher](promptdecipher.md) - A web-based chatbot-authoring system developed by Miina Koyama and colleagues that lets educators create and refine AI tutoring chatbots by directly correcting simulated bot responses.

* [Prosodiya](prosodiya.md) - Prosodiya is an adaptive digital game-based spelling-training application for mobile touch devices, developed for primary-school children with spelling difficulties.
* [PROTO-TEG](proto-teg.md) - PROTO-TEG is a self-improving intelligent tutoring system for geometry that selects didactic strategies using a student model and knowledge learned from recorded tutoring episodes.

* [PS2 Pal](ps2-pal.md) - A GPT-4-based AI tutoring platform developed by Kestin et al.

* [Puppet-and-touchscreen mindset scale for kindergarteners](puppet-and-touchscreen-mindset-scale-for-kindergarteners.md) - A touchscreen- and puppet-administered self-report assessment developed by Compagnoni et al.
* [Pychometrik](pychometrik.md) - Pychometrik is a Python-based item-analysis and Rasch item-calibration tool developed by NWEA for psychometricians calibrating assessment items.
* [PyNeuraLogic framework for lifted relational neural-symbolic learning](pyneuralogic-framework-for-lifted-relational-neural-symbolic-learning.md) - PyNeuraLogic is an open-source framework developed by Hooshyar et al.
* [PyramidApp](pyramidapp.md) - PyramidApp is a web-based collaborative-learning platform developed by Ishari Amarasinghe, Davinia Hernández-Leo, and H.
* [Python MCQ dataset for unlearning and relearning experiments](python-mcq-dataset-for-unlearning-and-relearning-experiments.md) - A dataset of 2,074 web-collected and LLM-generated Python multiple-choice questions spanning 22 concept categories, created by Jiajia Song et al.

#### Q {: #letter-q }

* [Q-Learning Lab](q-learning-lab.md) - Q-Learning Lab is a single-file, offline bilingual browser tool made by Jueng for teaching introductory tabular Q-learning through an interactive gridworld, Bellman-update visualizations, transition logs, and CSV export.
* [QCGE-SAS](qcge-sas.md) - A seven-point Likert self-assessment scale developed by Jeitziner et al.
* [Quality Counts California (QCC)](quality-counts-california-qcc.md) - A statewide quality rating and improvement framework run through a collaboration among First 5 California, the California Department of Education, and the California Department of Social Services to assess and support early-learning and care sites.

* [Quality Teaching for English Learners (QTEL)](quality-teaching-for-english-learners-qtel.md) - QTEL is a professional-development and instructional programme that supports educators in teaching rigorous academic content and academic English simultaneously to secondary English Learners.

#### R {: #letter-r }

* [RAMPUP](rampup.md) - RAMPUP is a named mathematics-learning programme developed and run by its project team, using contextualized activity structures to support students’ equivalence reasoning.

* [RaumIntelligenzFörderung (RIF) platform for spatial ability development and measurement](raumintelligenzforderung-rif-platform-for-spatial-ability-development-and-measurement.md) - An online platform developed by experts in spatial ability and geometry that provides organized spatial-problem task groups for teacher-assigned practice and performance measurement.

* [Reading Horizons Discovery](reading-horizons-discovery.md) - Reading Horizons Discovery is Reading Horizons' digital K–3 foundational reading curriculum based on systematic, cumulative, explicit phonics instruction informed by Orton–Gillingham and Structured Literacy.

* [Real-world middle school family engagement strategies](real-world-middle-school-family-engagement-strategies.md) - A playbook/framework that organizes middle-school family-engagement strategies and implementation guidance; its maker or operating organization is not identified in the supplied description.
