* [Rocky Mountain Prep Family Advocacy Support Team (FAST)](rocky-mountain-prep-family-advocacy-support-team-fast.md) - Rocky Mountain Prep's Family Advocacy Support Team (FAST) is a referral-based family-support programme providing emergency basic-needs assistance and connections to longer-term resources for students' families.
* [RP-Observe observation tool](rp-observe-observation-tool.md) - RP-Observe is a Rutgers University-developed school observation tool for assessing restorative-justice implementation and outcomes.
* [RSAT](rsat.md) - RSAT is a computer-based online assessment developed by Gonny Schellings and colleagues to measure readers’ comprehension and spontaneous reading-strategy use during sentence-by-sentence reading.
* [Rubric-aligned concept-annotated grading benchmarks (REC-CBM)](rubric-aligned-concept-annotated-grading-benchmarks-rec-cbm.md) - REC-CBM is a research dataset release that adds rubric-aligned, expert-validated concept annotations to the Mohler, ASAP 2.0, and MOCHA open-ended response-grading benchmarks for interpretable automated grading research.
* [RULER](ruler.md) - RULER is a social-emotional learning programme developed and run by the Yale Center for Emotional Intelligence that teaches recognizing, understanding, labeling, expressing, and regulating emotions.

#### S {: #letter-s }

* [S Bio and S Phy knowledge concept tagging datasets](s-bio-and-s-phy-knowledge-concept-tagging-datasets.md) - Benchmark datasets constructed by Zhiwei Yang et al.
* [SACD Research Program](sacd-research-program.md) - The U.S.
* [Sackett's mental alertness tests](sacketts-mental-alertness-tests.md) - A paper-and-pencil assessment battery by L.
* [Safe Passage](safe-passage.md) - A Chicago Public Schools initiative that places adult monitors along predetermined routes to help students travel safely to and from designated schools.
* [SaferSanerSchools™ Whole-School Change](safersanerschools-whole-school-change.md) - A whole-school restorative-practices programme disseminated through the International Institute of Restorative Practices that trains school staff and teams to improve relationships, responsibility, restoration, and school climate.
* [SAFETUTORS benchmark](safetutors-benchmark.md) - A benchmark dataset of single-turn and multi-turn mathematics, physics, and chemistry tutoring dialogues, created and human-validated by Rima Hazra et al.
* [Saga Math](saga-math.md) - Saga Math is an in-school, high-dosage mathematics tutoring programme for secondary students, offered in small-group traditional and technology-enhanced models and implemented by Saga Education.
* [SageModeler](sagemodeler.md) - SageModeler is a free, web-based, open-source computational modeling tool developed by the Concord Consortium for supporting K–12 learners in building, revising, and visualizing models without coding.
* [SAILS](sails.md) - SAILS is a computer-based senior-year remedial mathematics curriculum and programme operated through Tennessee’s secondary–postsecondary education system to help students complete college-level math requirements before enrollment.
* [Salzburger Lesescreening (SLS) reading fluency test](salzburger-lesescreening-sls-reading-fluency-test.md) - A standardized, timed reading-fluency assessment in which students judge the substantive correctness of sentences, used with elementary school learners and administered by trained educators or research staff.
* [Sample Size Guide](sample-size-guide.md) - A Mathematica planning guide prepared for the Gates Foundation that helps education solution developers consider sample-size requirements across design, refinement, early-evidence, and effectiveness-validation phases.
* [Save the Tree of Life](save-the-tree-of-life.md) - A study-developed online game-based training program delivered through the eLea platform to teach inductive reasoning through mathematical tasks.
* [SAVVY interactive visual analytics system](savvy-interactive-visual-analytics-system.md) - SAVVY is a research-developed interactive visual analytics system that helps teachers analyze student attention patterns and revise instructional videos.
* [Scale for Early Mathematics Anxiety (SEMA)](scale-for-early-mathematics-anxiety-sema.md) - SEMA is a 20-item, face-supported self-report questionnaire developed by Wu et al.
* [Scale for measuring graduate students' perceived generative AI literacy (five dimensions, 15 indicators)](scale-for-measuring-graduate-students-perceived-generative-ai-literacy-five-dimensions-15.md) - A validated 15-indicator self-report scale developed by Zhi et al.
* [Scale for Students' Attitudes Toward AIGC Feedback in English Pronunciation Learning](scale-for-students-attitudes-toward-aigc-feedback-in-english-pronunciation-learning.md) - A 16-item five-point Likert self-report scale developed by Zhong et al.
* [Scales measuring academic self-concepts and social, dimensional, and temporal comparison-related beliefs](scales-measuring-academic-self-concepts-and-social-dimensional-and-temporal-comparison-rel.md) - A study-developed German-language Likert questionnaire measuring students' mathematics and German academic self-concepts and their social, dimensional, and temporal comparison-related beliefs.
* [Scaling Apprenticeship and Closing the Skills Gap](scaling-apprenticeship-and-closing-the-skills-gap.md) - Two U.S.
* [Scholastic RISE and RISE Up](scholastic-rise-and-rise-up.md) - Scholastic RISE and RISE Up is a Scholastic Tier 2 small-group reading intervention programme for grades 1–5 that provides structured phonics, word study, comprehension, and guided-writing instruction through station-based materials and digital resources.
* [School Climate Index (SCI)](school-climate-index-sci.md) - A California Department of Education Safe and Supportive Schools indicator that aggregates student self-report data to measure school climate across supports, engagement, safety, violence, victimization, substance use, and truancy.
* [School District of Philadelphia administrative dataset on teacher employment and student–teacher linkages](school-district-of-philadelphia-administrative-dataset-on-teacher-employment-and-studentte.md) - An administrative dataset supplied by the School District of Philadelphia containing teacher employment, teacher characteristics, student–teacher linkages, and school characteristics for analyzing teacher turnover and equitable access to effective teachers.
* [School Improvement Grants (SIG)](school-improvement-grants-sig.md) - A U.S.
* [School-Home Early Language and Literacy assessment](school-home-early-language-and-literacy-assessment.md) - A language and literacy assessment that measures young children's English narrative production and related expressive-language skills through tasks such as the Bear Story picture-prompt narrative.
* [School-Wide Information System (SWIS)](school-wide-information-system-swis.md) - An internet-based data-entry and reporting system developed for schools to collect, manage, and analyze office discipline referral data for behavior-support decision making.
* [Science Student Electronic Exit Ticket (SEET)](science-student-electronic-exit-ticket-seet.md) - A web-based application developed by Ali Raza and colleagues that collects student experience exit-ticket surveys and provides whole-class and demographic-disaggregated visualizations for teachers.
* [Scientific Reasoning Inventory (SRI)](scientific-reasoning-inventory-sri.md) - A paper-and-pencil assessment developed by Van de Sande et al.
* [SciEntsBank](scientsbank.md) - SciEntsBank is a benchmark dataset of human-annotated student science short answers and semantic-correctness labels used to develop and evaluate automated short-answer assessment systems.
* [Scratch](scratch.md) - Scratch is an MIT Media Lab-developed block-based programming language and online sharing ecosystem in which learners create, remix, debug, and share interactive projects.
* [ScratchJr](scratchjr.md) - ScratchJr is a tablet-based introductory programming app developed by the DevTech Research Group at Tufts University in collaboration with the MIT Media Lab for young children to create interactive stories and games.
* [SCRIPT (Step-based Coding for Research and Intelligent Programming Tutoring)](script-step-based-coding-for-research-and-intelligent-programming-tutoring.md) - SCRIPT is a web-based intelligent tutoring system developed by Alina Deriyeva and colleagues for teaching Python programming while recording fine-grained learner data and supporting adaptable pedagogical and learner-model research.
* [SCY-Lab](scy-lab.md) - SCY-Lab is a digital learning environment developed by the SCY project that supports collaborative inquiry through mission-based activities, writing tools, annotation tools, and information resources.
* [SDT-based Chinese-language ESP motivation questionnaire (adapted LLOS-IEA)](sdt-based-chinese-language-esp-motivation-questionnaire-adapted-llos-iea.md) - A 23-item Chinese-language Likert questionnaire adapted by Liu (2016) from Noels's LLOS-IEA to measure seven self-determined motivation subtypes among Chinese college students studying English for Specific Purposes.
* [Sea Turtle Trek](sea-turtle-trek.md) - A 124-page environmental-education curriculum packet for grades 6–8, developed for Hammocks Beach State Park and providing hands-on sea-turtle and conservation activities aligned with North Carolina standards.
* [SEAD framework: six policy areas for equitable social, emotional, and academic development](sead-framework-six-policy-areas-for-equitable-social-emotional-and-academic-development.md) - The Education Trust's policy framework organizes state and district action across six areas to support students' integrated social, emotional, and academic development, particularly for underserved P-12 learners.
* [SEAD integration framework (the three-legged stool)](sead-integration-framework-the-three-legged-stool.md) - A guide-based framework for integrating explicit social-emotional skill instruction, embedded academic practice, and relationship-rich environments into K–12 school improvement.
* [SEEQ-S and TEEQ-S](seeq-s-and-teeq-s.md) - Parallel 15-factor student-feedback and teacher-self-evaluation instruments developed by Marsh et al.
* [SEERNet](seernet.md) - SEERNet is an Institute of Education Sciences-funded network that supports responsible, inclusive, theory-driven research using data from digital learning platforms through shared infrastructure, governance, datasets, and research-practice partnerships.
* [Self-Assessment of Place-Based Systems Change Efforts](self-assessment-of-place-based-systems-change-efforts.md) - A self-assessment instrument developed by Mathematica and Equal Measure to help community partnerships examine their strengths and identify areas for deeper place-based systems-change work.
* [Self-Determined Learning Model of Instruction](self-determined-learning-model-of-instruction.md) - A teacher-implemented instructional model developed by Palmer et al.
* [Self-Regulated Strategy Development (SRSD)](self-regulated-strategy-development-srsd.md) - Self-Regulated Strategy Development is a structured instructional framework developed by Karen Harris and Steven Graham for teaching academic strategies and self-regulation skills, especially in writing and other achievement domains.
* [SelfCode2.0 dataset of student and expert line-by-line code explanations](selfcode20-dataset-of-student-and-expert-line-by-line-code-explanations.md) - A corpus of line-level code explanations produced by novice programming students and experts, annotated for correctness and released for evaluating code-explanation and semantic-similarity systems.
* [Semester-long simulated-MOOC EEG dataset from an 11-lesson biology course](semester-long-simulated-mooc-eeg-dataset-from-an-11-lesson-biology-course.md) - A dataset of semester-long, stage-labeled 14-channel EEG recordings from six undergraduates completing lecture, virtual-lab, and quiz activities in an 11-lesson simulated-MOOC biology course, released by Hui Xie et al.
* [Senior Digital Literacy Evaluation (SDLE) instrument](senior-digital-literacy-evaluation-sdle-instrument.md) - A 110-item assessment developed by Martínez-Alcalá and colleagues to measure older adults’ digital literacy and technological competencies across computer use, Internet use, and domestic and daily-life devices.
* [SensEnablr](sensenablr.md) - SensEnablr is a student-facing learning analytics dashboard developed by Gomathy Ramaswami and colleagues that combines descriptive, predictive, and prescriptive analytics to help students interpret and act on their learning data.
* [Sensory Profile](sensory-profile.md) - The Sensory Profile is a parent-reported standardized questionnaire developed by Winnie Dunn for assessing children's sensory processing in everyday activities, including auditory, visual, tactile, movement, and social-emotional responses.
* [Sentence-level episode-labeling corpus and RoBERTa classifier trained on ThinkARM annotations](sentence-level-episode-labeling-corpus-and-roberta-classifier-trained-on-thinkarm-annotati.md) - A sentence-level corpus of ThinkARM-annotated Omni-MATH reasoning traces with a RoBERTa classifier for automatically labeling problem-solving episodes, developed by Chenguang Wang et al.
* [Sentinel AI-powered security game platform](sentinel-ai-powered-security-game-platform.md) - Sentinel is a mobile game platform developed by Bingjun Li et al.
* [Serie Leamos](serie-leamos.md) - Serie Leamos is a free digital Spanish library created by Georgia State University's Spanish program and School of Art and Design, offering student-written and student-illustrated stories with multiple reading levels, vocabulary support, activities, downloadable texts, and audiobooks.
* [Seven categories of charter school core functions for organizing distress indicators](seven-categories-of-charter-school-core-functions-for-organizing-distress-indicators.md) - A charter-school authorizer toolkit framework that organizes longitudinal review of school distress indicators across leadership, governing board, operations, financial management, talent, culture, and instruction.
* [Seven co-designed Learning Transition Design Principles for LER technologies](seven-co-designed-learning-transition-design-principles-for-ler-technologies.md) - A co-designed framework from the report authors for guiding and evaluating learning and employment record technologies serving HSE learners and workers.
* [Seven-item parent-reported AAC implementation experience score](seven-item-parent-reported-aac-implementation-experience-score.md) - A seven-item parent-reported questionnaire developed by Jarrar Hala et al.
* [SFAILQ: 32-item Six-Facet Artificial Intelligence Literacy Questionnaire](sfailq-32-item-six-facet-artificial-intelligence-literacy-questionnaire.md) - A 32-item self-report assessment developed by Liu Qingqi et al.
* [Shape School](shape-school.md) - A computerized preschool assessment developed for research use to measure cognitive flexibility and related executive-control components through color, shape, and switching conditions.
* [Shaping Our Appalachian Region (SOAR)](shaping-our-appalachian-region-soar.md) - A nonprofit regional revitalization programme that coordinates Appalachian individuals and organizations around shared values and goals, including technology, computer science education, and digital-economy workforce development.
* [Shared dataset combining the Anthropic Economic Index with Census BTOS employer adoption data](shared-dataset-combining-the-anthropic-economic-index-with-census-btos-employer-adoption-d.md) - A dataset used by the brief series that combines Anthropic's task-level AI exposure index with U.S.
* [Shared Measures Construct Map](shared-measures-construct-map.md) - A one-page construct-map instrument in a practical measurement guide that defines six dimensions, sub-constructs, and relationships for monitoring ELA and math curriculum-shift implementation.
* [SHIELD](shield.md) - A VSCode extension developed by Rohit Mehra et al.
* [Shiny App for finding optimal designs in classroom climate studies](shiny-app-for-finding-optimal-designs-in-classroom-climate-studies.md) - A Shiny web application created by Steffen Zitzmann and colleagues to help educational researchers plan classroom-climate studies by optimizing statistical power or study budget under multilevel design assumptions.
* [Shiny App for simulating and visualizing the goals-performance-emotions model](shiny-app-for-simulating-and-visualizing-the-goals-performance-emotions-model.md) - A publicly accessible R Shiny application released by the article's authors for simulating and visualizing the dynamics of goals, performance, and emotions in their hypothesized model.
* [Short student survey](short-student-survey.md) - A study-specific student questionnaire created and administered by the evaluation team to measure mathematical experiences, belonging, self-efficacy, and perceptions of instructional implementation.
* [Simba Wachanga](simba-wachanga.md) - A year-long interdisciplinary rites-of-passage programme for African-American boys, implemented by the East End Neighborhood House in Cleveland through Elders, resource people, and Big Brothers.
* [SimCalc](simcalc.md) - SimCalc is a mathematics curriculum package developed by the SimCalc project that integrates dynamic representation software, student workbooks, and teacher professional development for teaching rate, proportionality, and linear functions.
* [Simple Solutions](simple-solutions.md) - A branded supplemental curriculum from Simple Solutions that provides brief daily distributed-practice lessons with spiral review and optional online resources for K–8 subjects.
* [SIMS80 cross-national longitudinal database](sims80-cross-national-longitudinal-database.md) - A cross-national longitudinal mathematics achievement and self-concept database from the IEA's Second International Mathematics Study, covering 13-year-old students, schools, and classroom and student background variables in the USA, Ontario, and New Zealand.
* [SimSE](simse.md) - SimSE is a dataset of 1,135 simulated five-minute teaching sessions by pre-service teachers, annotated for metacognitive modeling components and collected through the TeachSim platform by Xu and colleagues.
* [Simucase](simucase.md) - Simucase is a clinical simulation platform developed and operated by Simucase for speech-language pathology, audiology, and occupational therapy education.
* [SINE](sine.md) - SINE is a domain-agnostic pipeline that uses an open-weight language model, deterministic validation, and automated repair to generate playable choice-based interactive-fiction scenarios from structured educational content.
* [Single Stop USA's Community College Initiative](single-stop-usas-community-college-initiative.md) - A Single Stop USA programme delivered through community colleges that connects low-income students with benefits screening, application assistance, tax and legal services, and case management to address nonacademic barriers.
* [SIPPS](sipps.md) - SIPPS (Systematic Instruction in Phonological Awareness, Phonics, and Sight Words) is a structured-literacy curriculum developed and distributed by Collaborative Classroom for foundational reading instruction and intervention in grades K–12.
* [SISTC integrated student dataset of 1,027 cleaned records with 14 features for dropout prediction](sistc-integrated-student-dataset-of-1027-cleaned-records-with-14-features-for-dropout-pred.md) - A de-identified dataset created by SISTC researchers by integrating enrollment, academic performance, demographic, and enrollment-history records to support modeling of student withdrawal and cancellation risk.
* [Situational Thinking Styles Scale for Sixth Grade Students](situational-thinking-styles-scale-for-sixth-grade-students.md) - A situational multiple-choice scale developed by Chaiyapornpattana et al.
* [Six adapted GenAI ethical scenarios for assessing scenario-based ethical judgment](six-adapted-genai-ethical-scenarios-for-assessing-scenario-based-ethical-judgment.md) - A six-item scenario-based assessment adapted from Dodson et al.
* [Six design principles for instructionally relevant assessment systems](six-design-principles-for-instructionally-relevant-assessment-systems.md) - A Badrinarayan (2024) framework for state education agencies to design summative assessment systems intended to support standards-aligned disciplinary learning and improve classroom instruction.
* [Six Design Principles for Summer Learning and Beyond framework](six-design-principles-for-summer-learning-and-beyond-framework.md) - A framework developed by Summer Learning et al.
* [Six NGSS-aligned middle school student drawing assessment dataset](six-ngss-aligned-middle-school-student-drawing-assessment-dataset.md) - A dataset of middle school students’ scientific drawings from six NGSS-aligned modeling assessment items, annotated by domain experts across three proficiency levels and released with example drawings.
* [Six R&D Practice Dimensions Framework](six-rd-practice-dimensions-framework.md) - A framework from Research and Development in America's Schools that helps school systems assess and develop R&D practice across strategy, structure, intensity, spread, engagement, and evidence.
* [Six skill-tagged response datasets from ASSISTments and Mindspark used for model comparison](six-skill-tagged-response-datasets-from-assistments-and-mindspark-used-for-model-compariso.md) - Agarwal et al.
* [Six-domain AI skills and competencies framework for educators and educational leaders](six-domain-ai-skills-and-competencies-framework-for-educators-and-educational-leaders.md) - A Digital Promise framework organizing AI literacy and readiness competencies for PK–12 educators and educational leaders across technical, analytical, interpersonal, ethical, adaptive, and implementation domains.
* [Six-domain AI skills and competencies framework for PK-12 learners](six-domain-ai-skills-and-competencies-framework-for-pk-12-learners.md) - A Digital Promise framework that organizes PK-12 learner AI competencies across technical, practical, ethical, analytical, interpersonal, and adaptive domains.
* [Six-standard framework for faculty technology integration in higher education](six-standard-framework-for-faculty-technology-integration-in-higher-education.md) - A six-standard framework developed by Helen Crompton et al.
* [Six-strategy state policy framework for increasing educator workforce diversity](six-strategy-state-policy-framework-for-increasing-educator-workforce-diversity.md) - An EdTrust policy framework that organizes state and district actions to increase the racial and cultural diversity of the educator workforce and improve students' access to diverse educators.
* [Six-Tool Starter Set for State and District Evidence-Based Improvement](six-tool-starter-set-for-state-and-district-evidence-based-improvement.md) - A modifiable six-tool framework produced for state and district education agencies to examine improvement practices and select evidence-based interventions.
* [Six-topic taxonomy for guiding GenAI responsible use policy content](six-topic-taxonomy-for-guiding-genai-responsible-use-policy-content.md) - A RAPID GenAI Working Group framework that organizes responsible generative-AI policy guidance into AI literacy, safety, ethics, transparency, implementation guidelines, and evaluation and impact.
* [Skills-first network logic model](skills-first-network-logic-model.md) - A logic-model planning framework presented in the report Understanding, Valuing, and Utilizing Skills-First Practices as an Organization Supporting to guide stakeholders in building an aligned skills-first system.
* [SKT-SRL: a scenario-based conditional strategy knowledge test](skt-srl-a-scenario-based-conditional-strategy-knowledge-test.md) - A scenario-based offline assessment developed by Laura Dörrenbächer-Ulrich and colleagues that measures college students’ ability to distinguish useful from less useful cognitive, metacognitive, and motivational learning strategies in specific situations.
* [Skyline ELA](skyline-ela.md) - A K–2 literacy curriculum launched and implemented by Chicago Public Schools in 2021.
* [slidesqaqa](slidesqaqa.md) - slidesqaqa is a Flask-based application that uses document extraction and multimodal LLM inference to analyze PDF presentation slides and generate hierarchical topics, slide-role classifications, learning goals, and context-aware comprehension questions.
* [SLM prototype Cognitive Agent with QKV-style retrieval over a natural-language Knowledge Base](slm-prototype-cognitive-agent-with-qkv-style-retrieval-over-a-natural-language-knowledge-b.md) - A research prototype developed by Hyeongdon Moon et al.
* [SmartPhysics: Rotation Lab](smartphysics-rotation-lab.md) - A self-contained, AI-generated browser application developed by Josep Ll.
* [SmartTutor](smarttutor.md) - A simulated Python tutoring environment developed by Oluseyi Olukolaa for testing pedagogical safety and adaptive tutoring policies in educational reinforcement-learning systems.
* [SMART®](smart.md) - Start Making a Reader Today (SMART®) is a low-cost Oregon school-based programme that provides volunteer one-on-one reading tutoring and free books to K–2 students at risk of reading failure.
* [SMDEP](smdep.md) - SMDEP is a free six-week residential science-enrichment programme run at 12 national sites for minority and socioeconomically disadvantaged undergraduates interested in medical or dental careers.
* [Sobrato Early Academic Language (SEAL) model](sobrato-early-academic-language-seal-model.md) - A PreK–Grade 3 oral-language development model developed by the Sobrato Family Foundation for multilingual learners, including English learners with disabilities.
* [Social Presence Measure (Kreijns et al., 2020)](social-presence-measure-kreijns-et-al-2020.md) - A 27-item Rasch-validated, two-dimensional self-report instrument developed by Kreijns et al.
* [Social Problem Situations Inventory for Children (SPSIC)](social-problem-situations-inventory-for-children-spsic.md) - A researcher-developed performance assessment by Yılmaz and colleagues that measures children's social problem-solving across six dimensions through scored responses to social problem situations.
* [Social Sciences Education Framework for California Public Schools](social-sciences-education-framework-for-california-public-schools.md) - A California state education framework that organizes K–12 social sciences curricula around disciplinary understanding, skills, diversity, values, and civic participation.
* [Social, Academic, and Emotional Behavior Risk Screener (SAEBRS)](social-academic-and-emotional-behavior-risk-screener-saebrs.md) - SAEBRS is a universal screening assessment for students’ social, academic, and emotional behavior risk, used by schools and districts to identify needs and guide mental-health supports.
* [SocioPatterns RFID badges](sociopatterns-rfid-badges.md) - A SocioPatterns wearable RFID proximity-sensing system that records face-to-face contact durations between participants for collaboration and network analysis.
* [Socrates](socrates.md) - Socrates is a playground-and-grader system developed by Xinming Yang et al.
* [Socratic Circles](socratic-circles.md) - An open educational curriculum package developed by the RCS Core Team comprising professional-development modules, a classroom guidebook, and Community Socratic Circle materials for facilitating text- and question-centered discussions.
* [SocraticEdu](socraticedu.md) - SocraticEdu is a corpus of 797 Chinese multi-turn Socratic tutoring trajectories for elementary science, reconstructed from platform logs and supplemented with synthetic dialogues by Wang et al.
* [SocratiCode](socraticode.md) - A prompt-based Socratic and adaptive generative-AI tutoring framework for K–12 beginners learning Python, developed by the study authors.
* [SocratiCode replication package with full prompt specification](socraticode-replication-package-with-full-prompt-specification.md) - A Zenodo-archived replication package containing the full topic-prompt specification for the SocratiCode generative-AI programming tutor, released by the study authors to support replication and extension.
* [socratictest.com](socratictestcom.md) - socratictest.com is a custom platform developed by Ilya Mikhelson to administer Socratic Test assessments with adaptive conversation, evidence logging, and human-AI grading workflows.
* [Software engineering open-ended answer dataset](software-engineering-open-ended-answer-dataset.md) - An anonymized dataset created by Pecuchova et al.
* [SOPHIE 2.0](sophie-20.md) - SOPHIE 2.0 is a browser-based platform developed by Masum Hasan and colleagues that uses audiovisual virtual patients, LLM-generated dialogue, and automated feedback to support clinical communication practice for healthcare learners.
* [Sound Partners](sound-partners.md) - Sound Partners is a structured one-to-one early-literacy tutoring programme developed by the University of Oregon and delivered by trained paraprofessional tutors.
* [South Australian Well-being and Engagement Collection (WEC)](south-australian-well-being-and-engagement-collection-wec.md) - The WEC is an annual student survey administered by the South Australian Department for Education to measure emotional well-being, school engagement, learning readiness, and health and well-being in public-school students in Years 4–12.
* [South Fayette School District CS/STEAM pathway](south-fayette-school-district-cssteam-pathway.md) - A CS/STEAM curriculum pathway developed and implemented by South Fayette School District, with lesson plans organized around four interdisciplinary throughlines and adopted or adapted by other projects.
* [Southwest English Learner Literacy (SWELL) Partnership](southwest-english-learner-literacy-swell-partnership.md) - A partnership-run programme framework and logic model that guides the adaptation, testing, and scale-up of Write to Succeed professional learning to improve English learner literacy outcomes.
* [Span-task battery distinguishing STM (simple span) from WM (complex span) in children](span-task-battery-distinguishing-stm-simple-span-from-wm-complex-span-in-children.md) - A child memory assessment battery developed and administered by Robert Kail and colleagues, combining simple- and complex-span tasks with reading and processing-speed measures for children aged 7 to 13.
* [Spanish MAP Growth Reading](spanish-map-growth-reading.md) - A Spanish-language, computer-adaptive K–8 reading achievement assessment developed by NWEA and linked to the English MAP Growth Reading scale.
* [Spanish MAP Reading Fluency](spanish-map-reading-fluency.md) - Spanish MAP Reading Fluency is NWEA's adaptive Spanish-language assessment for oral reading fluency and foundational reading skills, administered in group settings with automatic speech scoring and reports for educators.
* [Spanish MAP Reading Fluency Foundational Skills](spanish-map-reading-fluency-foundational-skills.md) - NWEA's Spanish-language early-grades reading assessment measures foundational skills across kindergarten through grade 3 and provides achievement and growth norms for interpreting scores over time.
* [Special Education Leadership Academy (Massachusetts)](special-education-leadership-academy-massachusetts.md) - A WestEd Learning Innovations leadership-development programme, funded by the Massachusetts Department of Elementary and Secondary Education, that supports experienced Massachusetts special education directors through sessions, follow-up meetings, and peer networking.
* [Special-R1](special-r1.md) - Special-R1 is a released artifact suite comprising a ZPD-filtered multi-domain tutoring corpus, the SPED-Tutor-Mix disability-aware evaluation set, seven model checkpoints, and an evaluation pipeline created by Unggi Lee et al.
* [Specialty Schools National Dashboard](specialty-schools-national-dashboard.md) - An interactive NWEA data-visualization dashboard that reports MAP Growth achievement and growth patterns for U.S.
* [Spelling Mastery](spelling-mastery.md) - A McGraw-Hill Direct Instruction spelling curriculum for grades 1–6 that uses phonemic, morphemic, and whole-word strategies in scripted daily lessons across six levels.
* [SpellRead™](spellread.md) - SpellRead™ is a structured literacy programme for struggling readers in grade 2 and above, delivered through systematic explicit auditory and visual reading instruction in small groups and supported by an instructor monitoring system.
* [SPIRaL (Students Partners for Information Research and Literacy)](spiral-students-partners-for-information-research-and-literacy.md) - SPIRaL is Purdue's two-semester students-as-partners undergraduate research programme in which information-studies students investigate societal information challenges while developing information-literacy and research skills.
* [Splash and Bubbles for Parents](splash-and-bubbles-for-parents.md) - A PBS KIDS companion app for the Splash and Bubbles television series that supports joint parent-child viewing, science-related conversation, and hands-on activities.
* [SPREE](spree.md) - A process developed and applied by the Corporation for National and Community Service to help funders and grantees evaluate, select, and scale evidence-based interventions.
* [Springboard Summer](springboard-summer.md) - Springboard Summer is a five-week intensive summer literacy programme run by Springboard Collaborative for rising kindergarteners through fourth graders, combining literacy instruction, family engagement, educator support, and incentives.
* [Spritz](spritz.md) - Spritz is a Discord-based LLM conversational agent that detects collaboration tensions, elicits private individual reflections, and returns anonymized perspective syntheses for interdisciplinary student teams.
* [SQEIAIGRASQ questionnaire and Quadratic Equation Achievement Test (QEAT)](sqeiaigrasq-questionnaire-and-quadratic-equation-achievement-test-qeat.md) - A pair of validated instruments developed by Oladayo et al.
* [SRA 2Inform](sra-2inform.md) - SRA 2Inform is a web-based, teacher-facing data-management application supporting the Corrective Reading program by tracking student performance.
* [SRA Real Math Building Blocks PreK](sra-real-math-building-blocks-prek.md) - A supplemental preschool mathematics curriculum developed by Douglas Clements and Julie Sarama and distributed by SRA/McGraw-Hill, combining software, manipulatives, print materials, group activities, learning centers, and home activities.
* [SRL-AIDE](srl-aide.md) - A teacher professional-development framework that combines explicit self-regulated-learning instruction with live-actor classroom simulations, feedback, video modelling, and guided reflection for in-service elementary teachers.

* [SSL](ssl.md) - SSL is a structured early-literacy intervention programme whose developers provide lessons using modelling, guided practice, progress monitoring, and reteaching.
* [SSM (Simple Surface Warfare Model) digital wargame platform](ssm-simple-surface-warfare-model-digital-wargame-platform.md) - A Swedish Defence University digital, map-based naval tactics wargame in which opposing teams maneuver units, manage sensors and weapons, and solve increasingly complex scenarios in real time.
* [ST Math](st-math.md) - ST Math is game-based instructional software for K–12 mathematics developed and provided by the MIND Research Institute, using visual-spatial puzzles to support mathematics learning.
* [Stages of Concern Questionnaire (SoCQ)](stages-of-concern-questionnaire-socq.md) - A 35-item Likert self-report instrument developed by Loucks and colleagues within the Concerns-Based Adoption Model to measure educators' intensity across seven stages of concern about an educational innovation.
* [Stakeholder-facing web application for real-time text complexity and curriculum alignment analysis](stakeholder-facing-web-application-for-real-time-text-complexity-and-curriculum-alignment.md) - A Flask-based application developed by Jordan J.
* [StanBKT](stanbkt.md) - StanBKT is an open-source Python package made by Siddhartha Pradhan et al.

* [Standards for Professional Learning](standards-for-professional-learning.md) - A seven-standard framework for organizing results-oriented educator professional learning, developed and maintained by Learning Forward with partner organizations.
* [Standards of Excellence for Outcomes Based Contracting™](standards-of-excellence-for-outcomes-based-contracting.md) - A framework from COBC that organizes outcomes-based edtech contracting around population and program definition, outcomes and metrics, contingent payment, mutual accountability, and continuous improvement.
* [Stanford Educational Data Archive (SEDA)](stanford-educational-data-archive-seda.md) - A Stanford Education Data Archive dataset providing district-level estimates of mathematics and ELA achievement and growth for Grades 3–8 across U.S.

* [Stanford-revision of the Binet retest dataset from 126 institutionalized mental defectives](stanford-revision-of-the-binet-retest-dataset-from-126-institutionalized-mental-defectives.md) - A published dataset compiled by Poull from Stanford-revision Binet retests of 126 institutionalized children and adults, reporting IQ changes across retest intervals.

* [Starch's 600-word dictionary-sampled spelling test in six comparable 100-word lists](starchs-600-word-dictionary-sampled-spelling-test-in-six-comparable-100-word-lists.md) - A standardized dictated spelling assessment developed by Edward L.
* [STARI](stari.md) - A supplemental, multicomponent reading intervention for struggling middle-school readers, developed by the Strategic Education Research Partnership (SERP) and delivered through thematic units during the school day.
* [StatBot](statbot.md) - StatBot is a retrieval-augmented GPT-4 chatbot developed and deployed by Yuan-Hsuan Lee et al.
* [State Digital Equity and Opportunity Framework](state-digital-equity-and-opportunity-framework.md) - A cross-sector digital-equity planning framework developed in A Framework for Digital Equity that guides state education, housing, and workforce agencies in setting objectives, requirements, metrics, and funding priorities.
* [State epistemic curiosity measure](state-epistemic-curiosity-measure.md) - A researcher-developed multi-item, 5-point Likert self-report assessment by Henk G.

* [STEM Smart](stem-smart.md) - Stony Brook University's umbrella STEM outreach programme, comprising federally funded initiatives that provide mentoring, research and internship opportunities, career preparation, and tutoring across the STEM education pipeline.
* [STEM-tastic Adventures](stem-tastic-adventures.md) - A free mobile app developed through a National Science Foundation-funded co-design project with preschool teachers and families to provide children ages 3–5 with playful computational-thinking and STEM learning activities.
* [STEMHub](stemhub.md) - STEMHub is a modular AI-enhanced personalised and adaptive learning platform developed by Nwogo et al.
* [STEMscopes](stemscopes.md) - STEMscopes is a comprehensive digital and print K–12 science curriculum with hands-on materials, interactive investigations, and embedded assessments, developed and provided by Accelerate Learning, Inc.
* [Step One](step-one.md) - Step One is a free age-friendly pre-beginning ESL and U.S.
* [Stepping Stones to Literacy](stepping-stones-to-literacy.md) - Stepping Stones to Literacy is a supplemental early-literacy curriculum developed and distributed by Sopris West for underachieving kindergarten and older preschool readers.
* [Sternberg's intellectual styles inventory](sternbergs-intellectual-styles-inventory.md) - A self-report inventory developed by Robert Sternberg that measures students' intellectual-style preferences, including legislative, executive, and judicial orientations, using 1-to-9 ratings of descriptive statements.
* [STORY MINE](story-mine.md) - STORY MINE is a digital recovery-narrative module in the Minddistrict client portal that provides curated hopeful recovery stories as video podcasts with written summaries and audio narration.
* [Strategic Data Project (SDP) Fellowship](strategic-data-project-sdp-fellowship.md) - Fellowship programmes run by the Strategic Data Project and Education Pioneers that place and support fellows in education agencies to strengthen data-use capacity and practices.

* [Strategic Instruction Model](strategic-instruction-model.md) - Strategic Instruction Model (SIM) is a multi-tiered adolescent-literacy curriculum and instructional programme created and disseminated by the University of Kansas Center for Research on Learning, including its Learning Strategies Curriculum variants Xtreme Reading and Fusion Reading.
* [Strategic Reading](strategic-reading.md) - Strategic Reading is a structured adolescent-literacy curriculum developed and delivered by the University of Kansas Center for Research on Learning.
* [Strategic Teaching and Reading Project](strategic-teaching-and-reading-project.md) - A North Central Regional Educational Laboratory professional-development programme that trains teachers to use five reading-comprehension strategies: metacognition, prior knowledge, inferencing, word meaning, and text structure.
* [Strategies for Improving Attendance in Pre-Kindergarten and Kindergarten](strategies-for-improving-attendance-in-pre-kindergarten-and-kindergarten.md) - A Go-Learn-Grow toolkit created by Martha Bleeker and colleagues that provides attendance-improvement strategies and practical materials for districts, schools, and early-childhood providers.
* [Strategies to Improve Reading Partnership (STIR)](strategies-to-improve-reading-partnership-stir.md) - STIR is a REL Midwest co-developed K–1 literacy instruction improvement programme that provides educators with evidence-based phonological-awareness and phonics resources, data-use tools, training, and coaching.
* [Strong Field Framework adapted to Breakthrough R&D in Education](strong-field-framework-adapted-to-breakthrough-rd-in-education.md) - A framework developed and used by the ATS Hub to organize field-building around five conditions intended to strengthen and coordinate education R&D.
* [StructRAG](structrag.md) - A Sun Y.
* [Structured Word Inquiry (SWI)](structured-word-inquiry-swi.md) - A morphology-centered framework for teaching English reading and spelling by integrating grapheme–phoneme correspondences with morphology, etymology, and vocabulary through tools such as word sums and morphological matrices.
* [Student Behavioral Health Incentive Program (SBHIP)](student-behavioral-health-incentive-program-sbhip.md) - A California initiative that distributed Medi-Cal managed care plan funding to county offices of education and local educational agencies from 2022 through 2024 to expand school-affiliated behavioral health services for TK–12 students.
* [Student Learning Style Questionnaire](student-learning-style-questionnaire.md) - A questionnaire developed by Anthony Grasha and Sheryl Riechmann that measures six student learning-style orientations based on attitudes toward course content, teachers, peers, and classroom procedures.
* [Student Performance and Learning Behavior Dataset for Educational Analytics (Zenodo, N = 14,003)](student-performance-and-learning-behavior-dataset-for-educational-analytics-zenodo-n-14003.md) - A CC0 Zenodo dataset of 14,003 anonymized higher-education student records compiled from two public Kaggle datasets for behavioral profiling, educational analytics, and adaptive-learning recommendation research.
* [Student_100 dataset for student simulation](student-100-dataset-for-student-simulation.md) - A dataset created by Tao Wu et al.
* [StudentBench](studentbench.md) - StudentBench is a public platform and associated open dataset developed by Curtis Northcutt et al.
* [StudentSimEval](studentsimeval.md) - StudentSimEval is a benchmark dataset, fixed per-student record schema, held-out evaluation split, and scoring code created by Ke Yang et al.
* [StudentTracker for High Schools](studenttracker-for-high-schools.md) - A National Student Clearinghouse data service that enables states, districts, and schools to examine former K–12 students’ postsecondary enrollment, persistence, and completion outcomes.
* [Student–Teacher Relationship Scale (STRS)](studentteacher-relationship-scale-strs.md) - The STRS is a teacher-report questionnaire developed by Robert C.
* [Study-developed NGSS-focused assessments of 1st grade three-dimensional learning and science vocabulary in-use](study-developed-ngss-focused-assessments-of-1st-grade-three-dimensional-learning-and-scien.md) - These are researcher-developed assessments measuring first-grade NGSS-focused science learning and science-vocabulary use in the study.
* [StudyChat dataset](studychat-dataset.md) - StudyChat is a publicly available dataset released by Menggian Wu and collaborators containing naturalistic undergraduate student–LLM programming dialogues linked to assignment submissions.
* [SuaCode Africa](suacode-africa.md) - A two-month smartphone-based online programming curriculum developed for high school students, university students, and recent graduates in Africa and the diaspora, using Processing and the APDE Android app.
* [Success Boston Coaching](success-boston-coaching.md) - Success Boston Coaching is a college-access coaching programme run through Boston-area coaching partner organizations to support students' transition to and persistence in college.
* [Success for All](success-for-all.md) - Success for All is a comprehensive K–5 school reform and reading programme developed and operated by the Success for All Foundation for schools serving students at risk of reading difficulty.
* [Success Project](success-project.md) - A school-based college-access and student-success programme implemented by full-time Success coordinators through the 6to16 curriculum, grades-and-attendance monitoring, and high-school application support.
* [Successor Prep](successor-prep.md) - A KIPP school leadership programme that prepares new principals to lead existing KIPP schools.
* [Suite of Mathematica tools supporting grantees to develop, refine, and scale evidence-based solutions](suite-of-mathematica-tools-supporting-grantees-to-develop-refine-and-scale-evidence-based.md) - A Mathematica-developed suite of tools, including a Prior Research Table and measurement, evaluation, and design-research templates, that helps education grantees develop, refine, and scale evidence-based solutions.
* [Sukoon](sukoon.md) - A web application developed by Muhammad Fahad Bashir that combines Random Forest stress-severity classification with a culturally aware LLM chatbot to provide wellness guidance for Pakistani university students.
* [Summer Medical and Dental Education Program](summer-medical-and-dental-education-program.md) - A summer enrichment programme that prepares students from underrepresented groups to become more competitive applicants to medical and dental schools, historically administered through the Association of American Medical Colleges and partner institutions.
* [Summit](summit.md) - Summit is a computer-based arithmetic tutoring system developed by Feurzeig and White that teaches addition and subtraction through coordinated visual animation and spoken explanations in standard-algorithm and bin-model representations.
* [Superkids Reading Program](superkids-reading-program.md) - The Superkids Reading Program is a K–2 beginning-reading curriculum developed and provided by the Rowland Reading Foundation, covering phonemic awareness, phonics, vocabulary, comprehension, writing, and spelling.
* [Supplemental Educational Services](supplemental-educational-services.md) - A No Child Left Behind programme administered through states and approved public or private providers to offer free out-of-school tutoring and academic support to eligible students in low-performing schools.
* [Supplemental matrix of intervention strategies for 18 Tier 2 literacy interventions](supplemental-matrix-of-intervention-strategies-for-18-tier-2-literacy-interventions.md) - A study-developed matrix that organizes implementation strategies from 18 Tier 2 literacy interventions to help CNMI PSS staff assess feasibility and inform intervention selection or local design.
* [Supplementary spreadsheet for effect size calculations](supplementary-spreadsheet-for-effect-size-calculations.md) - A spreadsheet accompanying Lakens (2013) that calculates and recommends effect sizes for t-test and ANOVA results from user-entered study statistics.
* [SupplyNet](supplynet.md) - SupplyNet is a gamified visual simulation system developed by Yanjia Li et al.
* [Supporting Effective Educator Development](supporting-effective-educator-development.md) - A U.S.
* [Survey dataset and analysis code for digital distraction strategy mining](survey-dataset-and-analysis-code-for-digital-distraction-strategy-mining.md) - An OSF-hosted dataset of self-reported college-student survey responses and accompanying R analysis code for mining associations between online-learning strategies and digital distraction, released by Shi et al.
* [Survey of Learning Behaviors](survey-of-learning-behaviors.md) - A self-report assessment used to measure students' self-regulation, including self-monitoring and knowledge-acquisition strategies, administered before and after instruction.
* [SVLAT: Scientific Visualization Literacy Assessment Test](svlat-scientific-visualization-literacy-assessment-test.md) - SVLAT is a standardized test developed by Patrick Phuoc Do et al.
* [Symbol Math task](symbol-math-task.md) - A computer-based experimental assessment developed by Mielicki et al.
* [SYMP Test](symp-test.md) - The SYMP Test is a group-administered, timed paper-and-pencil assessment created by Brankaer et al.
* [Symphony Math](symphony-math.md) - Symphony Math is a web-browser and iPad technology intervention program that provides individualized mathematics practice for K–8 students, developed and distributed as the Symphony Math program.
* [Syntea](syntea.md) - Syntea is IU International University of Applied Sciences' AI-based learning-assistant software for question answering, interactive tutoring, personalized feedback, progress tracking, and exam preparation.
* [Synthesis Lab](synthesis-lab.md) - Synthesis Lab is a web application that integrates with social annotation tools to help learners categorize peer annotations into conceptual building blocks and develop knowledge syntheses.
* [Systems Review](systems-review.md) - Systems Review is an inquiry-based, systems-focused approach for teams working to improve a system, developed and facilitated by R15CC.

#### T {: #letter-t }

* [T-SQsrl paired teacher and student SRL questionnaires](t-sqsrl-paired-teacher-and-student-srl-questionnaires.md) - A paired teacher-and-student questionnaire instrument developed by Avivit Arvatz and colleagues to assess self-regulated-learning subprocesses and reflection through self-report and triangulated open-ended responses.
* [T3 Turnaround Teacher Teams](t3-turnaround-teacher-teams.md) - T3 Turnaround Teacher Teams is a Teach Plus and Boston Public Schools school-turnaround programme that places trained teacher leaders and coaches in low-performing schools to support shared leadership, data use, and collaborative planning.
* [TACTCorpus and TACTBench](tactcorpus-and-tactbench.md) - Dongjie Yang et al.'s released corpus, diagnostic benchmark, annotations, and model weights support reproducible research on pedagogically adaptive next-turn generation for one-to-one ESL tutoring.

* [Taklif.AI](taklifai.md) - Taklif.AI is an LLM-powered platform developed by Kurdya et al.
* [Talent Development Framework](talent-development-framework.md) - The GTL Center's Talent Development Framework Excel-based data tool lets state and district users enter human-capital data and generate charts of educator shortages and gaps across the career continuum.
* [Talent Development High School](talent-development-high-school.md) - A whole-school reform programme developed by Johns Hopkins University's Talent Development High School model to support ninth-grade students through small learning communities, double-dose curricula, extra help, and family and community involvement.
* [Talent Search](talent-search.md) - A U.S.
* [Talent Together registered teacher apprenticeship](talent-together-registered-teacher-apprenticeship.md) - Talent Together is a Michigan partnership programme that provides uncertified school employees with a paid, job-embedded route to teacher certification.
* [Talent Transfer Initiative](talent-transfer-initiative.md) - A U.S.
* [TalkingPoints](talkingpoints.md) - TalkingPoints is an education technology nonprofit's platform that provides universally designed, auto-translated two-way communication and guidance for family-school partnerships.
* [TAP System](tap-system.md) - A whole-school teacher evaluation, compensation, career-ladder, mentoring, and professional-development programme developed and operated by the National Institute for Excellence in Teaching.
* [TapaCarp](tapacarp.md) - TapaCarp is a tangible user-interface-based CSCL environment developed for classroom training of carpenter apprentices by Cuendet, Dehler-Zufferey, Ortoleva, and Dillenbourg.
* [TaraCarp tangible tabletop environment for carpenter vocational training](taracarp-tangible-tabletop-environment-for-carpenter-vocational-training.md) - TaraCarp is a tangible user-interface tabletop environment developed for vocational carpenter training, combining projected virtual artifacts with physical materials and orchestration tools.
* [Targeted Reading Instruction](targeted-reading-instruction.md) - Targeted Reading Instruction (TRI) is a one-to-one tutoring programme for struggling kindergarten and first-grade readers that provides brief daily instruction in foundational literacy skills with webcam-supported teacher coaching.
* [Tarski's World](tarskis-world.md) - Tarski's World is a computer-based learning environment developed by Barwise and Etchemendy for teaching and practicing first-order logic through visual, manipulable worlds.
* [Taxonomy Betty (Betty's Brain variant)](taxonomy-betty-bettys-brain-variant.md) - A Betty's Brain teachable-agent software variant developed by Doris B.
* [TCLAS High-Quality, Sustainable Residency Program grant](tclas-high-quality-sustainable-residency-program-grant.md) - A Texas Education Agency grant initiative that funded paid teacher-residency placements and technical assistance for districts and state-approved educator-preparation providers.
* [Teach For America](teach-for-america.md) - Teach For America runs a nationwide alternate-route teacher-preparation and teacher-placement programme that recruits and prepares corps members to teach in low-income K–12 schools.
* [Teach For America Investing in Innovation scale-up program](teach-for-america-investing-in-innovation-scale-up-program.md) - A Teach For America program that recruited, trained, and placed new corps-member teachers in high-poverty schools.
* [Teach For America Summer Training Institute](teach-for-america-summer-training-institute.md) - A branded 5- to 7-week teacher-preparation programme run by Teach For America in which corps members teach summer school under supervision while receiving coaching, feedback, and coursework.
