* [Teacher as Social Context Questionnaire (TASC) and Situations in School Questionnaire (SIS)](teacher-as-social-context-questionnaire-tasc-and-situations-in-school-questionnaire-sis.md) - The TASC and SIS are questionnaire-based assessment instruments used to measure students' or teachers' perceptions of teacher autonomy support, structure, involvement, control, and chaos in K–12 and post-secondary educational settings.
* [Teacher Assessment of College-going Climate measure](teacher-assessment-of-college-going-climate-measure.md) - A school-level survey-based measure developed by Roderick et al.
* [Teacher enjoyment scale developed for this study (5 class-referenced items)](teacher-enjoyment-scale-developed-for-this-study-5-class-referenced-items.md) - A five-item, class-referenced Likert self-report scale developed by Frenzel et al.
* [Teacher Incentive Fund](teacher-incentive-fund.md) - A U.S.
* [Teacher instrument measuring knowledge of students' operational and relational strategies](teacher-instrument-measuring-knowledge-of-students-operational-and-relational-strategies.md) - A four-item teacher instrument developed by Şimşek et al.
* [Teacher Moments](teacher-moments.md) - Teacher Moments is a free, openly licensed digital teaching-simulation platform developed by the MIT Teaching Systems Lab for immersive, scenario-based practice in teaching decisions.
* [Teacher Quality Partnership](teacher-quality-partnership.md) - A U.S.
* [Teacher residency model](teacher-residency-model.md) - A teacher-preparation programme model run by partnerships between local education agencies and institutions of higher education, combining credential coursework, a yearlong mentored clinical placement, and financial support for residents.
* [Teacher-facing chatbot authoring platform with structured configuration components and testing features](teacher-facing-chatbot-authoring-platform-with-structured-configuration-components-and-tes.md) - A web-based no-code platform developed by Riahi et al.
* [Teacher-Student Data Link](teacher-student-data-link.md) - An implementation initiative contracted by the Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation and conducted by Mathematica to help states develop best practices for linking student and teacher data to improve educational outcomes.
* [TeacherEval-2023](teachereval-2023.md) - A public corpus of 12,450 classroom-dialogue transcripts annotated with rubric-aligned teacher-performance scores and expert explanations across eight pedagogical dimensions, created by Li et al.
* [Teachers and Parents as Partners](teachers-and-parents-as-partners.md) - A University of Nebraska–Lincoln family-school partnership intervention in which a school specialist, teacher, and parent jointly identify needs, set goals, implement an individualized plan, and monitor student progress.
* [Teachers Involve Parents in Schoolwork](teachers-involve-parents-in-schoolwork.md) - Teachers Involve Parents in Schoolwork (TIPS) is a researcher- and educator-designed partnership programme that engages families through teacher-assigned interactive homework across grade levels and subjects.
* [Teachers' Sense of Efficacy Scale (TSES, 24-item long form)](teachers-sense-of-efficacy-scale-tses-24-item-long-form.md) - The Teachers' Sense of Efficacy Scale is a 24-item self-report assessment developed by Tschannen-Moran and Hoy to measure teachers' perceived efficacy in classroom management, student engagement, and instructional practices.
* [TeachFX 300-hour manually transcribed classroom audio dataset](teachfx-300-hour-manually-transcribed-classroom-audio-dataset.md) - A 300-hour manually transcribed classroom-audio dataset created by TeachFX to improve transcription models and support research on equitable automated speech recognition across diverse English dialects.
* [Teaching and Learning International Survey 2013 dataset](teaching-and-learning-international-survey-2013-dataset.md) - The OECD's TALIS 2013 dataset contains international survey data from teachers and school principals on school conditions, teaching and learning environments, professional development, and working conditions across 38 countries.
* [Teaching Fractions Toolkit](teaching-fractions-toolkit.md) - REL Midwest's Teaching Fractions Toolkit is a grade 6 mathematics professional-development and support package featuring teacher learning modules, reflection tools, formative-assessment probes, and resources for educational leaders.
* [Teaching is My Favorite Color](teaching-is-my-favorite-color.md) - A teacher-designed and teacher-led support and professional-development community for teachers of color across three southwestern Pennsylvania school districts.
* [Teaching Monster Challenge benchmark](teaching-monster-challenge-benchmark.md) - A benchmark released by Lin et al.
* [Teaching Proficiency through Reading and Storytelling (TPRS)](teaching-proficiency-through-reading-and-storytelling-tprs.md) - TPRS is a language-teaching method developed by Ray and Seely that uses comprehensible target-language input, physical-response vocabulary introduction, collaborative story creation, and reading or retelling activities for second- and foreign-language learners.
* [TeachingCoach](teachingcoach.md) - TeachingCoach is an AI-based conversational coaching system developed by Isabel Molnar and colleagues to help higher-education instructors diagnose teaching challenges, develop strategies, plan next steps, and reflect through evidence-grounded multi-turn dialogue.
* [TeachingCoach synthetic dialogue dataset (406,183 training examples)](teachingcoach-synthetic-dialogue-dataset-406183-training-examples.md) - Isabel Molnar et al.'s dataset of GPT-4o-generated, expert-filtered multi-turn instructor–consultant dialogues conditioned on teaching rules, instructor profiles, and teaching challenges.
* [Team Pennsylvania Foundation teacher and principal evaluation pilot project](team-pennsylvania-foundation-teacher-and-principal-evaluation-pilot-project.md) - A Team Pennsylvania Foundation statewide pilot that developed value-added estimates and informed the design of a comprehensive teacher and principal evaluation system in Pennsylvania.
* [TEAM Student](team-student.md) - A branded whole-school framework that centers students and families in school decisions through individualized academic, social, and emotional support and family partnership.
* [TEAMUP](teamup.md) - TEAMUP is a commercial multiplayer 3D serious game made by The Barn for training and assessing team communication, coordination, leadership, self-organisation, and collaboration.
* [Technology Education Curriculum Guide](technology-education-curriculum-guide.md) - A curriculum guide issued by an education authority that specifies technology-education content, instructional approaches, course sequences, and time allocations for junior- and senior-high programmes.
* [Technology Integration Matrix (TIM)](technology-integration-matrix-tim.md) - The Technology Integration Matrix is a framework developed by the Florida Center for Instructional Technology at the University of South Florida for describing learning-environment characteristics and levels of classroom technology integration.
* [TECTA: Tennessee Early Childhood Training Alliance](tecta-tennessee-early-childhood-training-alliance.md) - A Tennessee statewide programme that provides childcare providers and administrators with free orientation training, professional development, credential support, and tuition assistance through participating public higher-education institutions.
* [TEEMSS](teemss.md) - TEEMSS is a Concord Consortium physical-science curriculum for grades 3–8 that combines inquiry-based investigations with handheld computers, sensors, interactive models, and teacher reporting tools.
* [TEEMSS sound unit test](teemss-sound-unit-test.md) - The TEEMSS sound unit test is a nine-item, standards-aligned science assessment developed by the TEEMSS researchers to measure elementary and middle school students' understanding of sound before and after instruction.
* [TEFA](tefa.md) - TEFA is a technology-enhanced formative-assessment pedagogy developed by Ian D.
* [TEFA-T](tefa-t.md) - A teaching-factory instructional framework developed for automotive vocational education that integrates vehicle troubleshooting with a nine-step work-process syntax and supporting instructional materials.
* [Tekster](tekster.md) - Tekster is a 16-lesson, strategy-focused writing instruction curriculum for Grades 4–6, developed by Bouwer and colleagues for Dutch general education classrooms and delivered through student workbooks, a teacher manual, teacher training, and modeling videos.
* [TELESCOPE](telescope.md) - TELESCOPE is a telehealth-based lung-cancer-screening shared-decision-making coaching and patient-navigation programme developed and delivered by trained patient navigators for primary-care patients.
* [Ten Aspects of Community framework](ten-aspects-of-community-framework.md) - A Forum for Youth Investment framework that categorizes community features and assets affecting youth across ten domains.
* [Ten-item Greek-language trust questionnaire assembled from established HRI trust scales with Lawshe content validation](ten-item-greek-language-trust-questionnaire-assembled-from-established-hri-trust-scales-wi.md) - A 10-item Greek-language Likert questionnaire developed by Anna-Maria Velentza et al.
* [Tennessee Educator Preparation Report Card](tennessee-educator-preparation-report-card.md) - A statewide accountability and reporting instrument for educator preparation providers, produced and administered by the Tennessee State Board of Education, Tennessee Department of Education, and Tennessee Higher Education Commission.
* [TenSquare's Data-Driven School Improvement Process](tensquares-data-driven-school-improvement-process.md) - A branded continuous-improvement programme developed and run by TenSquare to audit schools, create multi-year redesign plans, implement evidence-based interventions, and monitor and adjust improvement efforts.
* [Terracotta](terracotta.md) - Terracotta is a software tool developed by the article's researchers that integrates with Canvas to automate consent, random assignment, data collection, and de-identified exports for embedded learning experiments.
* [TerraFlops](terraflops.md) - TerraFlops is an open-source wrapper described by Eimler et al.
* [Test Boredom Scale (TBS-Trait and TBS-State)](test-boredom-scale-tbs-trait-and-tbs-state.md) - A multi-item self-report assessment developed by Goetz et al.
* [Test of Spatial Assembly (TOSA)](test-of-spatial-assembly-tosa.md) - The TOSA is an individually administered 2-D and 3-D spatial assembly assessment developed by Verdine et al.
* [Test of the rational-number central conceptual structure with capacity-graded item subsets](test-of-the-rational-number-central-conceptual-structure-with-capacity-graded-item-subsets.md) - A study-developed assessment by Morra et al.
* [Tests A, B, C, and D: four diagnostic tests for the fundamental geometry abilities](tests-a-b-c-and-d-four-diagnostic-tests-for-the-fundamental-geometry-abilities.md) - A paper-and-pencil diagnostic assessment battery developed by Minnick for high-school geometry pupils to identify weaknesses in the abilities underlying geometric proof.
* [TFT partnership](tft-partnership.md) - A REL Midwest partnership framework that organizes professional-learning activities and expected administrator, teacher, and student outcomes across short-, medium-, and long-term horizons.
* [The 'Card list'](the-card-list.md) - A carbon-duplicate perforated ranking form designed by Stenquist for supervisors and other judges to rank workers or teachers on vocational and moral traits.
* [The 10 Elements of Black Excellence](the-10-elements-of-black-excellence.md) - A community-informed framework developed by LBUSD's Center to organize its foundation, aims, and practical approaches for advancing Black excellence.
* [The 33-exercise scientific method test in geography content](the-33-exercise-scientific-method-test-in-geography-content.md) - A paper-and-pencil geography assessment by Herring containing 33 multiple-option exercises designed to measure pupils' ability to use elements of the scientific method rather than recall geography content.
* [The 3E framework](the-3e-framework.md) - The 3E framework is a rubric-based assessment developed by Masum Hasan et al.
* [The AI Assessment Scale (AIAS)](the-ai-assessment-scale-aias.md) - A scale-based framework developed by Perkins et al.
* [The AI House](the-ai-house.md) - A four-layer pedagogical framework developed by Vinicius Santana Gomes for training public-sector employees to use generative AI safely, practically, and in compliance with legal and governance requirements.
* [The American Public School (Finney)](the-american-public-school-finney.md) - A Macmillan survey textbook for normal school students covering the historical development of American public education and major European educational influences.
* [The Arc's Self-Determination Scale](the-arcs-self-determination-scale.md) - A 72-item self-report assessment developed by Michael Wehmeyer for measuring global self-determination and its autonomy, self-regulation, psychological empowerment, and self-realization dimensions in adolescents with cognitive disabilities.
* [The Binet Point Scale](the-binet-point-scale.md) - A paper scoring reorganization of the Stanford Revision of the Binet Scale developed by Arthuk S.
* [The Bullying Prevention Pack (BPP)](the-bullying-prevention-pack-bpp.md) - A five-week, teacher-led primary-school bullying-prevention programme developed by Peadar Donohoe and colleagues that uses discussions, literature-based definitions, role-play, defending-behaviour coaching, an anti-bullying contract, and monthly follow-up check-ups.
* [The Collaborative](the-collaborative.md) - The Collaborative is a branded initiative run by its organizing collaborative to support state leaders through participatory design, relationship-building, collaboration, and leadership development.
* [The Congruency-based Metacognition Scale (CMS)](the-congruency-based-metacognition-scale-cms.md) - A paired child–parent questionnaire developed by dos Santos Kawata et al.
* [The Constructive Ability Test](the-constructive-ability-test.md) - A standardized block-building performance test developed by Kelley to assess children's constructive and executive ability, initiative, and manipulative skill through objectively graded self-directed structures.
* [the design helper](the-design-helper.md) - Michael Eckersley's design-helper computer programs generate graphical concepts and patterns that students use to complete images related to specific design questions.
* [The Electric Company Summer Learning Program](the-electric-company-summer-learning-program.md) - The Electric Company Summer Learning Program is a Sesame Workshop transmedia summer curriculum package combining Electric Company video episodes, scripted group activities, and the Prankster Planet online game for children ages 6 to 8.
* [The Evaluation Toolkit](the-evaluation-toolkit.md) - A Benton Foundation and EDC Center for Children and Technology toolkit of instruments that helps teachers assess technology-rich learning activities, student skill development, learning, and media literacy.
* [The Expert Mathematician](the-expert-mathematician.md) - A three-year middle-school mathematics curriculum developed by the program's creators that combines Logo programming software with consumable print materials across general mathematics, pre-algebra, and algebra I.
* [The K-16 Square](the-k-16-square.md) - A K-16 education-system alignment framework drafted by state education CEOs with the National Association of System Heads and The Education Trust to coordinate standards for graduation, college admission, teaching, and teacher preparation.
* [The Language Inquiry](the-language-inquiry.md) - Frogner's 100-item language-attitude questionnaire helps teachers assess their own and their students' attitudes toward language by comparing responses with benchmark judgments from ten linguists.
* [The Leandro sound basic education framework](the-leandro-sound-basic-education-framework.md) - A North Carolina constitutional education framework defining the components of a sound basic education and guiding state action, accountability, and resource recommendations for K–12 public education.
* [The Meta-Attention Knowledge Questionnaire (MAKQ)](the-meta-attention-knowledge-questionnaire-makq.md) - A self-report and open-ended questionnaire developed by Mulvihill et al.
* [The Nomads](the-nomads.md) - The Nomads is a 3D mathematics game developed by Jiaqi Yu and colleagues to train adaptive expertise in rational-number arithmetic through resource management, trading, and other variable-answer tasks.
* [The Opportunity Atlas](the-opportunity-atlas.md) - A publicly available tract-level dataset and interactive mapping tool created by Raj Chetty and collaborators at Opportunity Insights, providing estimates of children's long-term outcomes across U.S.
* [The Pedagogy Benchmark](the-pedagogy-benchmark.md) - A publicly released dataset developed by Lelièvre et al.
* [The Pressey 'Cross-out' brief group intelligence scale for grades 4-8](the-pressey-cross-out-brief-group-intelligence-scale-for-grades-4-8.md) - A four-test group intelligence assessment developed by Pressey for measuring general ability in grades 4–8 and preliminarily identifying pupils for further individual study.
* [The Reading Edge](the-reading-edge.md) - The Reading Edge is a whole-school cooperative-learning reading programme adapted from Student Team Reading and delivered by classroom teachers during regular language-arts instruction.
* [The Region 15 FACE Collaborative](the-region-15-face-collaborative.md) - A Region 15 Comprehensive Center initiative that convenes state education agency family and community engagement leaders across Arizona, California, Nevada, and Utah to share practices, learn from experts, and develop state-level projects.
* [The River conceptual model](the-river-conceptual-model.md) - A Flash-based interactive geography learning object developed by Daniel Churchill that lets secondary students manipulate river parameters, calculate discharge, and visualize river-flow relationships across handheld and other presentation devices.
* [The Scaffolded AI Literacy (SAIL) framework](the-scaffolded-ai-literacy-sail-framework.md) - A Delphi-developed framework by MacCallum et al.
* [The Scholastic Quotient (S. Q.)](the-scholastic-quotient-s-q.md) - A historical scholastic-ranking assessment formula devised by Cummins that weights pupils' A–D marks from school records to estimate and classify scholastic standing.
* [The supplemented Thorndike drawing scale of snow scenes with human figures in action](the-supplemented-thorndike-drawing-scale-of-snow-scenes-with-human-figures-in-action.md) - A norm-referenced rating scale developed by Childs for judging elementary and secondary pupils' snow-scene drawings by comparison with anchored sample drawings and averaged ratings from two judges.
* [the Toolkit](the-toolkit.md) - The Toolkit is a four-part framework created by its authors to help teachers plan, implement, and evaluate technology-based lessons.
* [The Triple-Focus Program](the-triple-focus-program.md) - The Triple-Focus Program is a pull-out reading-disability intervention developed by Lovett and colleagues that combines PHAST decoding and comprehension strategy instruction with RAVE-O lexical enrichment.
* [The Writing Road to Reading](the-writing-road-to-reading.md) - Spalding's The Writing Road to Reading is a comprehensive K–6 speech-to-print literacy curriculum developed and provided by Spalding Education International, integrating explicit instruction in phonemic awareness, phonics, spelling, writing, and reading comprehension.
* [Thermos](thermos.md) - Thermos is a student-facing learning analytics dashboard developed to visualize motivation, engagement, group-work skills, and study progress while offering actionable self-regulation support and connection suggestions.
* [Thinking Math!](thinking-math.md) - Innovamat's PreK–8 mathematics curriculum package combines socio-constructivist lessons, manipulative materials, student logbooks, digital planning and progress monitoring, adaptive practice, and professional development.
* [thinkSRSD](thinksrsd.md) - thinkSRSD is an online resource providing free materials to help educators and school districts implement Self-Regulated Strategy Development, with optional paid online and in-person professional development.
* [Thomson's Hindustani Test and Ballard's Cipher, Orientation, and Cryptogram Tests](thomsons-hindustani-test-and-ballards-cipher-orientation-and-cryptogram-tests.md) - A set of English group-administered intelligence-test instruments developed by Thomson and Ballard for school-age examinees.
* [Thonny Log Visualizer](thonny-log-visualizer.md) - A teacher-facing web application developed by Heidi Taveter et al.
* [Thorndike's objective scale for measuring handwriting quality](thorndikes-objective-scale-for-measuring-handwriting-quality.md) - A standardized handwriting-quality assessment scale developed by Edward L.
* [Three open-ended survey questions assessing CT practices in physics contexts](three-open-ended-survey-questions-assessing-ct-practices-in-physics-contexts.md) - A three-question open-ended survey instrument developed by Sean Savage et al.
* [Three-category framework for organizing school mental health and well-being supports](three-category-framework-for-organizing-school-mental-health-and-well-being-supports.md) - A National Charter School Resource Center framework that helps public charter-school leaders organize and assess school mental-health, well-being, and SEL supports across three categories.
* [Three-component framework for high-quality, equitable kindergarten entry assessments: content, administration, and validity](three-component-framework-for-high-quality-equitable-kindergarten-entry-assessments-conten.md) - A Yun et al.
* [Three-component framework for Portrait of a Graduate assessment systems](three-component-framework-for-portrait-of-a-graduate-assessment-systems.md) - A framework developed by Kyle Dunbar et al.
* [Three-dimensional framework for assessing problem posing ability: number, category, and quality](three-dimensional-framework-for-assessing-problem-posing-ability-number-category-and-quali.md) - A Dai et al.
* [Three-layer reusable pipeline for generating 3D-printed tactile statistical graphs](three-layer-reusable-pipeline-for-generating-3d-printed-tactile-statistical-graphs.md) - An open-source JavaScript pipeline by Lawrence Obiuwevwi et al.
* [Three-level RTI prevention model for identifying learning disabilities](three-level-rti-prevention-model-for-identifying-learning-disabilities.md) - A response-to-intervention framework that organizes general instruction, targeted tutoring, and special education evaluation around students' measured progress and responsiveness.
* [Three-minute acuity-sustain test of the eye's power to sustain clear seeing under lighting conditions](three-minute-acuity-sustain-test-of-the-eyes-power-to-sustain-clear-seeing-under-lighting.md) - A laboratory visual-acuity assessment developed by C.
* [TibetCPR](tibetcpr.md) - A low-cost self-guided CPR training system developed by Yibo Meng and colleagues that combines a sensor-equipped mannequin, electrotactile and visual performance feedback, and a Tibetan-language narrative game.
* [Time Explorers](time-explorers.md) - A mobile augmented-reality app developed by Heather Toomey Zimmerman and colleagues for family, place-based learning about Appalachian geological history.
* [Tips by Text](tips-by-text.md) - Tips by Text is a school-delivered text-message programme that gives parents of young children weekly information, home-activity suggestions, and developmental encouragement to support language, literacy, numeracy, and socioemotional learning.
* [TNTP Turnaround Leadership Teams Strategy (TLTS)](tntp-turnaround-leadership-teams-strategy-tlts.md) - A TNTP school-improvement programme that diagnoses school needs and develops and supports school leadership teams to strengthen leadership, instruction, school culture, and family engagement.
* [To&Through Project](tothrough-project.md) - The To&Through Project at the UChicago Consortium on School Research tracks Chicago Public Schools students from high school through college and publishes the data in public online tools.
* [Too Good for Drugs](too-good-for-drugs.md) - Too Good for Drugs is a classroom-delivered K–8 drug-prevention and character-education curriculum developed by the Mendez Foundation, with scripted lessons, role-play, cooperative activities, and skill-building exercises.
* [Too Good for Drugs and Violence](too-good-for-drugs-and-violence.md) - A school-based character education curriculum developed by the Mendez Foundation for promoting prosocial skills, positive character traits, and drug- and violence-free norms among students.
* [Too Good for Violence](too-good-for-violence.md) - A scripted K–8 school-based character-education curriculum developed by the Mendez Foundation, using role-playing, cooperative learning, group activities, and classroom discussions to promote character values and social-emotional skills.
* [Tool #2: Auditing Your Data Collection for Indicators of Distress](tool-2-auditing-your-data-collection-for-indicators-of-distress.md) - A structured worksheet for school authorizers to audit how they collect data on indicators of school distress and identify gaps in those processes.
* [Tool #3: Assessing Schools for Indicators of Distress rating scales](tool-3-assessing-schools-for-indicators-of-distress-rating-scales.md) - Aimee Evan et al.'s rating-scale assessment helps charter school authorizers and schools evaluate organizational indicators of school distress using school-level evidence and reflection prompts.
* [Tool for Representational Balance in Books](tool-for-representational-balance-in-books.md) - A 10-criterion rubric created by The Education Trust that guides readers in assessing how people, cultures, and topics are represented in books.
* [Toolkit for Building a Virtual Learning Cohort](toolkit-for-building-a-virtual-learning-cohort.md) - A Mathematica toolkit that helps facilitators plan and evaluate virtual learning cohorts through guiding values and ten transferable planning tools.
* [Toolkit of guides and templates for launching and sustaining an intermediary-led cohort of strategic learning grantees](toolkit-of-guides-and-templates-for-launching-and-sustaining-an-intermediary-led-cohort-of.md) - A Mathematica toolkit of adaptable guides and templates for designing and facilitating meetings and for developing theories of change, collecting data, and synthesizing findings in strategic learning initiatives.
* [TOP Guidelines](top-guidelines.md) - The Transparency and Openness Promotion (TOP) Guidelines are a policy framework developed by social scientists, editors, and funders to guide journals and funding agencies in adopting standards for transparent, preregistered, reproducible research.
* [Topic Checklist (TOC)](topic-checklist-toc.md) - A University of Limburg self-report instrument in which students rate time spent and perceived mastery across intended course topics to measure actual self-directed learning activities.
* [Tough As Nails, Nimble Fingers (TAN) virtual computing PD project](tough-as-nails-nimble-fingers-tan-virtual-computing-pd-project.md) - An NSF-funded Digital Promise and Bit Source initiative delivering virtual K–8 computational-thinking professional development, including CT Booster sessions and micro-credentials, in rural Kentucky.

* [TPB-based student questionnaire predicting school-year completion intentions](tpb-based-student-questionnaire-predicting-school-year-completion-intentions.md) - A questionnaire developed by Davis et al.

* [TRACE-grade-prediction training code and anonymized dataset release](trace-grade-prediction-training-code-and-anonymized-dataset-release.md) - Paul Savala's TRACE release provides training code and an anonymized subset of enrollment records for retraining and replicating grade and course prediction models at other institutions.

* [TRAIN longitudinal dataset (Grades 5-7, German nonacademic-track secondary schools)](train-longitudinal-dataset-grades-5-7-german-nonacademic-track-secondary-schools.md) - A longitudinal dataset from the Transition and Innovation (TRAIN) study containing standardized mathematics and German achievement, subject interest, differentiated-instruction, and academic-orientation measures for students in German nonacademic-track secondary schools.
* [Training a Multicultural Workforce](training-a-multicultural-workforce.md) - A 30-hour, six-module supervisor-training curriculum developed by Dyer for ESL-qualified instructors to help Australian workplace supervisors manage and train a multicultural workforce, including refugees and immigrants from non-English-speaking backgrounds.
* [Trait Emotional Intelligence Questionnaire (TEIQue, 30 items, four factors)](trait-emotional-intelligence-questionnaire-teique-30-items-four-factors.md) - A 30-item self-report questionnaire measuring 15 facets of trait emotional intelligence grouped into Emotionality, Sociability, Well-Being, and Self-Control, developed by Petrides and colleagues.
* [Transcend's Designing for Learning framework](transcends-designing-for-learning-framework.md) - A Transcend-developed framework and accompanying primer and card deck that organize professional learning around individual variability, identity, motivation, and cognition.
* [TransplantQA corpus](transplantqa-corpus.md) - A corpus of 102 U.S.
* [Trial Spanish-language NAEP mathematics assessment for Puerto Rico (2003 and 2005)](trial-spanish-language-naep-mathematics-assessment-for-puerto-rico-2003-and-2005.md) - A Spanish-language trial version of the National Assessment of Educational Progress mathematics assessment administered by the National Center for Education Statistics to public-school students in grades 4 and 8 in Puerto Rico.
* [TSDL](tsdl.md) - A state education agency’s linked administrative data system connecting teachers to students by course for policy implementation, educator-preparation evaluation, and analysis of student growth.
* [TSMTDF: form for determining objective-oriented taxonomy, strategy, and method-techniques with scoring rubric](tsmtdf-form-for-determining-objective-oriented-taxonomy-strategy-and-method-techniques-wit.md) - A researcher-developed open-ended form and rubric by Kaygısız et al.
* [TU/e CS overlap matrix and LU Erasmus+ decision set](tue-cs-overlap-matrix-and-lu-erasmus-decision-set.md) - A paired dataset comprising approved Computer Science course-overlap pairs from TU/e and anonymized Erasmus+ course-transfer decisions from Leiden University, assembled by Arthur Nijdam et al.
* [Turkish adaptation of Dweck's Goal Orientation Scale](turkish-adaptation-of-dwecks-goal-orientation-scale.md) - A Turkish-language adaptation of Dweck's four-item scale measuring students' learning goal orientation and performance goal orientation.
* [Turnaround Leader Competencies](turnaround-leader-competencies.md) - A Center on School Turnaround at WestEd competency framework identifying high-impact motives, habits, and behaviors for developing leaders responsible for rapid improvement in persistently low-performing schools.
* [TurnNJ](turnnj.md) - TurnNJ is Uncommon Schools' whole-school turnaround programme, implemented in schools in Camden and Newark to extend its school-improvement model to existing schools.
* [TURTLEAI benchmark](turtleai-benchmark.md) - A publicly released benchmark comprising Turtle Graphics programming-task datasets, code-evaluation tools, and synthetic-data generation methods, developed by Chao Wen et al.
* [Tutor CoPilot](tutor-copilot.md) - Tutor CoPilot is a human-AI tutoring-support tool developed by Wang and colleagues that provides customizable, real-time expert-like guidance to tutors during K–12 mathematics sessions.
* [TutorBench](tutorbench.md) - TutorBench is a source-grounded benchmark dataset created by Bingxi Zhao et al.
* [Tutoring with the Lightning Squad](tutoring-with-the-lightning-squad.md) - Tutoring with the Lightning Squad is a web-based, game-like small-group reading intervention for grades 1–5 that provides paired decoding and fluency tutoring, adaptive activities, and progress monitoring.
* [TutorTrace](tutortrace.md) - TutorTrace is a dataset and taxonomy developed by its authors that uses IDE telemetry to classify introductory Python learners' behavioral states around AI help-seeking and support tutor interventions.
* [TutorTrace dataset of IDE telemetry, behavioral segments, and observable metrics from 480 learners across four introductory Python deployments](tutortrace-dataset-of-ide-telemetry-behavioral-segments-and-observable-metrics-from-480-le.md) - TutorTrace is a publicly released dataset created by its authors that combines IDE telemetry, AI interaction logs, task outcomes, and expert-validated learner behavioral classifications for studying AI-assisted programming help-seeking.
* [Twelve Together](twelve-together.md) - A one-year voluntary peer-support and mentoring programme for middle- and high-school students, delivered through facilitated discussion groups and complementary academic, social, and college-oriented activities.
* [Twelve-strategy daily-diary emotion regulation measure across two measurement bursts](twelve-strategy-daily-diary-emotion-regulation-measure-across-two-measurement-bursts.md) - A study-developed daily-diary assessment measuring German primary and secondary school teachers’ use of 12 emotion-regulation strategies across two six-month-spaced measurement bursts.
* [Twig Science](twig-science.md) - Twig Science is Twig Education's phenomena-based K–8 science curriculum aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards and built around the 5E instructional model.
* [Type 1 model competency test](type-1-model-competency-test.md) - A paper-and-pencil grade-level competency assessment developed by Kasten et al., with scoring guidance, teacher notes, objective mappings, and error-interpretation support.
* [Type 2 checklist assessment](type-2-checklist-assessment.md) - A first-grade mathematics checklist assessment from the Model Competency-Based Mathematics Program that pairs critical objectives with teacher-rated performance tasks to document student progress.

#### U {: #letter-u }

* [UC Berkeley course transaction dataset and publicly released analysis code](uc-berkeley-course-transaction-dataset-and-publicly-released-analysis-code.md) - A UC Berkeley dataset of over 11 million timestamped student course-enrollment transactions from Fall 2016 through Spring 2022, released with public analysis code by Borchers et al.
* [UChicago Consortium's research-practice partnership model](uchicago-consortiums-research-practice-partnership-model.md) - A University of Chicago Consortium framework that treats research as public, relational, and adaptive infrastructure through long-term, place-based, reciprocal partnerships with education practitioners and communities.
* [UCLA IMPACT ECE/Multiple Subject Program](ucla-impact-ecemultiple-subject-program.md) - A UCLA Center X and Los Angeles Unified School District teacher-residency programme that prepared early-childhood and multiple-subject teachers through integrated graduate coursework, certification pathways, intensive clinical placements, and mentored practice.
* [UCSMP secondary mathematics curriculum](ucsmp-secondary-mathematics-curriculum.md) - A University of Chicago School Mathematics Project curriculum package for secondary mathematics courses, including student textbooks, teacher editions and resources, assessment materials, and technology resources.
* [UFOV test software (Visual Awareness Research Group)](ufov-test-software-visual-awareness-research-group.md) - A commercially available personal-computer implementation of the Useful Field of View test, developed and provided by the Visual Awareness Research Group to assess visual processing speed and attention.
* [UNESCO AI Competency Framework for School Students](unesco-ai-competency-framework-for-school-students.md) - A UNESCO framework that organizes 12 AI competencies for school students into four dimensions: human-centred mindset, ethics of AI, AI techniques and applications, and AI system design.
* [UNESCO AI Competency Framework for Teachers](unesco-ai-competency-framework-for-teachers.md) - A UNESCO framework specifying 15 AI-related teacher competencies across five dimensions to guide national competency frameworks and teacher training programmes.
* [UNESCO Recommendation on the Ethics of Artificial Intelligence](unesco-recommendation-on-the-ethics-of-artificial-intelligence.md) - A UNESCO global standard-setting framework that provides ethical principles and policy guidance for governments, educators, learners, and other stakeholders implementing and governing artificial intelligence.
* [UNESCO's eight-component GenAI framework](unescos-eight-component-genai-framework.md) - A UNESCO framework for evaluating whether generative-AI policies address ethical governance, inclusion, equity, access, gender, sustainability, and related responsible-use considerations in education.
* [UNIPS](unips.md) - UNIPS is a Finnish national online platform developed collaboratively by eight universities to provide self-study and guided short modules in university pedagogy for university staff and doctoral students.
* [Universal Design for Learning (UDL) framework](universal-design-for-learning-udl-framework.md) - CAST's framework for designing flexible instruction around learner variability through multiple means of Engagement, Representation, and Action & Expression.
* [University of Central Florida Center for Community Schools certification system for the Community Partnership Schools model](university-of-central-florida-center-for-community-schools-certification-system-for-the-co.md) - A UCF Center for Community Schools certification and technical-assistance programme that helps Florida schools implement and demonstrate fidelity to the Community Partnership Schools model through standards, self-assessment, coaching, mentoring, and external review.
* [University of Virginia Partnership for Leaders in Education turnaround competency model](university-of-virginia-partnership-for-leaders-in-education-turnaround-competency-model.md) - A seven-competency, four-level proficiency framework developed by the University of Virginia Partnership for Leaders in Education to assess and develop school leaders in turnaround contexts.
* [UpGrade](upgrade.md) - UpGrade is a free, open-source platform developed for conducting field trials in educational technology software, including experiments integrated with Carnegie Learning's MATHia adaptive mathematics tutoring system.
* [Upward Bound Math-Science (UBMS)](upward-bound-math-science-ubms.md) - A U.S.
* [Urban Science](urban-science.md) - Urban Science is a University of Wisconsin–Madison epistemic-game programme that engages learners in individual and collaborative urban-redevelopment planning through professional-practicum-like tasks and deliverables.
* [USDOL-ETA General Competency Model Framework](usdol-eta-general-competency-model-framework.md) - A U.S.
* [User experience design tools for courseware: persona creation kit, journey maps, and ecosystem maps](user-experience-design-tools-for-courseware-persona-creation-kit-journey-maps-and-ecosyste.md) - WestEd's freely available suite of participant-data-informed tools helps postsecondary gateway-math courseware designers examine learner and faculty needs, experiences, and ecosystems.
* [UTAUT2-inspired 15-item survey instrument for assessing student perceptions of ChatGPT-assisted activities](utaut2-inspired-15-item-survey-instrument-for-assessing-student-perceptions-of-chatgpt-ass.md) - A study-developed post-activity survey by Elizondo-García et al.
* [Uzgiris-Hunt ordinal scales of infant psychological development](uzgiris-hunt-ordinal-scales-of-infant-psychological-development.md) - A set of six sequential ordinal scales developed by C.

#### V {: #letter-v }

* [Validated 192-respondent GenAI chatbot adoption dataset with 13 clustering variables](validated-192-respondent-genai-chatbot-adoption-dataset-with-13-clustering-variables.md) - A Qualtrics-based dataset of 192 higher-education students' and educators' GenAI chatbot perceptions and experiences, standardized across 13 variables for clustering into adoption personas.
* [Validated AI-TPACK scale for mathematics teacher education students](validated-ai-tpack-scale-for-mathematics-teacher-education-students.md) - A 24-item, six-variable, seven-point Likert self-report scale developed by Meijuan Xie and colleagues to assess mathematics teacher education students' AI-TPACK competence, self-efficacy, and teaching beliefs.
* [Validated measure of the whole-number central conceptual structure](validated-measure-of-the-whole-number-central-conceptual-structure.md) - A validated instrument developed by Morra et al.
* [Valor Collegiate Academy Compass Model](valor-collegiate-academy-compass-model.md) - Valor Collegiate Academy's whole-school SEL and mental-health programme uses five interdependent domains, developmental badges, and weekly mentor-facilitated Compass Circles for students in Grades 5–12.
* [Value-Added Assessment System for DC Schools and Teachers](value-added-assessment-system-for-dc-schools-and-teachers.md) - A district accountability assessment system prepared for and run by District of Columbia Public Schools to estimate school and teacher value added.
* [Vantage](vantage.md) - Vantage is a virtual assessment platform developed by Globerson et al.
* [VARIA benchmark package with follow-up harness](varia-benchmark-package-with-follow-up-harness.md) - Ernesto Lee and colleagues' VARIA package provides assessment-variant blueprints, rubrics, canonical solutions, prompting strategies, evaluation metrics, and runnable follow-up analysis code for benchmarking LLM-generated construct-equivalent assessment variants.
* [VERA (Virtual Experimentation Research Assistant) ecological modeling tool](vera-virtual-experimentation-research-assistant-ecological-modeling-tool.md) - A publicly available online ecological modeling tool hosted through the Smithsonian's Encyclopedia of Life that supports conceptual modeling, agent-based simulation, hypothesis-driven experimentation, and model–simulate–refine activities.
* [Verified teacher-subject-student link data](verified-teacher-subject-student-link-data.md) - A dataset of teacher-verified mathematics and English/language arts subject and student links used to assess the accuracy of administrative teacher–student linkage data and examine departmentalization.
* [VeriForge](veriforge.md) - VeriForge is a mixed-initiative writing environment developed by Ruqi Sun et al.
* [Verizon Innovative Learning Schools](verizon-innovative-learning-schools.md) - A Verizon and Digital Promise programme that equips Title I middle and high schools with devices, connectivity, digital-skills training, STEM curriculum, and dedicated learning-coach support.
* [Vibe Compiler](vibe-compiler.md) - Vibe Compiler is a NotebookLM- and Gemini-based research-logic compilation tool developed by Riichiro Mizoguchi and colleagues to help researchers turn vague intuitions into coherent paper logic while preserving their epistemic agency.
* [ViBSE (Virtual Baltic Sea Explorer)](vibse-virtual-baltic-sea-explorer.md) - A web-based virtual expedition and laboratory environment designed by education, biology, and chemistry professionals for interdisciplinary inquiry into environmental change using authentic Baltic Sea research materials and data.
* [vICC R package](vicc-r-package.md) - The vICC R package is an R software implementation of Bayesian tests for within-person variance homogeneity and membership models, released by the article's authors for researchers analyzing repeated-measures data.
* [Videodisc authoring and production system (VAPS)](videodisc-authoring-and-production-system-vaps.md) - VAPS is an Army-oriented hardware and software system for authoring, producing, editing, testing, mastering, and replicating interactive videodisc training materials.
* [Virginia RETHINKS Health Sciences Education initiative](virginia-rethinks-health-sciences-education-initiative.md) - A TAACCCT-funded initiative organized across Virginia's 23 community colleges to redesign health-sciences education, strengthen workforce connections, and improve college efficiencies.
* [Virtual Collaborative Research Institute (VCRI) CSCL environment](virtual-collaborative-research-institute-vcri-cscl-environment.md) - A groupware environment developed to support collaborative learning, inquiry tasks, and research projects through shared communication, information, argumentation, and co-writing tools.
* [Virtual professional learning community (vPLC)](virtual-professional-learning-community-vplc.md) - The vPLC is a scalable professional-learning programme run by the ASSISTments initiative to help teachers, especially in rural and underresourced settings, implement ASSISTments effectively.
* [Virtually Connecting](virtually-connecting.md) - Virtually Connecting is a social-justice-oriented open educational practice run by founders and volunteers that facilitates conversations between conference attendees and people unable to attend.
* [VisA instrument (Visualization and Accuracy)](visa-instrument-visualization-and-accuracy.md) - A paper-and-pencil, group-administered assessment developed by Jacobse et al.
* [VISIGARP](visigarp.md) - VISIGARP is a domain-independent SWI-PROLOG/XPCE educational tool that generates interactive diagrams from GARP qualitative simulations so students can inspect system states, transitions, causal dependencies, and entity relations.
* [Vision to Learn (VTL)](vision-to-learn-vtl.md) - Vision to Learn is a nonprofit school-based vision programme that provides low-income students with screenings, comprehensive eye examinations, and free corrective glasses through mobile clinics and partner schools.
* [VISMATIC](vismatic.md) - A secured, low-cost JupyterHub sandbox and process-monitoring framework developed by Germán Arroyo et al.
* [Visual immediate-memory test of twelve consonants used as a fatigue measure in day school children](visual-immediate-memory-test-of-twelve-consonants-used-as-a-fatigue-measure-in-day-school.md) - A timed visual immediate-memory assessment developed and used by W.
* [Visual4Math](visual4math.md) - Visual4Math is a web-based prototype developed by Zhengxu Li et al.
* [VITAL (Visibly Improve Teaching and Learning) professional learning service](vital-visibly-improve-teaching-and-learning-professional-learning-service.md) - WestEd's VITAL service builds K–12 teachers' capacity for collaborative inquiry through structured teaching-and-learning cycles, peer feedback, reflection, and analysis of student and teacher learning.
* [Vocabulary gloss questionnaire (24 items on gloss exposure and perceived effectiveness)](vocabulary-gloss-questionnaire-24-items-on-gloss-exposure-and-perceived-effectiveness.md) - A Chinese-language 24-item questionnaire developed by Cheng et al.
* [Vocabulary Improvement Program (VIP)](vocabulary-improvement-program-vip.md) - A 15-week vocabulary-development curriculum for English language learners and native English speakers in grades 4–6, developed by Diane August, Maria S.
* [Vocabulary Knowledge Scale (VKS)](vocabulary-knowledge-scale-vks.md) - A researcher-developed vocabulary knowledge assessment created by Paribakht and Wesche to track learners’ acquisition and development of targeted words.
* [Vocabulary Levels Test (Schmitt, Schmitt & Clapham, 2001)](vocabulary-levels-test-schmitt-schmitt-clapham-2001.md) - A frequency-level receptive vocabulary assessment developed by Norbert Schmitt, Diane Schmitt, and Clapham that measures knowledge of words from 2000-, 3000-, 5000-, and 10000-word frequency bands plus academic vocabulary.
* [VoicePod audio prompting device](voicepod-audio-prompting-device.md) - VoicePod is a portable digital recording-and-playback system made by Attainment Company Inc.
* [Voyager Universal Literacy System®](voyager-universal-literacy-system.md) - Voyager Universal Literacy System® is a core reading curriculum developed for students through grade three, combining teacher-led individual, small-group, and whole-class literacy instruction with computer-based practice in phonological skills, comprehension, fluency, language development, and writi
* [VTDS](vtds.md) - A computer-controlled videodisc training system described by Bunderson et al.

* [Washington's Human-AI-Human (H-AI-H) model](washingtons-human-ai-human-h-ai-h-model.md) - A Washington State education-guidance framework that positions AI between human inquiry and human reflection, editing, and understanding so that it augments rather than replaces human judgment.
* [Washington, DC's Early Childhood Educator Pay Equity Fund (PEF)](washington-dcs-early-childhood-educator-pay-equity-fund-pef.md) - A Washington, DC initiative that provides funding to increase compensation for early childhood educators and reduce pay disparities with K–12 teachers.

* [Watson–Glaser Critical Thinking Appraisal (W-GCTA)](watsonglaser-critical-thinking-appraisal-w-gcta.md) - A paper-and-pencil standardized assessment developed by Watson and Glaser that measures critical-thinking subskills including inference, assumption recognition, deduction, interpretation, and argument evaluation.

* [Web-based GAI chatbot with instructor-facing learning analytics dashboard](web-based-gai-chatbot-with-instructor-facing-learning-analytics-dashboard.md) - A login-based web application built with OpenAI's Assistants API that provides course-specific Socratic tutoring and instructors with dashboards for analyzing student conversations and engagement.

* [WestEd Center on School Turnaround Journeys Project](wested-center-on-school-turnaround-journeys-project.md) - A WestEd Center on School Turnaround initiative that follows schools' turnaround efforts over time and shares implementation episodes, webinars, research, and practitioner dialogue.
* [WestEd MTSS coaching model](wested-mtss-coaching-model.md) - WestEd's framework provides school-based coaching to support implementation of academic and behavioral multi-tiered systems of support (MTSS).

* [WhichEnglish internet grammar quiz dataset (669,498 speakers)](whichenglish-internet-grammar-quiz-dataset-669498-speakers.md) - A large publicly released dataset of responses to the WhichEnglish online English grammar quiz, created by Hartshorne and colleagues to study syntactic knowledge across ages, language backgrounds, and English exposure.
* [Whole Child Approach](whole-child-approach.md) - A framework for organizing education around developmental relationships, supportive environments, rich learning, skill and mindset development, and integrated supports, developed from the science of learning and development literature.
* [Whole School Safety (WSS) Framework](whole-school-safety-wss-framework.md) - A Chicago Public Schools framework developed with community-based organizations that organizes school safety planning around physical safety, emotional safety, and relational trust.
* [Whole School, Whole Community, Whole Child (WSCC) model](whole-school-whole-community-whole-child-wscc-model.md) - A Centers for Disease Control and Prevention framework that organizes coordinated school health and education efforts across 10 components to support K–12 students' health, learning, and development.
* [Whole-Person Education Guide](whole-person-education-guide.md) - A district-facing framework and facilitated planning guide for aligning whole-person education initiatives, developed for adoption by education systems.
* [Wiki Design Platform](wiki-design-platform.md) - A collaborative-learning wiki platform developed by Gerry Stahl and colleagues that provides awareness, navigation, communication, and analysis components for online intersubjective work.

* [Wilson Language Error Tests (Tests A, B, and C)](wilson-language-error-tests-tests-a-b-and-c.md) - A standardized written-language assessment published by the World Book Company in which pupils identify and correct embedded errors in compositions, with forms and grade-based norms for grades III–XII.
* [Wilson Reading System](wilson-reading-system.md) - The Wilson Reading System is a structured literacy curriculum developed by Wilson Language Training for teaching decoding, spelling, fluency, vocabulary, and related reading skills to learners with word-level reading difficulties.
* [WindowDash](windowdash.md) - WindowDash is a synthetic food-delivery teaching-case dataset and accompanying instructional materials developed by Bang An et al.
* [Wisconsin Information and Technology Literacy Standards](wisconsin-information-and-technology-literacy-standards.md) - A Wisconsin Department of Public Instruction framework defining information and technology literacy content standards, grade-banded performance standards, and proficiency indicators for local curriculum guidance.

* [WISE Agent](wise-agent.md) - WISE Agent (Writing Improvement and Smart Evaluation Agent) is an LLM-based writing-feedback tool developed by Qi Zhu et al.
* [Wolfram Computable Document Format (CDF)](wolfram-computable-document-format-cdf.md) - Wolfram Research's software document format renders interactive Mathematica code as graphical, customizable documents in a standalone player or browser environment.

* [Woodworth-Wells substitution test](woodworth-wells-substitution-test.md) - A speeded perceptual-substitution assessment in which examinees classify labeled forms or symbols into corresponding categories, developed by Woodworth and Wells and adapted here for children.
* [Woolley composite adolescent measurement scale](woolley-composite-adolescent-measurement-scale.md) - A historical composite assessment scale that combines percentile-ranked mental and physical test results into age- and sex-normed scores for educational and vocational guidance.
* [Word Association Test (WAT)](word-association-test-wat.md) - A timed psycholinguistic assessment instrument that elicits and analyzes learners' spontaneous word associations to diagnose the organization and integration of concepts in a knowledge domain.
* [Wordometer app for the SMI iView X eye tracker](wordometer-app-for-the-smi-iview-x-eye-tracker.md) - A prototype eye-tracking application created by Kai Kunze and colleagues that estimates the number of words a person reads from gaze data recorded by the SMI iView X.
* [WordStream Maker](wordstream-maker.md) - WordStream Maker is a no-code software tool developed by Huyen N.
* [Workforce AI literacy framework of six skill and competency domains](workforce-ai-literacy-framework-of-six-skill-and-competency-domains.md) - A workforce-oriented framework organizing AI literacy into six domains—Technical Knowledge, Practical Implementation, Analytical Skills, Interpersonal and Communication Skills, Ethical Evaluation, and Adaptive Skills—developed by Zachariah et al.
* [Workforce Readiness Level (WRL)](workforce-readiness-level-wrl.md) - Smith et al.
* [Working Memory Power Test for Children (WMPT)](working-memory-power-test-for-children-wmpt.md) - A computer-administered assessment developed by ebilities that measures children's working-memory accuracy and confidence-based metacognitive monitoring across increasing difficulty levels.
* [Working Students Success Network (WSSN)](working-students-success-network-wssn.md) - A comprehensive integrated-services strategy implemented by community colleges to support low-income students and students of color through financial, career, and other coordinated supports.
* [World History Project curriculum](world-history-project-curriculum.md) - World History Project is a free, online, year-long high-school world-history curriculum made by the OER Project, combining readings, videos, historical-thinking activities, and teacher guidance.
* [World of Words (WOW)](world-of-words-wow.md) - A supplementary prekindergarten curriculum developed by the World of Words project that builds vocabulary, concept knowledge, and science content knowledge through topic-centered conversations and shared book readings.
* [WorldWide Telescope](worldwide-telescope.md) - WorldWide Telescope is an interactive scientific data-visualization and aggregation software package developed by Microsoft Research, with astronomical imagery, a 3D Universe, and narrated interactive tours.
* [WrAFT (Writing Assessment and Feedback Tool)](wraft-writing-assessment-and-feedback-tool.md) - WrAFT is a deployable, modular LLM-powered automated writing evaluation and feedback system developed by Adnan Labib and colleagues for argumentative-essay writers.
* [Wraparound program](wraparound-program.md) - The Wraparound program is a structured supplemental academic-services programme run by its provider to support academic achievement in a low student-to-teacher-ratio setting.
* [Write to Succeed](write-to-succeed.md) - A professional learning programme developed and iteratively tested by the Southwest English Learner Literacy (SWELL) Partnership to improve literacy instruction, support English learners, and incorporate culturally and linguistically relevant instruction.
* [Writing to Read](writing-to-read.md) - Writing to Read is a computer-supported beginning-literacy programme developed by IBM that progresses from sound-and-letter production to writing words, sentences, and stories, with supplementary practice activities and progress reporting.
* [WWC seven-recommendation framework for preparing young children for school](wwc-seven-recommendation-framework-for-preparing-young-children-for-school.md) - A What Works Clearinghouse practice-guide framework that organizes seven evidence-informed recommendations for preschool educators across social-emotional, executive-function, mathematics, and literacy instruction.
* [WWW Entrez](www-entrez.md) - WWW Entrez is an NCBI-developed World Wide Web interface for searching and linking MEDLINE and public nucleotide and protein sequence databases.

#### X {: #letter-x }

* [Xtreme Reading](xtreme-reading.md) - Xtreme Reading is a structured supplemental curriculum for struggling adolescent readers, derived from the University of Kansas Strategic Instruction Model and delivered through trained teachers using prescribed strategy lessons and materials.

#### Y {: #letter-y }

* [Year Up](year-up.md) - Year Up is a workforce-development programme that combines occupational and technical training, advising and support, paid internships, and post-program job-placement assistance for low-income young adults, operated by Year Up.

* [Youth At-Risk of Homelessness (YARH)](youth-at-risk-of-homelessness-yarh.md) - A programme funded by the U.S.
* [Youth Mental Health Corps (YMHC)](youth-mental-health-corps-ymhc.md) - Youth Mental Health Corps is a programme in which young adults provide supervised, relationship-centered mental-health navigation to school students while receiving training, credentials, and career preparation for behavioral-health work.

* [Zearn Math](zearn-math.md) - Zearn Math is a nonprofit-developed online supplemental K–8 mathematics curriculum and digital learning program used during math instruction, intervention, and tutoring.

* [Zirkus Empathico](zirkus-empathico.md) - Zirkus Empathico is a touchscreen application developed for socio-emotional competence training through emotion-recognition, empathy, and prosocial-behavior activities.
* [Zoo U](zoo-u.md) - Zoo U is a single-player point-and-click educational game developed by Melissa E.

* [95 Phonics Core Program](95-phonics-core-program.md) - A whole-class, Tier I supplemental phonics curriculum for grades K–5, developed and provided by 95 Percent Group, with structured lessons, teacher guides, student workbooks, manipulatives, and digital presentation materials.
* [95 Phonics Lesson Library (PLL)](95-phonics-lesson-library-pll.md) - A phonics curriculum from 95 Percent Group providing tiered small-group lesson plans and teacher and student materials organized around 75 phonics subskills.
* [95 Phonics Screener for Intervention](95-phonics-screener-for-intervention.md) - The 95 Phonics Screener for Intervention is an early-literacy phonics screening and progress-monitoring assessment from 95 Percent Group used to identify students’ skill gaps and guide intervention.
* [[HIDDEN APP]](hidden-app.md) - A mobile AI-supported numeracy education system developed by Luiz Rodrigues et al.
